### [1] [A descent-set obstruction for the Casas-Alvero conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14726)
*Mohammad F. Marashdeh*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研宄Casas--Alvero猜想,通过两项约简将其转化为组合计数的行列式问题。将根平移到原点后,条件由中心正规形式的支撑集分层。当单一根足够时,遗留的整数就是MacMahon行列式,即下降集为支撑集的排列数。由于每个子集都是下降集,不存在少于三个"回收根"的反例;在特征p下,整除某计数但不整除伴随整数z_i的素数是坏素数。该分层还为两个已知特征p命题提供了简短证明,并给出坏素数的新判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Casas--Alvero猜想是代数几何与组合学交叉领域的著名未解问题,断言特征零域上次数为d的首一多项式若与其各阶导数共享非常数因子,则必为一次多项式的d次幂。该猜想虽在诸多特殊情形已被验证,但一般情形的证明仍悬而未决,本文旨在通过新的约简方法深化对该猜想结构的理解。

Method: 论文采用两项关键约简:(1) 将根平移至原点后,利用中心正规形式的系数支撑集对Casas--Alvero轨迹进行分层;若进一步指定每个存活条件由哪一根实现,则系统变为单位下三角形式,完全消去f的系数。(2) 当单一根足以实现全部条件时,问题归结为一个整数,该整数恰为MacMahon行列式,即下降集等于支撑集的排列数。由此构造特征p下的"坏素数"判据,并基于支撑集元素个数进行分类讨论。

Result: 论文证明:由于集合{1,...,d}的每个子集都可能是某排列的下降集,故不存在少于三个回收根的反例;最大计数为Euler zigzag数A_d,从而不规则素数691在d=11时为坏素数。分层方法给出两个已知特征p命题的简短证明,并单独处理二元支撑集的情形(在一切特征下均解决),由此推出反例的中心正规形式至少含四项,并获得一个免Gröbner的坏素数判据。

Conclusion: 通过分层与约简,本文将Casas--Alvero猜想的检验转化为组合计数的算术性质问题,建立了猜想与MacMahon行列式、欧拉锯齿数之间的联系,提供了系统判定坏素数的新工具,并为猜想的进一步证明开辟了组合与算术的途径。

Abstract: The Casas--Alvero conjecture asserts that a monic polynomial $f$ of degree $d$ over a field of characteristic zero sharing a non-constant factor with each of $f',\dots,f^{(d-1)}$ is the $d$-th power of a linear polynomial. Two reductions are carried out. Once a root is translated to the origin, the condition at index $i$ is vacuous unless the coefficient $a_{d-i}$ is nonzero, so the Casas--Alvero locus stratifies by the support of the centred normal form; prescribing in addition which root realises each surviving condition makes the system unit lower triangular and eliminates the coefficients of $f$ entirely. When one root suffices, what remains is a single integer, and that integer is MacMahon's determinant: the number of permutations of $\{1,\dots,d\}$ with descent set the support of $f$. Since every subset is a descent set, no counterexample has fewer than three recycled roots; in characteristic $p$ a prime dividing one of these counts but none of the accompanying integers $z_{i}$ is a bad prime, the largest count in degree $d$ being the Euler zigzag number $A_{d}$, so that the irregular prime $691$ is bad for $d=11$. The stratification also yields short proofs of the two characteristic-$p$ propositions on which all known cases rest; supports of two elements are settled separately in every characteristic, whence a counterexample has at least four terms in centred normal form and a further Gröbner-free criterion for bad primes.

</details>


### [2] [Exponential sums over primes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14777)
*James Maynard,Mayank Pandey,Maksym Radziwiłł*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文改进了1937年Vinogradov关于指数和（von Mangoldt函数加权的指数和）的经典估计，将误差项从N^{4/5}改进到N^{19/24}。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vinogradov在1937年给出了von Mangoldt函数在指数和中的经典界，其误差项为N^{4/5}。这一界在数论（尤其是素数分布、解析数论中的零密度估计等问题）中有广泛应用，改善这一估计具有重要的理论价值。

Method: 本文将α表示为a/q+ε的形式（其中(a,q)=1，q≤N^{1/2}，|ε|≤1/(qN^{1/2})），定义B:=max(q,qN|ε|)，并证明了von Mangoldt函数的指数和上界为N^{o(1)}(N/B^{1/2}+N^{19/24})。

Result: 证明了|Σ_{n<N}Λ(n)e(nα)| ≤ N^{o(1)}(N/B^{1/2}+N^{19/24})，将Vinogradov经典结果的N^{4/5}改进为N^{19/24}（注意19/24≈0.7917 < 0.8=4/5）。

Conclusion: 本文显著改进了Vinogradov（1937）的经典指数和估计，将误差项从N^{4/5}提升到N^{19/24}，对于后续解析数论研究可能产生重要影响。

Abstract: Let $α=a/q+ε$ with $(a,q)=1$, $q\le N^{1/2}$ and $|ε|\le 1/(qN^{1/2})$, and let $B:=\max(q,qN|ε|)$. We show that \[ \Bigl|\sum_{n<N}Λ(n)e(nα)\Bigr|\le N^{o(1)}\Bigl(\frac{N}{B^{1/2}}+N^{19/24}\Bigr). \] This improves on the classical bound of Vinogradov from 1937, which has $N^{4/5}$ in place of $N^{19/24}$.

</details>


### [3] [Reciprocity for The Short Twisted Second Moment of The Riemann Zeta Function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14852)
*Ze Sen Tang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了一个关于黎曼zeta函数在短区间上扭曲二阶矩的互反公式，将Khan的互反公式推广到短区间情形，并显式给出了扭曲参数与区间长度之间的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将已有的一阶矩或长区间上的互反公式推广到短区间（长度为T^δ，δ∈(1/2,1)）设置中，以更精细地理解zeta函数在临界线上的高阶矩行为，并揭示扭曲参数与区间长度之间的内在联系。

Method: 利用解析数论中的调和分析技术，对黎曼zeta函数在高度T、临界线上长度为T^δ（其中δ∈(1/2,1)）的短区间上的扭曲二阶矩建立互反公式，显式推导扭曲因子与区间长度的依赖关系。

Result: 成功获得了短区间上的扭曲二阶矩互反公式，将Khan的经典互反公式推广到δ∈(1/2,1)的短区间情形，并明确刻画了扭曲参数与区间长度参数δ之间的显式联系。

Conclusion: 该结果表明短区间上的扭曲二阶矩满足一个对称的互反关系，且区间长度与扭曲参数之间存在精确的关联，这为研究zeta函数在短区间上的统计分布提供了新的工具。

Abstract: We prove a reciprocity formula for a twisted second moment of the Riemann zeta function over a short interval of length $T^δ$, where $δ\in (\frac{1}{2},1)$, centered at height $T$ on the critical line. This result extends Khan's reciprocity formula to the short interval setting and makes explicit the relation between the twisting parameters and the interval length.

</details>


### [4] [Bounding The Number of Zeros Near the Central Point in Families of Cuspidal Newforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15063)
*Lucas Chen,Christopher Housholder,Joshua Khan,Steven J. Miller,Devayani Pradhan*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了固定权重和素数水平下的偶全纯尖点新形式族中的低零点问题，重点关注在中央点附近的规范化窗口中具有零点的形式数量以及这些零点在形式间的分布。在广义黎曼假设下，证明了沿无限素数水平序列，具有低零点的形式数至少为 N^{7/8} log N；利用高阶中心矩和适当的检验函数证明了该族中正比例的形式在指定窗口内有零点；最后获得多项式上尾界，并证明正比例的形式具有有界且非零数量的低零点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解L函数族中低零点的分布是解析数论的核心问题，特别是关于中央点附近零点是否存在及其数量的统计规律，这与Katz-Sarnak猜想和秩等算术问题密切相关。已有研究中，对于全纯尖点新形式族中精确量化具有低零点形式数量的问题仍缺乏细致结果，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 本文采用一阶和更高阶中心矩分析方法，结合适当的检验函数来研究低零点分布。具体地：(1) 构造无限素数水平序列，利用矩方法证明低零点形式数的下界 N^{7/8} log N；(2) 使用高阶中心矩和精心选择的检验函数证明正比例形式在窗口内有零点；(3) 通过多项式上尾界技术分析零点数量的分布尾部行为。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 沿无限素数水平序列，具有低零点的形式数 ≥ N^{7/8} log N；(2) 在广义黎曼假设下，正比例（positive proportion）的族成员在指定窗口内具有零点；(3) 获得了零点数量的多项式上尾界，并证明正比例形式具有有界且非零数量的低零点。

Conclusion: 本文在广义黎曼假设下显著改进了对偶全纯尖点新形式族中低零点分布的理解，证明了正比例的形式具有低零点，且零点数量分布具有良好的尾界性质。这些结果深化了低零点统计的理论框架，为进一步研究L函数族中零点的精细结构提供了新的技术手段。

Abstract: We study low-lying zeros in families of even holomorphic cuspidal newforms of fixed weight and prime level, with particular emphasis on the number of forms having a zero in a prescribed normalized window about the central point and on the distribution of the number of such zeros among the forms. We quantify the number of forms having at least one zero in the window and study the distribution of the number of zeros in that window among the forms. Assuming the Generalized Riemann Hypothesis, we obtain new lower bounds for the number of forms having a low-lying zero. We first prove that, along an infinite sequence of prime levels $N$, the number of such forms is $\gg N^{7/8}\log N$. We then use higher centered moments and an appropriate test function to show that a positive proportion of the family has a zero in a prescribed window. Finally, we obtain polynomial upper-tail bounds for the number of zeros occurring there and show that a positive proportion of forms have a bounded, nonzero number of low-lying zeros.

</details>


### [5] [Transcendence of continued fractions over function fields and a quantitative version of Uchiyama's theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15119)
*Federico Accossato,Nadir Murru,Giuliano Romeo,Giulia Salvatori*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了特征零域K上的形式幂级数域K((T^{-1}))中的连分数。作者改进了准周期连分数的Maillet-Baker准则类比，证明了Uchiyama的Roth定理类比的定量版本（为代数幂级数的异常好有理逼近数量给出显式界），由此导出代数元素收敛子分母增长的Davenport-Roth型上界，并证明回文连分数要么是二次的要么是超越的（与实数情形相同，但采用不同的证明策略）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: K((T^{-1}))中的连分数与经典实数连分数类似但研究尚不充分。已有的特殊连分数族超越性结果有限，准周期连分数的Maillet-Baker准则类比需要改进，且需要一个定量的Roth定理版本来给出更精确的逼近界。

Method: 假设K的特征为零，作者首先证明Uchiyama的Roth定理（函数域中的类比）的定量版本，为代数幂级数的异常好有理逼近数量建立显式上界。利用这个定量估计，导出收敛子分母增长的Davenport-Roth型上界。最后，通过这个定量框架证明回文连分数要么二次要么超越的性质。整个证明策略区别于实数情形中的经典方法。

Result: 在特征零条件下：(1) 改进了准周期连分数的Maillet-Baker准则的函数域类比；(2) 证明了Roth定理的定量版本，给出异常好有理逼近数量的显式界；(3) 获得代数元素收敛子分母的Davenport-Roth型上界；(4) 证明了回文连分数为二次或超越。

Conclusion: 本文在特征零的函数域中建立了多个连分数相关的重要定量结果，特别是定量的Roth型定理和回文连分数的超越性判定，进一步推进了K((T^{-1}))中连分数与经典实数连分数理论的平行发展。

Abstract: Given a field $K$, let $K((T^{-1}))$ be the field of formal power series. Continued fractions in $K((T^{-1}))$ can be defined by analogy with classical real continued fractions and have been widely studied. Some results establish the transcendence of elements of $K((T^{-1}))$ arising from special families of continued fractions, but much remains to be explored. In this paper, assuming that $K$ has characteristic zero, we improve the known analogues of the Maillet--Baker criteria for quasi-periodic continued fractions. A central tool that we prove is a quantitative version of Uchiyama's analogue of Roth's theorem in function fields, which gives an explicit bound for the number of exceptionally good rational approximations to an algebraic power series. This quantitative estimate also yields a Davenport--Roth-type upper bound on the growth of the denominators of the convergents of algebraic elements. Finally, we prove that palindromic continued fractions are either quadratic or transcendental, as in the real case, but using a different proof strategy.

</details>


### [6] [Algebraic geometric framework of Rogers--Ramanujan identities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15219)
*Yifeng Huang,Kenny Lau,Ken Ono,Peter Paule*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文证明了Huang-Jiang-Oblomkov关于无限秩q级数Z_{a,b}(q)的猜想在(a,b)=(3,4),(3,5),(3,7),(3,8)情形成立，该q级数由有限域上可交换幂零矩阵对(A,B)（满足A^a=B^b）的计数构造，猜想其等于由(a-1)(b-1)/2个模单位构成的显式乘积。证明通过更精细的和到和的恒等式实现，Lau和Ono随后推广证明了该恒等式及完整a=3情形，且已在Lean中形式化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Rogers-Ramanujan型恒等式极为稀少，识别能够成族产生这类恒等式的结构是核心问题。Huang, Jiang和Oblomkov提出了一种新来源：对每对互素整数a,b>1构造一个无限秩q级数Z_{a,b}(q)，来自有限域上满足A^a=B^b的交换幂零矩阵对的计数，并猜想其等于显式模单位乘积。此前仅a=2情形（即Andrews-Gordon恒等式）已知，a>2的情形在本文之前没有任何已知案例。验证这一猜想对理解这类恒等式的深层结构至关重要。

Method: 本文针对(a,b)=(3,4),(3,5),(3,7),(3,8)四种情形证明猜想。证明的关键途径是先建立更精细的和到和(sum-to-sum)恒等式，该恒等式在q=1时对所有与3互素的b成立，并被推测对所有b成立（后由Lau和Ono在一般情形证明）。本文利用组合计数分析和q级数变换技巧，将矩阵对计数产生的生成函数转化为乘积形式。

Result: 成功证明了(a,b)=(3,4),(3,5),(3,7),(3,8)四种情形的猜想，其中(3,4)和(3,5)的乘积分别由3个和4个模单位（level a+b）构成。建立了所有b与3互素时在q=1情形的和到和恒等式，并推测其在一般q下成立。该恒等式后被Lau和Ono完整证明，从而完成了整个a=3情形。所有恒等式已在Lean证明助手中由AxiomProver形式化验证。

Conclusion: 本文首次给出了a>2时该猜想的验证案例，确立了a=3情形的完整证明路径（经Lau和Ono推广后完成），表明来自交换幂零矩阵对的q级数确实能产生Rogers-Ramanujan型的稀有恒等式族。该工作不仅证明了具体案例，还通过提出并证明（部分）精细恒等式为一般情形提供了可推广的方法论，并通过Lean形式化验证确保了正确性。

Abstract: The Rogers--Ramanujan identities equate a $q$-series whose exponents are governed by a quadratic form with an infinite product supported on two residue classes modulo~$5$. Identities of this shape are scarce, and a central problem is to identify the structures that produce them in families. Huang, Jiang, and Oblomkov have proposed a source of a new kind: to each pair of coprime integers $a,b>1$ they attach an infinite-rank $q$-series $Z_{a,b}(q)$, assembled from counts of commuting nilpotent matrix pairs $(A,B)$ with $A^a=B^b$ over finite fields, and they conjecture that it equals an explicit product of $(a-1)(b-1)/2$ modular units of level $a+b$. The $a=2$ cases are the Andrews--Gordon identities; no case with $a>2$ was known. We prove the conjecture for $(a,b)=(3,4)$, $(3,5)$, $(3,7)$, and $(3,8)$. Our proofs pass through a finer sum-to-sum identity, which we conjecture for all $b$ coprime to $3$ and establish for all $b$ when $q=1$. Lau and Ono have since proved that identity in general, and with it the full $a=3$ case. These identities have been formalized and verified in Lean by AxiomProver.

</details>


### [7] [Interlacing for zeros of the Serre derivative of Eisenstein series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15248)
*Maggie Bohanek,Owen McGinty,Erick Ross,Yanhui Su,Hui Xue*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了Eisenstein级数及其Serre导数在下弧上的零点分布。首先给出零点位置的精确估计，然后在此基础上证明四个主要结果：Serre导数的零点满足Stieltjes交错性、标准交错性的分类、与Eisenstein级数零点的交错性，以及尖点投影的零点都在下弧上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Rankin和Swinnerton-Dyer在1970年证明了Eisenstein级数$E_k$的非椭圆零点在基本域中全部位于下弧上；Sugibayashi最近证明Serre导数$\vartheta_k(E_k)$也具有同样的性质。然而，这些零点的精确位置以及它们之间的交错关系尚不清楚，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 本文首先对$\vartheta_k(E_k)$在下弧上的零点位置给出非常精确的估计。基于这些位置估计，通过分析零点在圆弧上的排序，系统地研究了不同类型交错性（Stieltjes交错和标准交错）的条件，并利用第三个结果作为工具推导出第四个结果。

Result: 本文证明了四个主要结果：(1) 对任意$\ell > k$，$\vartheta_\ell(E_\ell)$的零点与$\vartheta_k(E_k)$的零点在下弧上Stieltjes交错；(2) 精确分类了$\vartheta_\ell(E_\ell)$与$\vartheta_k(E_k)$的零点在下弧上标准交错的充要条件；(3) $\vartheta_k(E_k)$的零点总是与$E_{k+2}$的零点在下弧上标准交错；(4) 作为应用，$\vartheta_k(E_k)$的尖点投影的零点全部位于下弧上，推广了Xue和Zhu的结果。

Conclusion: 本文通过精确的位置估计揭示了Eisenstein级数Serre导数零点在下弧上的深层交错结构，并将Xue-Zhu的结果推广至更一般的情形，为模形式零点分布理论提供了新的视角和工具。

Abstract: In 1970, Rankin and Swinnerton-Dyer showed that the non-elliptic zeros of Eisenstein series $E_k$ in the fundamental domain all lie on the lower arc $\{ e^{iθ}: \fracπ{2} < θ< \frac{2π}{3}\}$. Very recently, Sugibayashi showed that the same property also holds for the Serre derivative $\vartheta_k(E_k)$ of Eisenstein series. In this paper, we first give very precise estimates for where exactly these zeros are located on the lower arc. These location estimates then allow us to prove four main results. First, we show that the zeros of $\vartheta_\ell(E_\ell)$ Stieltjes interlace with the zeros of $\vartheta_k(E_k)$ on the lower arc for all $\ell > k$. Second, we classify precisely when the zeros of $\vartheta_\ell(E_\ell)$ (standard) interlace with the zeros of $\vartheta_k(E_k)$ on the lower arc. Third, we show that the zeros of $\vartheta_k(E_k)$ always (standard) interlace with the zeros of $E_{k+2}$ on the lower arc. Fourth, as an application of the third main result, we show that the zeros of the cuspidal projection of $\vartheta_k(E_k)$ all lie on the lower arc, extending a result of Xue and Zhu.

</details>


### [8] [Positive quasimodular forms and the sign uncertainty principle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15415)
*Seewoo Lee*

On the other hand, the supersingular cycles in this paper offer a key advantage over their MNT counterpart: every instance of our infinite family of supersingular curves can be efficiently constructed via Br{ö}ker's algorithm, whereas it is only feasible to construct a relatively small, bounded number of MNT instances via the CM method. In other words, while both constructions give infinite families of pairing-friendly curves in theory, only the supersingular construction gives rise to infinite numbers of cycles that can be realised in practice.
  Supersingular cycles offer benefits that are relevant in the context of recursive pairing-based proof systems. They afford flexibility in the choices of underlying finite fields; one can choose primes $p$ for which the underlying field arithmetic is efficient and for which $p-1$ is divisible by a large power of 2. Or, as we study in detail, using supersingular cycles unlocks the possibility of connecting the cycle with other pairing-friendly elliptic curves that are defined over much smaller finite fields, where proof system arithmetic is much more efficient. Indeed, constructing these so-called lollipops of pairing-friendly curves was the motivating problem (posed by researchers back in 2019) that inspired the present work.

Abstract: We extend the author's wavelet and tent space characterization of the local Hardy space $h^1(\mathbb{R}^n)$ to the full range $0 < p \le 1$. Building on Meyer's program for $H^1(\mathbb{R}^n)$ and its dyadic tent space, we introduce a local dyadic tent space $t_p$ of coefficient pairs, a father layer on the unit lattice and a mother layer on the small dyadic cubes, develop its atomic theory, and prove that membership in $h^p(\mathbb{R}^n)$ is equivalent to seven conditions: the unconditionality of the inhomogeneous wavelet expansion in $L^p$, the $L^p$-integrability of three wavelet square functions, atomic decompositions into local atoms adapted to cubes or balls, and a Goldberg-type splitting $f - \varphi * f \in H^p(\mathbb{R}^n)$, $\varphi * f \in L^p(\mathbb{R}^n)$, tested against an admissible class of functions. The space $t_p$ and its atomic theory appear here for the first time in the quasi-Banach range $p < 1$. Over pairs of a modulus of continuity grading the regularity and a radial decreasing majorant grading the decay, we prove that the splitting holds exactly when the modulus is dominated by the Hölder modulus of exponent $n_p - \lceil n_p \rceil + 1$, $n_p := n(1/p - 1)$, and the majorant satisfies an $\ell^p$-Dini summability over the scales, iterated once more at integer $n_p$. The admissible class is thus maximal for the Goldberg-type splitting: it extends Goldberg's Schwartz class to Hölder total order exactly $n_p$ with majorants down to the critical decay $|x|^{-n/p}$, where a new molecular theory governs the embedding into $H^p$, with explicit counterexamples on each axis. Byproducts include bounds for mollified atoms and molecules, coefficient size conditions, quasi-norm equivalences, and the unconditional $L^2$-convergence of the wavelet-built atomic decomposition, with the error of every finite section an exact Parseval identity.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We address the question of which common differences can arise in arithmetic progressions contained in fractal sets. For a compact set $C\subset\mathbb R$, we investigate not only whether arithmetic progressions occur in $C$, but the full collection of their common differences. More generally, for a finite pattern $P$, we study the set of scales at which affine copies of $P$ appear in $C$.
  For affine self-similar sets satisfying strong separation, we obtain explicit restrictions on admissible common differences.
  Specializing to middle-$\varepsilon$ Cantor sets, we prove that the longest arithmetic progression has length four whenever $3-2\sqrt2<\varepsilon\le 1/3$, showing that the maximal progression length drops immediately from six at the critical parameter $\varepsilon=3-2\sqrt2$. We further develop recursive bounds for the sets of admissible common differences and derive explicit blackout intervals, namely ranges of scales for which arithmetic progressions cannot occur.

Abstract: Let $I=(0,ρ)$, where $0<ρ\leq\infty$, and let $\mathbb{P}_n(I)$ denote the cone of real positive semidefinite $n\times n$ matrices whose entries belong to $I$. We begin by providing the characterization for functions $f \in C^{2n-2}(I)$. Writing $\mathcal{E} =x\frac{d}{dx}$ and denoting the Euler--Hankel matrix by $$ \mathcal{H}_n(f;x)=\bigl[\mathcal{E}^{i+j}f(x)\bigr]_{i,j=0}^{n-1}, $$ we prove that $f[-]$ preserves positivity on $\mathbb{P}_n(I)$ if and only if $f^{(k)}(x) \geq0$ for $0\leq k\leq n-1$ and $\mathcal{H}_n(f;x)\succeq 0$ for every $x\in I$. We then show how the smoothness hypothesis can be entirely removed to obtain the same characterization for general functions, where the above conditions are interpreted as inequalities between distributions. Abstract: Let $λ>-1/2$, $λ\neq0$, and let $Δ_λ=-\frac{d^2}{dx^2}-\frac{2λ}{x}\frac{d}{dx}$ be the Bessel operator on $\mathbb R_+=(0,\infty)$ studied by Muckenhoupt and Stein (TAMS 1965). Abstract: We develop a rigorous mathematical framework for statistical mechanics models on hypergraphs, and give conditions for lifting the classical connection between Potts model partition functions and the Tutte polynomial from graphs to hypergraphs. We define hypergraphical models and their associated partition functions, and extend these to special classes of models induced by families of interaction functions. We illustrate the theory by applying it to three hypergraph interaction families: Parity Ising, Delta Potts, and And Ising. The induced hypergraphical models are not isomorphic to each other, but the first two reduce to the same graphical Ising models. We identify three polymatroids naturally associated with hypergraphs for these models: respectively, the binary matroid of the incidence matrix over $\mathbb{F}_2$, the hypergraphical polymatroid, and the boolean polymatroid. For graphs, the first two reduce to the classical graphical matroid, and their partition functions recover the multivariate Tutte polynomial. The Tutte polynomial thus admits at least two distinct generalizations for hypergraphs, both satisfying a deletion-contraction recurrence: the Tutte polynomial of the binary matroid of the hypergraph incidence matrix, and a multivariate version of the Poincaré polynomial of the hypergraphical polymatroid. The partition functions of And Ising models are likewise multivariate versions of the Poincaré polynomial, here of the boolean polymatroid. These examples illustrate the much greater range of hypergraphical models and underscore the need for the unifying theory.

Abstract: The semi-random graph process is an adaptive random graph process in which an online algorithm is initially given an empty graph on $n$ vertices. We also consider the offline version of the process in which the algorithm is given the entire sequence of random vertex choices before the selection takes place. For a given graph property, the objective of the algorithm is to force the graph to satisfy this property asymptotically almost surely in as few rounds as possible.

Abstract: We give a complete proof of the 2011 Tu--Deng conjecture. The proof then reduces the conjecture to normalized inequalities for the coefficients of that polynomial. A 2011 conjecture by the author which came to be called the Cusick Conjecture (it is a consequence of the Tu--Deng Conjecture) was proved by K. Cheng in 2026. The proof in the present paper extends the cyclic deletion ideas of Cheng. The new ideas might be applicable to other problems.

Abstract: Recent major breakthroughs in $q,t$-combinatorics include the introduction of the Dyck path algebra $\mathbb{A}_{q,t}$ by Carlsson and Mellit and of the Catalanimals by Blasiak et al., both of which led, among other things, to independent proofs of different extensions of the rational shuffle conjecture of Bergeron et al.

Our formula bypasses the elliptic Hall algebra, turning these operators into transparent, workable tools whose action we can compute exactly and efficiently on any symmetric function, not just constants.
  Our second main contribution consists of a series of explicit formulas relating the Neguţ operators to the Theta operators introduced by D'Adderio et al. To prove these formulas, we provide an extension of the aforementioned Theta operators to the entire algebra $\mathbb{A}_{q,t}$, allowing us to obtain a series of new combinatorial results. The algebraic computations underlying this extension have been formalized in Lean.

Abstract: We give an effective algorithm for determining the transportation distance between two given probability density functions defined on the vertices of a graph $G=(V,E)$ by analyzing an associated polytope. The vertices of the polytope correspond to feasible flows on spanning trees in $G$, and the $1$-skeleton of the polytope is a projection of the spanning tree state graph associated with the Glauber dynamics on $G$. The optimal value of this transportation problem, known as the $1$-Wasserstein distance, can be computed by tracing the transportation cost along the vertices of this polytope. We show that a local minimum of the transportation cost is also a global minimum, and this leads to a steepest descent algorithm for solving the transportation problem. If the probability density functions take discrete values in $δ\mathbb{Z}$ for some $δ>0$, then the optimal transport cost can be reached in at most $\frac{|V|-1}δ$ steps. As an application, we give an efficient algorithm for computing the Ollivier--Ricci curvature of a graph.

Result: 1) 对置换σ=[2,3,...,n,1]（或其逆），给出了E_σ(t)关于停车函数下降统计的组合解释；2) 证明了该特殊情形下E_σ(t)等于A_{n-1}型非交叉分拆格有序复形的h-多项式；3) 通过算子方法完全解决了Athanasiadis等人关于h(Δ(NC_W),t)的公开问题；4) 对任意k-可除非交叉分拆偏序集NC_W^(k)，证明了h(Δ(NC_W^(k)),t)具有交错对称分解性质。

Conclusion: 本文通过将Lascoux的公开问题与Athanasiadis等人的公开问题建立联系，不仅给出了Demazure字符多项式族的特例组合解释，还发展了一种统一的算子方法，从而同时解决了两个独立的公开问题，并获得了更广泛的关于k-可除非交叉分拆h-多项式的对称分解性质。

Abstract: In the study of the generating series of Demazure characters, Lascoux used isobaric divided differences to define a family of polynomials $\mathcal{E}_σ(t)$ indexed by permutations $σ$, and asked for a satisfactory expression of these polynomials. In this paper we obtain a combinatorial interpretation of $\mathcal{E}_σ(t)$ for the permutation $σ=[2,3,\ldots,n,1]$ or its inverse in terms of the descent statistic of parking functions of length $n-1$. Based on this progress on Lascoux's open problem, we find that the polynomial $\mathcal{E}_σ(t)$ for this special case coincides with the $h$-polynomial $h(Δ(\mathrm{NC}_W),t)$ of the order complex of the noncrossing partition lattice associated to the irreducible Coxeter group $W$ of type $A_{n-1}$. We are inspired by this coincidence to give an operator approach to $h(Δ(\mathrm{NC}_W),t)$ for any finite Coxeter group $W$. As an application, we completely solve an open problem on $h(Δ(\mathrm{NC}_W),t)$ which was proposed by Athanasiadis, Douvropoulos and Kalampogia-Evangelinou. For any $k$-divisible noncrossing partition poset $\mathrm{NC}^{(k)}_W$, we also obtain the interlacing symmetric decomposition property of the $h$-polynomial $h(Δ(\mathrm{NC}^{(k)}_W),t)$.

</details>

Conclusion: 该工作系统性地推进了对damage number这一参数的理解，覆盖了多个重要图类，并从复杂度理论角度表明精确计算damage number在一般情况下是不可行的（除非PSPACE=P），为后续研究指明了方向。

Abstract: We study a variant of Cops and Robbers in which the robber attempts to visit as many vertices of the graph as possible without being captured, while the cop aims to keep the robber confined to a small set of vertices. The \textit{damage number} of a graph $G$, introduced by Cox and Sanaei in 2019, is the maximum number of vertices the robber can visit in a game of Cops and Robbers on $G$.
  In this paper, we determine damage numbers for several classes of graphs, including hypercubes, Hamming graphs, Johnson graphs, incidence graphs of projective planes, and Erdős-Rényi random graph $G(n,p)$, for $p \gg \log^{2/5}(n) / n^{1/5}$. We also show that the problem of determining the damage number of a graph is {\sf PSPACE}-complete.

</details>

Abstract: For $1\leq k<n$, let $P(n,k)$ be the directed overlap graph whose vertices are the $k$-permutations of $[n]$ and whose arcs are the $(k+1)$-permutations. Isaak proved that $P(n,n-2)$ has no directed Hamiltonian cycle for $n\geq4$ and asked whether it nevertheless has a directed Hamiltonian path. We answer this question affirmatively by showing that $P(n,n-2)$ has a Hamiltonian path.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 作者针对独立数为2的图类，将目标从单一要求"包含K⌈n/2⌉作为奇子式"放宽为更灵活的"包含K⌈n/2⌉作为奇子式或作为全奇浸入"。通过引入全奇浸入（totally odd immersion）这一更强的图结构概念，作者给出了该二选一结论的证明。

Result: 证明了对每个独立数为2的n顶点图G，要么存在K⌈n/2⌉作为其奇子式，要么存在K⌈n/2⌉作为其全奇浸入。这一结果在Kühn等人反例的基础上，为独立数为2的图类提供了一个可证明的、接近原猜想强度的结论。

Abstract: Kühn, Sauermann, Steiner and Wigderson recently disproved the Odd Hadwiger Conjecture, even for graphs with independence number 2. For this class of graphs the conjecture is known to be equivalent to the following: every $n$-vertex graph $G$ with independence number 2 contains $K_{\lceil \frac n2 \rceil}$ as an odd minor. While this does not hold, we prove that every graph $G$ with independence number 2 contains $K_{\lceil \frac n2 \rceil}$ as an odd minor or as a totally odd immersion.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义煎饼图是彩色置换群 Z_m≀S_n 上的 Cayley 图，在排序网络和互连网络设计中具有重要应用。谱隙是衡量图扩张性质的核心参数，确定其渐近行为对于理解这些图族的扩张性至关重要，同时可用于验证或反驳关于扩展图族的已有猜想。

Method: 证明结合了 Cesi 的半递归谱隙不等式，并对与颜色-位置对的陪集 Schreier 图相关联的算子进行 Fourier 分解。通过这种分解，可以将谱隙问题转化为对更小、更简单算子的谱分析，从而获得上下界。

Result: 对所有 m,n≥2，谱隙满足 α_m/n ≤ γ(P(m,n)) ≤ 1/n。对固定 m，γ = Θ_m(1/n)（n→∞）；对固定 n，γ = Θ_n(m^{-2})（m→∞）。该结果否证了 Blanco 和 Buehrle 关于固定 n 时广义煎饼图构成扩展图族的猜想。

Conclusion: 广义煎饼图的谱隙关于维度 n 呈 1/n 阶衰减（固定 m），关于颜色数 m 呈 m^{-2} 阶衰减（固定 n）。特别地，固定 n 时该图族不是扩展族，回答并否证了先前文献中的猜想。

Abstract: The generalized pancake graph $P(m,n)$ is the Cayley graph of the group of colored permutations $\mathbb{Z}_m\wr S_n=(\mathbb{Z}_m)^n\rtimes S_n$ generated by generalized prefix reversals. As a consequence, for every fixed $m\geq2$, $γ(P(m,n))$ is $Θ_m(1/n)$ as $n\to\infty$. The proof combines Cesi's semi-recursive spectral-gap inequality with a Fourier decomposition of the appropriate operators associated with a coset Schreier graph of color-position pairs. For fixed $n\geq2$, we also establish that $γ(P(m,n))$ is $Θ_n(m^{-2})$ as $m\to\infty$. This disproves a conjecture of Blanco and Buehrle asserting that, for fixed $n$, the corresponding undirected generalized pancake graphs form an expander family.

</details>

Method: 主要方法是对相关的双重指数和建立中心化的四阶矩估计，利用该估计设计狄利克雷卷积/范德科尔普特类论证，将加法同余式的计数转化为对指数和的矩控制，从而在亚平方根阈值内推出渐近公式。对于一般r≥5，通过优化r项情形的参数选择得到统一的阈值α_r。

Abstract: We obtain asymptotic formulas for additive congruences
  \[
  \sum_{i=1}^r m_i x_i^{-s}\equiv λ\pmod p,
  \]
  where the \(m_i\) range over arbitrary subsets of \(\mathbb F_p^\ast\) and the \(x_i\) over shifted intervals. For five terms, in the balanced case of common cardinality \(N\), the asymptotic holds uniformly in \(λ\) whenever

  N>p^{14/29+\varepsilon},
  \]
  giving a genuine sub-square-root range. The main input is a centered fourth-moment estimate for the associated double exponential sums. The same method yields sub-square-root thresholds for every fixed \(r\ge 5\), including \(N>p^{8/17+\varepsilon}\) for six terms, with

  \]
  as \(r\to\infty\).

Abstract: Let $F$ be a graph. A graph $G$ is called $F$-free if $G$ does not contain $F$ as a subgraph. Let ${\rm EX}(n,F)$ denote the set of $F$-free graphs of order $n$ with the maximum edges. Result: 证明了c_sub≥13/6（连通次立方图）和c_cub≥17/8（连通立方图）。具体家族中，B̂*_t满足|V|=76·2^t−12, γ=26·2^t−4, ρ=12·2^t−2；B̂•_t满足|V|=108·2^t−14, γ=34·2^t−4, ρ=16·2^t−2。两个族均产生γ(G)≤2ρ(G)+1的无界加法违背。

Conclusion: 该构造否证了γ(G)≤2ρ(G)+1这一猜想，即使对于连通三次立方图也不成立。两个图族通过相同的二元连接器复合和闭合引理构造，仅连接器和初始装配不同，说明该方法具有一定的普适性。

Abstract: For a graph \(G\), let \(γ(G)\) and \(ρ(G)\) denote its domination number and packing number, respectively. Let \(c_{\mathrm{sub}}\) and \(c_{\mathrm{cub}}\) denote the respective limsups of \(γ(G)/ρ(G)\) over connected subcubic and connected cubic graphs as \(ρ(G)\to\infty\). We prove \[
  c_{\mathrm{sub}}\geq\frac{13}{6},
  \qquad
  c_{\mathrm{cub}}\geq\frac{17}{8}, \] by constructing two explicit binary branching families. The connected noncubic subcubic graphs \(\widehat B_t^\star\) satisfy \[
  |V(\widehat B_t^\star)|=76\cdot2^t-12,\qquad
  γ(\widehat B_t^\star)=26\cdot2^t-4,\qquad
  ρ(\widehat B_t^\star)=12\cdot2^t-2, \] whereas the connected cubic graphs \(\widehat B_t^\bullet\) satisfy \[
  |V(\widehat B_t^\bullet)|=108\cdot2^t-14,\qquad
  γ(\widehat B_t^\bullet)=34\cdot2^t-4,\qquad
  ρ(\widehat B_t^\bullet)=16\cdot2^t-2. \] The constructions use the same binary connector composition and closing lemma, with different connectors and initial assemblies. As a consequence, both families give unbounded additive violations of \(γ(G)\leq2ρ(G)+1\), disproving the proposed inequality even for connected cubic graphs.

</details>

Method: 将 Fallon 和 Iverson 的倍增构造推广为张量乘法构造，利用复 Hadamard 矩阵与等角紧框架的配对；利用友好 Hadamard 矩阵对的存在性保证配对的构造；基于 Primes Powders 的友好对和 Paley 会议矩阵给出具体构造；推广 Turyn 的会议矩阵幂次构造到等角紧框架。

Conclusion: 通过推广倍增构造为张量乘法构造，并结合友好 Hadamard 对与 Paley 会议矩阵等数学工具，系统地扩展了等角紧框架的构造方法，获得了覆盖多个参数族的新构造。

Abstract: In this paper we provide new constructions of equiangular tight frames of size $2d$ in $\mathbb{C}^d$. We generalize the doubling construction of Fallon and Iverson to a tensor multiplication construction based on a suitable pair consisting of a complex Hadamard matrix and an equiangular tight frame. In particular, such a pair always exists whenever there is an amicable pair of real Hadamard matrices. Most notably, amicable Hadamard pairs of order $q+1$ exist for all prime powers $q\equiv 3\pmod 4$. We also find specific constructions based on a family of pairs of order 6 and on pairs whose equiangular tight frames are defined by Paley conference matrices with $q\equiv 1\pmod 4$. Finally, we provide a power construction of equiangular tight frames that generalizes the construction of Turyn for conference matrices.

</details>

*Zhen Liu,Qinghou Zeng*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Erdős、Gallai和Tuza证明了对于任意m条边的图，α₁(G)+τ₁(G)=Ω(m^(2/3))，并提出了确定最优渐近常数的问题。该问题涉及图中三角形结构与边集的度量关系，是极值图论中的经典开放问题。

Result: 建立了极限等式lim_{m→∞} min_{|E(G)|=m} (α₁(G)+τ₁(G))/m^(2/3) = 3/2，从而确定了最优渐近常数为3/2。

Conclusion: 该结果完全解决了Erdős-Gallai-Tuza提出的问题，给出了α₁(G)+τ₁(G)在m^(2/3)量级上的精确渐近最优常数。

Abstract: For a graph $G$, let $α_1(G)$ be the maximum size of an edge set containing at most one edge from every triangle, and let $τ_1(G)$ be the minimum size of an edge set meeting every triangle. Erdős, Gallai, and Tuza proved that $α_1(G)+τ_1(G)=Ω(m^{2/3})$ for every $m$-edge graph and asked for the optimal asymptotic constant. We prove $$\lim_{m\to\infty} \min_{G,\,|E(G)|=m} \frac{α_1(G) + τ_1(G)}{m^{2/3}} = \frac{3}{2},$$ thereby establishing that the sharp constant is $3/2$ and solving the problem.

</details>

*Yongke Qu,Guoqing Wang,Yuanlin Li*

TL;DR: 本文证明了对于每一个含有指数为$p$的循环子群的有限非交换群$G$（其中$p$是$|G|$的最小素因子），Erdős-Ginzburg-Ziv常数$s(G)$满足$s(G)=\eta(G)+\exp(G)-1$，从而确认了Gao关于有限Abel群上Erdős-Ginzburg-Ziv常数的猜想在非交换情形下的自然推广。作为进一步推论，还确定了这类群的所有广义Erdős-Ginzburg-Ziv常数$s_{m\exp(G)}(G)$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Erdős-Ginzburg-Ziv常数$s(G)$与短积一常数$\eta(G)$是组合数论中刻画有限群结构的重要不变量。Gao在有限Abel群上提出了关于$s(G)$与$\eta(G)$关系的猜想，本文旨在验证该猜想在非交换群情形下的自然推广是否成立，并进而计算相应的广义常数。

Method: 本文针对含有指数为$p$（最小素因子）的循环子群的有限非交换群$G$，利用群结构分析和积一子序列的组合方法，建立了$s(G)=\eta(G)+\exp(G)-1$的精确等式，并在此基础上通过推广的常数定义推导出所有广义Erdős-Ginzburg-Ziv常数$s_{m\exp(G)}(G)$。

Result: 对每个含有指数为$p$的循环子群的有限非交换群$G$（$p$为$|G|$的最小素因子），确认了非交换Gao猜想的等式成立，即$s(G)=\eta(G)+\exp(G)-1$；同时完整确定了该群族的所有广义Erdős-Ginzburg-Ziv常数$s_{m\exp(G)}(G)$。

Conclusion: 该结果不仅推广了Gao在有限Abel群上的猜想至更广泛的非交换群族，而且为有限群中积一子序列理论提供了新的精确结论，丰富了组合数论与有限群论的交叉研究。

Abstract: Let $G$ be a finite group, and let $\exp(G)$ denote its exponent. The Erdős-Ginzburg-Ziv constant $s(G)$ is the least integer forcing a product-one subsequence of length $\exp(G)$, while the short product-one constant $η(G)$ is the least integer forcing a nonempty product-one subsequence of length at most $\exp(G)$. The natural nonabelian extension of a conjecture [W. Gao, \emph{On zero-sum subsequences of restricted size II}, Discrete Math. 2003] on the Erdős-Ginzburg-Ziv constant in finite abelian groups predicts that $s(G)=η(G)+\exp(G)-1.$ We confirm this equality for every finite nonabelian group $G$ having a cyclic subgroup of index $p$, where $p$ is the smallest prime divisor of $|G|$. As further consequences, we determine all generalized Erdős-Ginzburg-Ziv constants $s_{m\exp(G)}(G)$ for this family of groups.

</details>

Abstract: The dual affine Robinson--Schensted correspondence and the affine matrix-ball construction give two related parametrizations of extended affine permutations. From the stable-window data of the dual correspondence, we introduce a dual weight \(β\) and prove that it is consistent with the original pair \((λ,N_0)\). We prove that \(β\) and the AMBC weight \(ρ\) have identical monodromy along affine Knuth paths. We further give an explicit relation between \(β\) and \(ρ\), showing that their difference depends only on the associated tabloids.

</details>

Result: 成功证明了Bickle关于k-塌缩图中度数为k的顶点最小数量的猜想，即该最小值恰好为max{⌈2n/(2k-1)⌉, k²-k-2-(k-3)n}。

Conclusion: 通过验证该猜想，本文完整刻画了k-塌缩图中低度顶点数量的极值下界，为该类图的结构理论提供了重要结论。

Abstract: A graph $G$ is said to be $k$-collapsible if $G$ has minimum degree $k$ and every non-null proper induced subgraph of $G$ has minimum degree less than $k.$ In 2018, Bickle conjectured that the minimum number of vertices of degree $k$ in a $k$-collapsible graph of order $n$ with $k\ge 3$ is ${\rm max}\{\lceil 2n/(2k-1)\rceil,\, k^2-k-2-(k-3)n\}.$ We prove this conjecture.

</details>

Motivation: 探索单词与其卷曲数变换在重复避免性质上的相互作用，确定在何种字母表大小下可以同时实现无限无重叠（overlap-free），并找到有限情形下的最长长度上界。

Method: 采用组合方法：对二元和三元字母表使用基于Thue-Morse的形态构造结合穷举广度优先搜索（BFS）来确定有限最大值；对四元字母表构造无限示例；使用自动定理证明工具Walnut验证无限构造的正确性，并通过独立的穷举BFS检查有限最大值。

Result: 三字母表上同时无重叠的单词及其C变换的最大长度为84；四字母表上存在同时无限无重叠的示例，证明4是最小字母表大小使得同时无重叠可以无限持续。

Conclusion: 卷曲数变换保持了限制重复的能力，但所需字母表大小随无限性需求而增长；四字母是最小能同时支持无限无重叠的字母表。

Abstract: We study repetition avoidance in a word ${\bf w}$ and its curling-number transform $C({\bf w})$. For alphabets of sizes $2$, $3$, and $4$, we use Thue-Morse-based morphic constructions and exhaustive finite searches. A ternary word for which both ${\bf w}$ and $C({\bf w})$ are overlap-free has length at most $84$, whereas over four letters an infinite example exists. Hence $4$ is the smallest alphabet size admitting simultaneous infinite overlap-freeness. The infinite constructions are verified in Walnut; the finite maxima are obtained by exhaustive breadth-first search and checked independently.

</details>

Motivation: Simonovits乘积猜想预测了禁止子图族极值图应具有某种"乘积结构"（即完整join形式），但其自然的弱化版本——仅要求在每个足够大的阶数上存在至少一个乘积极值图——是否成立是一个开放问题。本文旨在研究这一存在性问题的答案。

Result: 成功构造了所需的禁止子图族L和常数c>0，使得极值数ex(n,L)>t_3(n)+cn^{3/2}对所有足够大的n成立；同时证明所有足够大的L-极值图的补图至多有两个连通分量，因此不存在三个正阶图完整join形式的极值图。

Conclusion: 该结果为Simonovits乘积猜想的存在性弱化版本给出了否定答案：即使在每个足够大的阶数上仅要求存在一个乘积形式的极值图，这一性质也可能不成立。这表明乘积猜想及其变体需要更细致的条件才能成立。

Abstract: We construct a fixed finite family $\mathcal L$ of ordinary forbidden subgraphs with $p(\mathcal L)=3$ and a constant $c>0$ such that $$ex(n,\mathcal L)>t_3(n)+cn^{3/2}$$ at every sufficiently large order. Nevertheless, the complement of every sufficiently large $\mathcal L$-extremal graph has at most two connected components. In particular, no such extremal graph is a complete join of three graphs of positive order. This gives a negative answer to a natural existence-only question motivated by the Simonovits Product Conjecture, in which one asks only for one product extremizer at each sufficiently large order.

</details>

Abstract: We study spanning $H$-subdivisions in dense graphs where the length of every subdivision path is prescribed in advance. This problem is motivated in part by a question of Pavez-Signé [Combin. Probab. Comput. 33 (2024), 121--128], who asked whether the subdivision paths in a spanning $H$-subdivision can be required to have similar lengths.

For every graph $H$ with $h$ edges and no isolated vertices, write $E(H)=\{e_1,\ldots,e_h\}$, and every choice of integers $\ell_1,\ldots,\ell_h\ge4$ satisfying $\sum_{i=1}^h\ell_i=n-|V(H)|+h$ and $\sum_{\ell_i<αn}\ell_i\leβn$, the graph $G$ contains a spanning $H$-subdivision in which the $i$th edge of $H$ is replaced by a path of length exactly $\ell_i$. We also give a family of examples showing that a linear additive term in $h$ is necessary in general.

</details>

Abstract: Let $G$ be a graph together with a total order $\prec$ on its edges. We say that $\prec$ is realizable in $\mathbb{R}^d$ if there is a placement of the vertices of $G$ in $\mathbb{R}^d$ such that the Euclidean lengths of the edges induce exactly the order $\prec$. Almendra-Hernández and Martínez-Sandoval proved that every total order on the edges of the complete graph $K_n$ is realizable in $\mathbb{R}^{n-2}$. We show that the same is not true for the disjoint union of two complete graphs: for every $n\geq 3$ there is a total order on the edges of $K_n\sqcup K_n$ that is not realizable in $\mathbb{R}^{n-2}$, but is in $\mathbb{R}^{n-1}$. Surprisingly, the realizability of an order on a disconnected graph is not determined by its restrictions to the connected components. We also study realizability on the real line: we characterize which disjoint unions of two cycles are realizable, and estimate the largest number of edges an $n$-vertex graph can have while all of its edge-orders remain realizable on the line. In general dimension, we show that the largest number of edges of an $n$-vertex graph all of whose edge-orders are realizable in $\mathbb{R}^d$ is $dn+O\!\left(dn/\ln(dn)\right)$.

</details>

Abstract: We present a combinatorial proof of Simonis type MacWilliams identity for harmonic higher weight distributions of linear codes. Furthermore, we investigate the statistical moments of the harmonic higher weight enumerators for random linear codes. Defining the enumerators via rank functions of the generator matrices of linear codes, we prove that its expectation vanishes for all non-trivial harmonic functions due to the inherent symmetry of random matrices, and we also derive an explicit, non-trivial formula for the covariance.

Abstract: We study unordered triples of order-n floretion base vectors whose tile centroids form nondegenerate equilateral triangles. A scaled integer centroid map converts Euclidean completion into exact arithmetic on a regular triangular lattice. Residues modulo 3 give a self-contained same-orientation theorem: every equilateral centroid triangle uses three tiles of one orientation; a three-color theorem of Ivrissimtzis, Dodgson, and Sabin gives an independent geometric interpretation. Combining this obstruction with the finite triangular-lattice completion counts of Brouwer, Joe, Noble, and Noble yields $|E_n|=4^n(4^n-1)/12$. Synchronized local cyclic actions form a distinguished subclass with $|L_n|=(7^n-4^n)/3$, hence an exponentially vanishing fraction of all equilateral centroid triangles. On the no-e support ${i,j,k}^n$, a mod-2 rigidity argument forces every equilateral centroid triangle to be local, yielding $(2^n-1)3^{n-1}$ examples. We also introduce the unsigned vertex product and its centroid, the product point, and characterize when this point equals the Euclidean center for local cycles. The resulting parity automata produce linear recurrences and Fibonacci subfamilies, including reflection-symmetric product-centered triangles. Finally, an equilateral triangle is multiplication-generated exactly when its unsigned vertex product is the identity, equivalently when its product point is the origin. Within the local class these are precisely the nontrivial global cyclic orbits; exhaustive exact enumeration through order 5 finds no nonlocal examples.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 作者系统性地研究这一新参数，通过对特定图族（有界度图、稀疏图、无minor图）构造满足特殊直线条件的点配置来证明下界；同时利用概率方法或组合计数技巧为随机图建立SGdim的上界。

Abstract: For an undirected graph $G$, its \emph{Sylvester--Gallai dimension} $\text{SGdim}(G)$ is the largest affine dimension of a configuration of distinct real points indexed by $V(G)$ in which every line determined by an edge of $G$ is a special line (contains at least three points). Hence, the classical Sylvester--Gallai theorem can be stated as $\text{SGdim}(K_n)=1$ for the complete graph $K_n$. We initiate the systematic study of this new graph parameter and prove lower bounds for certain graph families (bounded degree, sparse, minor-free) as well as an upper bound for random graphs.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文证明了Akbari等人提出的关于边-路径矩阵（edge-path matrix）的两个猜想：猜想1涉及边-路径矩阵与图中边数上界的关系，猜想2断言图是欧拉图当且仅当边-路径矩阵的所有非对角元素均为偶数。

Motivation: 边-路径矩阵是图论中一个重要的矩阵，其非对角元素记录了任意两顶点之间最大边不相交路径的数量。Akbari等人提出了两个关于该矩阵的猜想，本文旨在证明这两个猜想，从而完善边-路径矩阵的理论基础。

Method: 本文通过严格的图论论证和代数推导，分别对两个猜想给出完整的证明。猜想1通过分析边-路径矩阵的特征值或矩阵元素与图边数的关系来证明上界；猜想2则通过分别证明充分性和必要性来建立图是欧拉图与矩阵元素全为偶数之间的等价关系。

Result: 本文成功证明了Akbari等人提出的两个猜想均成立：边-路径矩阵与图的边数上界之间的关系被严格确立，且图的欧拉性与边-路径矩阵所有元素均为偶数之间的等价性得到了完整证明。

Conclusion: 该工作解决了边-路径矩阵研究中的两个开放问题，进一步加深了对边-路径矩阵结构与图的拓扑性质之间联系的理解，为后续研究提供了坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: The edge-path matrix is a square matrix where each off-diagonal entry records the maximum number of edge-disjoint paths between the corresponding pair of vertices. Akbari et al. [On edge-path eigenvalues of graphs, Linear Multilinear Algebra 70 (2022) 2998-3008] proposed two conjectures: Conjecture 1 relates the edge-path matrix to an upper bound on the number of edges in the graph, while Conjecture 2 asserts that a graph is Eulerian if and only if all entries of its edge-path matrix are even. In this paper, we prove the two conjectures.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前对r-均匀k-皇冠C^r_{1,k}的线性极值数（即不含该超图作为子超图的r-均匀线性超图的最大边数）缺乏系统的构造性下界。本文旨在通过h-重横贯（h-fold transversal）框架，建立从任意固定模板(G,B)到线性极值数的传递，从而统一并推广已有的构造方法，并精确刻画相应的密度常数ρ_{r,k}。

Method: (1) 核心工具是传递引理：任何固定的配对(G,B)，其中B为h-重横贯，都给出ex^lin_r(n,C^r_{1,k}) ≥ |E(G)|/|B| · n − O(√n)的下界。(2) 通过关联计数（incidence counting）得到上界ρ_{r,k} ≤ r/h，并在对偶化后以成对平衡设计（pairwise balanced design）刻画等号情形。(3) 对r=q+1（q为素幂），利用截断射影平面给出下界q/h，并利用极大h-弧（maximal h-arc）达到上端点。(4) 通过填充（padding）截断射影平面构造，得到当k= r和εr ≤ k ≤ r时的渐近结果。(5) 对非相交模板，引入局部安全块条件（local safe-block condition）替代h-重横贯要求。

Result: (1) ρ_{r,k} ≤ r/h，等号由对偶后的成对平衡设计加正则可分子族刻画。(2) 对r=q+1（q为素幂），q/h ≤ ρ_{q+1,k} ≤ (q+1)/h；当极大h-弧存在时达到上端点，特别地q为偶数且h|q时ρ_{q+1,k}=(q+1)/h。(3) ρ_{r,r} = (1−o(1))r/2。(4) 当εr ≤ k ≤ r时，ρ_{r,k} = (1+o(1))r/(r−k+2)。(5) 非相交模板的传递由局部安全块条件控制。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一个统一的构造框架，通过h-重横贯和成对平衡设计深刻刻画了r-均匀k-皇冠的线性极值数，特别是在截断射影平面下给出了可达到的精确上界，并获得了k接近r时密度的渐近公式。该结果为此类超图极值问题提供了完整的工具链和严格的界。

Abstract: Let $C^r_{1,k}$ be the $r$-uniform $k$-crown and put $h=r-k+2$. For a finite linear intersecting $r$-uniform hypergraph $G$, let $τ_h(G)$ be the minimum size of a set meeting every edge of $G$ in at least $h$ vertices, and define \[ ρ_{r,k}=\sup_G\frac{|E(G)|}{τ_h(G)}. \] We prove that every fixed pair $(G,B)$, with $B$ an $h$-fold transversal, yields \[ \operatorname{ex}^{\mathrm{lin}}_r(n,C^r_{1,k}) \ge \frac{|E(G)|}{|B|}n-O_{G,B}(\sqrt n) \] for all sufficiently large $n$. Incidence counting gives $ρ_{r,k}\le r/h$, and equality is characterized after dualization by a pairwise balanced design with a distinguished regular subfamily.
  For $r=q+1$, where $q$ is a prime power, truncated projective planes give \[ \frac qh\le ρ_{q+1,k}\le\frac{q+1}{h}. \] The upper endpoint is attained whenever a maximal $h$-arc exists; in particular, if $q$ is even and $h\mid q$, then $ρ_{q+1,k}=(q+1)/h$. Padding the truncated-plane construction gives \[ ρ_{r,r}=(1-o(1))\frac r2 \] and, uniformly for each fixed $\varepsilon>0$ and $\varepsilon r\le k\le r$, \[ ρ_{r,k}=(1+o(1))\frac{r}{r-k+2}. \] For nonintersecting templates, the corresponding transfer is governed by a local safe-block condition that replaces the $h$-fold transversal requirement.

</details>


### [83] [Counting Lattice Points in Minkowski Sums of Cross Polytopes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16037)
*Hongyu Wang,Qilin Hou,Warut Thawinrak,Zhiyuan Liu,Ziyi Dai*

TL;DR: 本文受Postnikov关于单纯形Minkowski和格点计数的研究启发，研究了交叉多胞体Minkowski和的格点计数问题，建立了类似结果及若干推论，包括引入与draconian序列相关的support-enumerator、证明其与根多胞体的h*-多项式一致、证明关于preorders的h-多项式的猜想，以及推导出交叉多胞体Minkowski和格点数的通用公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Postnikov研究了单纯形Minkowski和的格点枚举问题并取得了重要成果，本文希望将类似的分析框架推广到交叉多胞体的Minkowski和上，并由此获得新的组合学结果和猜想证明。

Method: 引入support-enumerator的概念，将其与Postnikov的draconian序列及根多胞体的h*-多项式建立联系；利用根多胞体的对称性建立对偶性质；基于对偶性推导格点数的通用公式，进而计算Ehrhart多项式并研究其Ehrhart正性，同时推导边界格点数和表面积公式。

Result: 证明support-enumerator与对应根多胞体的h*-多项式一致，为h*-多项式提供新的解释并给出体积计算的简单方法；证明Chapoton和Athanasiadis关于preorders的h-多项式的猜想；证明交叉多胞体Minkowski和与其对偶多胞体具有相同的格点数；得到以draconian序列表示的格点计数通用公式，证明Ehrhart多项式具有Ehrhart正性，并给出边界格点数和表面积的类似公式。

Conclusion: 本文成功地将Postnikov关于单纯形的结果推广到交叉多胞体的Minkowski和，建立了全新的组合与几何联系，不仅解决了一个公开猜想，还提供了计算这些多胞体Ehrhart多项式、边界格点数和表面积的系统方法。

Abstract: Motivated by Postnikov's study of lattice-point enumeration in Minkowski sums of simplices, we investigate lattice points in Minkowski sums of cross polytopes and establish analogous results, together with several related consequences. In particular, we introduce the support-enumerator associated with Postnikov's notion of draconian sequences and show that it coincides with the $h^*$-polynomial of the corresponding root polytope. This provides a new interpretation of the $h^*$-polynomial and yields a simple method for computing the volume of the corresponding polytope. We further exploit the symmetry of such root polytopes to establish a duality property for support-enumerators, which in turn provides a proof of a conjecture by Chapoton and Athanasiadis concerning the $h$-polynomials of preorders. As an immediate consequence, we prove that a Minkowski sum of cross polytopes and its dual polytope have the same number of lattice points. This duality then leads to a general formula for the number of lattice points in Minkowski sums of cross polytopes in terms of draconian sequences. Our formula enables us to compute the Ehrhart polynomials of these polytopes and show that they are Ehrhart positive. Furthermore, the formula allows us to derive analogous formulas for the number of lattice points on their boundaries and for their surface volumes.

</details>
