<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 饱和数问题（确定给定图的最小饱和图边数）是极值图论中的经典问题。风筝图K作为一种特殊的四顶点图（从K4中移除一条边并在一度点接上悬挂边），其饱和数的确定尚未解决。作者旨在建立连通性与K-饱和图之间的联系，进而确定sat(n,K)并刻画极值图，从而部分回答Hua和Peng提出的开放问题。

Method: 首先建立连通性与K-饱和图之间的关系，利用这一关系推导出K-饱和图的结构性质，然后通过精确的下界估计和构造性构造，确定饱和数sat(n,K)，并完整刻画所有达到该下界的极值图。

Result: 确定了风筝图K的饱和数sat(n,K)的精确值，并完全刻画了所有极值图（即达到饱和数最小边数的K-饱和图）。该结果部分回答了Hua和Peng在[Discrete Math. 349 (2026) 114674]中提出的问题。

Conclusion: 本文通过连通性分析这一关键工具，成功解决了风筝图K的饱和数问题，不仅给出了精确的饱和数值，还完整刻画了极值结构，为该问题的更一般情形提供了重要的研究思路和阶段性结论。

Abstract: For a fixed graph $H$, a graph $G$ is $H$-saturated if $G$ does not contain a copy of $H$, but adding any edge $e \in E(\overline{G})$ to $G$ creates a copy of $H$. The saturation number $\mathrm{sat}(n,H)$ is the minimum number of edges in an $H$-saturated graph on $n$ vertices. Let $K$ be the kite graph, formed by removing one edge from $ K_4$ and then attaching a pendant edge to a vertex of degree two in the resulting graph.In this paper, we first establish a relationship between connectivity and $K$-saturated graphs, and subsequently determine the saturation number of the kite graph $K$. Moreover, we completely characterize all extremal graphs.Our result provides a partial answer to a problem raised by Hua and Peng [Discrete Math. 349 (2026) 114674].

</details>


### [85] [Towards discrete convex analysis over classical root systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16090)
*Yuni Iwamasa*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文建立了离散凸分析(DCA)在经典根系诱导的离散结构上的统一理论，将原有的整数格(Lattice)设定(本质对应A型根系)推广到所有经典根系。以欧几里得Coxeter复形的顶点集作为L-凸性的原始离散域，以根格作为M-凸性的对偶离散域，建立了L-凸与M-凸函数理论，证明局部最优蕴含全局最优，并通过离散Fenchel-Legendre共轭建立了积分L-凸与积分M-凸函数之间的一一对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散凸分析(DCA)作为可高效求解组合优化问题的统一理论框架，已在运筹学、经济学和纯数学等广泛领域展现出强大应用价值。但原有DCA框架本质上仅适用于A型根系(整数格)。鉴于DCA的广泛适用性，作者希望将其推广到所有经典根系对应的离散结构上，建立更一般的统一理论。

Method: 采用欧几里得Coxeter复形的顶点集作为L-凸性的原始离散域，以根格作为M-凸性的对偶离散域；利用根系相关的多面体结构来定义L-凸与M-凸函数，并结合根系引入积分性概念；通过离散Fenchel-Legendre共轭建立两类函数之间的对偶关系。

Result: 成功建立了DGCA在所有经典根系上的推广：证明了L-凸与M-凸函数的局部最优性可保证全局最优性；建立了积分L-凸函数与积分M-凸函数之间通过离散Fenchel-Legendre共轭的一一对应关系，将原DCA的共轭性从A型根系推广到所有经典根系。

Conclusion: 该工作将离散凸分析从A型根系推广至所有经典根系的离散结构上，为更广泛的组合优化问题提供了统一的凸分析理论框架，进一步拓展了DCA的适用范围。

Abstract: Discrete Convex Analysis (DCA) is a discrete analog of continuous convex analysis, originally proposed as a unified theoretical framework for efficiently solvable combinatorial optimization problems. Recently, DCA has proven to be a powerful tool across diverse fields, ranging from operations research to economics and pure mathematics.
  Motivated by the broad applicability of DCA, this paper establishes a unified theory of discrete convex analysis over discrete structures arising from classical root systems, extending the usual setting of the integer lattice, which essentially corresponds to type A. We adopt the vertex set of the Euclidean Coxeter complex as the primal discrete domain for L-convexity, and the root lattice as the dual discrete domain for M-convexity. Using the associated polyhedral structures, we formulate L- and M-convex functions together with notions of integrality determined by the root system. We show that local optimality guarantees global optimality for these functions. Furthermore, we establish that integral L-convex functions and integral M-convex functions correspond one-to-one via the discrete Fenchel--Legendre conjugate, thereby extending the conjugacy in the original DCA from type A to all classical root systems.

</details>


### [86] [Dense-core approach to the Brualdi--Hoffman--Turán problem on odd wheels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16127)
*Longfei Fang,Mingqing Zhai,Yuhan Zhang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文统一处理了具有固定边数的奇数轮图 W_{2k+1}（k≥2）的邻接谱极值问题，证明了 W_5-free 图满足 ρ(G)²−ρ(G)≤m（等号当且仅当 K_{n,n} 嵌入完美匹配），并对任意 k≥3 证明了 W_{2k+1}-free 图满足 ρ(G)²−(k−1)ρ(G)≤m−C(k,2)（等号当且仅当 K_k∨qK_1），完全解决了 Yu、Li 和 Peng 提出的猜想，并通过不同方法强化了关于奇圈、友谊图和奇扇图的已知结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决奇数轮图 W_{2k+1} 在固定边数条件下的邻接谱极值问题，验证并完全证明 Yu、Li 和 Peng 提出的猜想，同时统一处理 W_5 的例外情形与一般情形 k≥3 的谱极值问题。

Method: 结合边谱稳定性定理、残差函数和稠密核方法（dense-core method）。W_5 的例外情形和一般情形共享稠密核约化和边谱稳定性，但具有不同的刚性结构。

Result: 证明了 W_5-free 图（充分大的边数 m）满足 ρ(G)²−ρ(G)≤m，等号充要条件为 K_{n,n} 的每部分嵌入完美匹配（n 偶数，m=n²+n）；对任意 k≥3，W_{2k+1}-free 图满足 ρ(G)²−(k−1)ρ(G)≤m−C(k,2)，等号充要条件为 K_k∨qK_1（m=C(k,2)+kq）。完全解决了该猜想，并强化了奇圈、友谊图和奇扇图的已知结果。

Conclusion: 本文彻底解决了 Yu-Li-Peng 猜想，统一了奇数轮图的固定边谱极值问题，并为奇圈、友谊图和奇扇图提供了更强的谱极值上界，展示了稠密核方法结合边谱稳定性在谱极值图论中的有效性。

Abstract: We present a unified presentation of the fixed-size adjacency-spectral extremal problem for odd wheels $W_{2k+1}$, where $k\geq2$ and $W_{2k+1}=K_1\vee C_{2k}$. The exceptional case $W_5$ and the general case $W_{2k+1}$, $k\ge3$, share the same dense-core reduction and edge-spectral stability, but have different rigidity structures. We prove that every $W_5$-free graph of sufficiently large size $m$ satisfies $ρ(G)^2-ρ(G)\le m,$ with equality precisely for $K_{n,n}$ with a perfect matching embedded in each part, where $n$ is even and $m=n^2+n$. For any fixed $k\ge3$, every $W_{2k+1}$-free graph of sufficiently large size $m$ satisfies $ρ(G)^2-(k-1)ρ(G)\le m-\binom{k}{2},$ with equality precisely for $K_k\vee qK_1$ when $m=\binom{k}{2}+kq$. Our results completely settle a conjecture proposed by Yu, Li and Peng and, via a distinct approach, further strengthen known results concerning odd cycles, friendship graphs and odd fan graphs for sufficiently large $m.$ The proof combines the edge-spectral stability theorem, residual functions and the dense-core method.

</details>


### [87] [Equality Cases for the Face-Degree Majorization Theorem on Simplicial Complexes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16129)
*Yueli Han,Lu Lu*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文刻画了高维张-宋-范（Grone-Merris-Bai定理在单纯复形上的推广）部分和不等式中所有等号成立的情形。对于任意r维单纯复形X（r≥2），证明了前q个up-Laplacian特征值之和等于前q个共轭(r-1)-度序列之和当且仅当q≥max{rank B_r(X), Δ_{r-1}(X)}。与图的情形不同，等号只能在两个序列的所有非零项都耗尽之后才可能出现。作为推论，第一个部分和的相等以及整个序列的相等都等价于X恰好包含一个r-单纯形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Grone-Merris-Bai定理是图论中关于Laplacian谱与共轭度序列之间majorization关系的重要结果，Zhang、Song和Fan将其推广到单纯复形的高维情形。然而，该高维majorization定理中等号成立的条件尚不清楚。本文的目的是完整刻画这些等号情形，以深入理解图与高维复形之间的本质差异。

Method: 证明基于两个关键技术：(1) 局部down-Laplacian分解（local down-Laplacian decomposition），用于将高维问题化归为更细致的谱分析；(2) Ky Fan不等式的等号情形，用于精确刻画特征值部分和取到上界时的必要条件。通过这些工具，作者对单纯复形上up-Laplacian的前q个特征值之和与共轭(r-1)-度序列的前q项之和进行比较，并推导出等号的充分必要条件。

Result: 主要结果：对于所有r≥2的r维单纯复形X，前q个up-Laplacian特征值之和∑_{i=1}^{q}λ_{r-1,i}(X)等于相应共轭度序列部分和∑_{i=1}^{q}d_{r-1,i}^⊤(X)当且仅当q≥max{rank B_r(X), Δ_{r-1}(X)}。这意味着等号仅当两个序列的所有非零项都被计入时才能发生。此外，第一个部分和相等（q=1情形）以及整个序列完全相等均等价于X恰含唯一一个r-单纯形。

Conclusion: 本文完整刻画了高维majorization定理的所有等号情形，揭示了一个与图情形截然不同的现象：在图的情形中等号可以在部分和中出现，但在高维（r≥2）时，等号只能在所有非零项耗尽之后出现。这个结果深化了对单纯复形谱与其组合结构关系的理解，并进一步表明包含唯一r-单纯形这一极简结构是高维等号情形的本质特征。

Abstract: The Grone--Merris--Bai theorem states that the Laplacian spectrum of a
  simple graph is majorized by its conjugate degree sequence. Recently,
  Zhang, Song, and Fan extended this result to simplicial complexes by
  establishing a majorization relation between the spectrum of the
  $(r-1)$-dimensional up-Laplacian and the conjugate $(r-1)$-degree sequence.
  In this paper, we characterize all equality cases in the partial-sum
  inequalities of this higher-dimensional majorization theorem. For every
  $r$-dimensional simplicial complex $X$ with $r\ge2$, we prove that
  \[
  \sum_{i=1}^{q}λ_{r-1,i}(X)
  =
  \sum_{i=1}^{q}d_{r-1,i}^{\top}(X)
  \]
  if and only if
  \[
  q\ge \max\{\operatorname{rank}B_r(X),Δ_{r-1}(X)\}.
  \]
  Thus, unlike the graph case, equality can occur only after both sequences
  have exhausted all their nonzero terms. As consequences, equality in the
  first partial sum and equality between the entire sequences are both equivalent
  to $X$ containing a unique $r$-simplex. The proof is based on the local
  down-Laplacian decomposition and the equality case of the Ky Fan inequality.

</details>


### [88] [Directed strongly regular graphs from groups, loops and quasigroups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16202)
*Štefan Gyürki,Mikhail Klin*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文构造了四族无限的直向强正则图，其阶分别为 $2n^2$ 和 $3n^2$，通过群、拟群、环及其拉丁方来描述这些构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直向强正则图（Directed Strongly Regular Graphs, DSRG）是一类重要的组合结构，在编码理论、通信网络和代数图论中具有广泛应用。构造具有新参数的无限族DSRG是该领域的重要研究方向。

Method: 作者提出两种基于环积（wreath product）的Cayley有向图初等构造，并将其推广到任意拟群和环上，利用代数结构与拉丁方的性质系统性地生成四族无限直向强正则图。

Result: 成功构造了四族直向强正则图，其参数分别为 $(2n^2,3n-2,2n-1,n-1,3)$、$(2n^2,4n-2,2n+2,n+2,6)$、$(3n^2,4n-2,2n,n,4)$ 和 $(3n^2,6n-2,2n+6,n+6,10)$。

Conclusion: 通过将群上的Cayley构造推广至拟群和环，作者获得了四族新的无限直向强正则图，拓展了该领域已知的参数范围，体现了代数结构在图论构造中的威力。

Abstract: We introduce four infinite families of directed strongly regular graphs of orders $2n^2$ and $3n^2$. The constructions are described in terms of groups, quasigroups, loops and their Latin squares. Two preliminary Cayley digraph constructions over wreath products are extended to arbitrary quasigroups and loops, yielding directed strongly regular graphs with parameters $(2n^2,3n-2,2n-1,n-1,3),(2n^2,4n-2,2n+2,n+2,6),(3n^2,4n-2,2n,n,4),(3n^2,6n-2,2n+6,n+6,10)$.

</details>


### [89] [Twisted primitive group association schemes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16278)
*Akihiro Higashitani,Masanari Kamiya,Hirotake Kurihara*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究原始群结合方案的相交数是否能决定其组合同构类型。对于PSL(2,q)（q为奇素幂且q=11或q≥17，或q=2^f且f≥3），以及对称群A6和A8，构造了与共轭类划分代数同构但非组合同构的Schur划分，从而证明这些方案的相交数不能唯一决定其组合结构，即它们是不可分离的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心问题是探讨原始群结合方案中，"相交数"这一代数不变量是否足以决定方案本身的组合同构类型，这关系到群结合方案分类理论的基本问题。

Method: 对PSL(2,q)（特定条件下的奇素幂或偶素幂）以及A6、A8，显式构造Schur划分，使其与群共轭类划分代数同构但组合不同构，以此证明非可分性。

Result: 在PSL(2,q)（q=11或q≥17的奇素幂，或q=2^f且f≥3）及A6、A8上成功构造了所需的Schur划分，表明这些方案的原始群结合方案不能被其相交数唯一决定，即它们是非可分的。

Conclusion: 对于所列出的特殊线性群PSL(2,q)和交错群A6、A8，其原始群结合方案的相交数不足以唯一确定组合同构类，这些方案是非可分的。

Abstract: We give results on the question of whether the intersection numbers of a primitive group association scheme determine it up to combinatorial isomorphism. For $G=\operatorname{PSL}(2,q)$, where $q$ is an odd prime power with $q=11$ or $q\ge 17$, or $q=2^f$ with $f\ge3$, we construct a Schur partition that is algebraically isomorphic to the partition of $G$ into conjugacy classes but not combinatorially isomorphic to it. Consequently, the corresponding primitive group association schemes are not determined up to combinatorial isomorphism by their intersection numbers; in particular, they are non-separable.
  For $\mathfrak A_6$ and $\mathfrak A_8$, we also explicitly construct Schur partitions that are algebraically isomorphic to the corresponding partitions into conjugacy classes but not combinatorially isomorphic to them.

</details>


### [90] [Supersaturation for Eventown via Generator Switching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16321)
*Zicheng Han,Xiande Zhang,Yuhao Zhao*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了eventown族的超饱和问题。Berlekamp-Graver定理表明偶大小且两两偶交集的集合族最大规模为2^{⌊n/2⌋}。O'Neill猜想当族的大小超过该极值s时，至少存在s·2^{⌊n/2⌋-1}个奇交集对。本文将该猜想证明的范围从s≤2^{⌊n/8⌋}/n扩展到s≤2^{⌊n/2⌋}/26，即极值规模的固定正比例范围，并证明了该界在此范围内是紧的。此外还得到了对任意超量s均成立的下界，以及稳定性和移除结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Eventown定理刻画了偶偶交集合族的最大规模，但当超过该极值界限时，至少需要产生多少个奇交集对（超饱和问题）此前仅在小范围s上被解决。O'Neill提出了一个猜想，此前只对s=1,2以及非常小的s得到了验证。本文的目标是大幅扩展该猜想成立的范围。

Method: 本文采用线性代数与组合结构分析相结合的方法。通过将偶集合族与向量空间（特征2上）关联，利用生成子空间的结构来系统性地分析奇交集对的下界。作者通过精细的构造和计数论证，将猜想的证明范围推广到s≤2^{⌊n/2⌋}/26的固定正比例范围，并利用极值结构的稳定性分析建立移除引理。

Result: (1) 证明了O'Neill猜想对1≤s≤2^{⌊n/2⌋}/26成立，将已知范围从s≤2^{⌊n/8⌋}/n大幅扩展到极值规模的固定正比例部分。(2) 证明该界在整个范围内是紧的（sharp）。(3) 推导了适用于任意超量s的改进下界。(4) 建立了稳定性和移除结果：当e(F)<2^{⌊n/2⌋-1}时，F接近一个极值eventown族，且可通过删除少量成员使其变为eventown族。

Conclusion: 本文显著推进了eventown超饱和问题的研究，将O'Neill猜想的已知范围从亚指数级别扩展到极值规模的固定正比例（约3.8%），并辅以紧性、稳定性及移除结果，为该问题的完整解决提供了重要进展。

Abstract: An eventown family is a family of even-sized subsets of $[n]$ in which every two distinct members have an even-sized intersection. A classical theorem of Berlekamp and Graver shows that the maximum size of such a family is $2^{\lfloor n/2\rfloor}$. The supersaturation problem for eventown asks how many odd-intersection pairs must occur when this extremal bound is exceeded. For a family $\mathcal F$ of even-sized subsets of $[n]$, let $e(\mathcal F)$ denote the number of unordered pairs whose intersection size is odd. O'Neill conjectured that if $|\mathcal F|=2^{\lfloor n/2\rfloor}+s$, then $e(\mathcal F)\ge s\,2^{\lfloor n/2\rfloor-1}$ for \[ 1\le s\le 2^{\lfloor n/2\rfloor}-2^{\lfloor n/4\rfloor}. \] Previously, the conjecture was known for $s=1,2$, and, for $s\le 2^{\lfloor n/8\rfloor}/n$ with $n$ sufficiently large. We prove the conjectured bound for \[ 1\le s\le \frac{2^{\lfloor n/2\rfloor}}{26}, \] extending the known range to a fixed positive proportion of the extremal eventown size. The bound is sharp throughout this range. As further consequences, we derive a lower bound valid for arbitrary excess $s$, which improves the previously known estimate in an additional range. We also establish stability and removal results for families of extremal size satisfying $e(\mathcal F)<2^{\lfloor n/2\rfloor-1}$, showing that such a family is close to an extremal eventown family and can be made eventown by deleting a small number of its members.

</details>


### [91] [The matching extendability of optimal 2-planar graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16408)
*Xinyao Li,Heping Zhang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究最优2-平面图的匹配可扩展性，证明了每个偶数阶4连通最优2-平面图是1-可扩展的，给出了2-可扩展的判定准则，证明了不存在5-可扩展的最优2-平面图，构造了基于十二面体的4-可扩展最优2-平面图，并证明了偶数阶6连通最优2-平面图的距离3 m-可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2-平面图是一类重要的拓扑图类，已知其边数满足|E(G)| ≤ 5|V(G)| - 10，达到等号时为最优2-平面图。匹配可扩展性是图论中衡量图的结构稳健性的重要概念，研究最优2-平面图的匹配可扩展性有助于深入理解这类极值图的结构性质。

Method: 通过局部最优性（local optimality）这一技术手段，结合图的连通性假设（4连通、6连通）以及图的阶数奇偶性，对最优2-平面图进行结构分析，进而证明各类匹配可扩展性结论。还基于十二面体构造了具体的4-可扩展最优2-平面图。

Result: (1) 每个偶数阶4连通最优2-平面图都是1-可扩展的；(2) 给出了偶数阶4连通最优2-平面图2-可扩展的判定准则；(3) 不存在5-可扩展的最优2-平面图；(4) 构造了基于十二面体的4-可扩展最优2-平面图；(5) 每个至少含2m+2个顶点的偶数阶6连通最优2-平面图，对任意m≥0，都是距离3的m-可扩展的。

Conclusion: 本文系统刻画了最优2-平面图的匹配可扩展性，建立了连通性与匹配可扩展性之间的定量联系，并给出了匹配可扩展性的最佳上界（5-可扩展不可能性）以及具体达到4-可扩展的构造实例。

Abstract: A graph is 2-planar if it can be drawn in the plane such that each edge is crossed by at most two other edges. It is known that for a 2-planar graph $G$, $|E(G)| \le 5|V(G)| - 10$. When the equality holds, we call $G$ an optimal 2-planar graph. This paper investigates the matching extendability of optimal 2-planar graphs. By local optimality, we prove that every 4-connected optimal 2-planar graph $G$ of even order is 1-extendable, and give a criterion for $G$ to be 2-extendable. We also prove that no optimal 2-planar graph is 5-extendable and construct a 4-extendable optimal 2-planar graph based on the dodecahedron. Finally, we show that every 6-connected optimal 2-planar graph of even order with at least $2m+2$ vertices is distance 3 $m$-extendable for any $m \ge 0$.

</details>


### [92] [A new flag-transitive linear space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16412)
*Seyed Hassan Alavi,Ashraf Daneshkhah,Martin W. Liebeck,Cheryl E. Praeger*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文构造了一个新的旗传递2-(496,4,1)设计，其自同构群为PΓL₂(32)，修正了有限旗传递线空间分类中的一处遗漏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在有限旗传递线性空间的分类工作中存在一处遗漏，即某个2-(496,4,1)设计未被包含在内。本文旨在通过显式构造该设计来填补这一分类空白。

Method: 利用射影半线性群PΓL₂(32)作为自同构群，构造一个新的旗传递2-(496,4,1)设计。该设计的具体构造方法涉及对该群及其子群结构的分析，以确定满足旗传递性和设计参数的点和区组。

Result: 成功构造了一个新的旗传递2-(496,4,1)设计，其自同构群为PΓL₂(32)，并证明了该设计在已有的旗传递线空间分类中是被遗漏的。

Conclusion: 该构造修正了有限旗传递线性空间分类中的一个重要遗漏，使得分类结果更加完整和准确。

Abstract: We construct a new flag-transitive $2$-$(496,4,1)$ design with automorphism group $\mathrm{PΓL}_2(32)$. This corrects an omission in the classification of the finite flag-transitive linear spaces.

</details>


### [93] [On the finite group whose proper enhanced power graph is claw-free](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16434)
*Sudip Bera,Andrea Lucchini*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究有限群G的"真增强幂图"E**(G)为无爪图（不含K_{1,3}作为诱导子图）时G的结构。首先证明E(G)无爪当且仅当G为循环群；随后利用E(G)的支配顶点构成中心的一个循环子群（即G的cyclicizer cyc(G)），给出E**(G)无爪时G/cyc(G)的精确结构刻画：若G可解但非幂零，则G为亚循环群，或G/cyc(G)为Frobenius群或2-Frobenius群；若G不可解，则G/cyc(G)同构于PSL(2,q)或PGL(2,q)，从而给出不可解群的真增强幂图为无爪群的完整分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 增强幂图是研究有限群结构的重要图论工具。无爪图是图论中具有良好结构性质的图类，刻画其增强幂图为无爪图的有限群结构，有助于从图论角度理解群的代数性质，并推广已有关于增强幂图图论性质的研究成果。

Method: 本文采用代数与图论相结合的方法。首先证明E(G)无爪当且仅当G为循环群；然后研究E**(G)（由非支配顶点诱导的子图）的无爪性，利用支配顶点构成的cyclicizer cyc(G)（中心的一个循环子群），通过商群G/cyc(G)的结构分析，分别就可解、非幂零与非可解情形对G进行刻画。

Result: 获得了以下主要结果：1）E(G)无爪当且仅当G为循环群；2）当E**(G)无爪时，若G可解但非幂零，则G为亚循环群，或G/cyc(G)为Frobenius群或2-Frobenius群；3）若G非可解，则G/cyc(G)同构于PSL(2,q)或PGL(2,q)，由此给出非可解群的真增强幂图为无爪群的完整分类。

Conclusion: 本文通过引入cyclicizer并分析商群结构，完整刻画了真增强幂图为无爪图的有限群，特别对非可解情形给出了完整分类，深化了对增强幂图图论性质与有限群结构之间联系的理解。

Abstract: Let $G$ be a finite group. The \emph{enhanced power graph} of $G$, denoted by $\mathcal{E}(G)$, is the graph with vertex set $G$ in which two vertices $u$ and $v$ are adjacent if and only if there exists an element $w \in G$ such that both $u$ and $v$ belong to $\langle w \rangle$. The \emph{proper enhanced power graph} of $G$, denoted by $\mathcal{E}^{**}(G)$, is the subgraph of $\mathcal{E}(G)$ induced by the non-dominating vertices.
  The main objective of this paper is to investigate finite groups whose proper enhanced power graph is claw-free, that is, contains no induced subgraph isomorphic to the complete bipartite graph $K_{1,3}$. We first prove that $\mathcal{E}(G)$ is claw-free if and only if $G$ is cyclic. The set of dominating vertices of $\mathcal{E}(G)$ forms a cyclic subgroup of the center of $G$, namely the \emph{cyclicizer} $\cyc(G)$ of $G$. This allows us to give a precise description of the structure of $G/\cyc(G)$ when $\mathcal{E}^{**}(G)$ is claw-free. If $G$ is solvable but not nilpotent, then $G$ is metacyclic, or $G/\cyc(G)$ is either a Frobenius group or a $2$-Frobenius group. If $G$ is non-solvable, then $G/\cyc(G)$ is isomorphic to $\PSL(2,q)$ or $\PGL(2,q),$ and this allows us to give a complete classification of the non-solvable groups whose proper enhanced power graph is claw-free.

</details>


### [94] [Lattice balls with large additive energy in discrete cubes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16444)
*Xinyu Long*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限阿贝尔群子集A的加性能量E(A)。作者得到了一致于维度d的估计，将离散球Z^d∩B_d(R)的归一化加性能量与连续球B_d(R)的能量进行对比。当R_d/√d→∞时，归一化能量的d次方根收敛到4√3/9。作为应用，构造了满足特定能量增长率的集合A_n⊂{0,1,...,n-1}^d，回答了Shao的问题。此外还证明了在Gram矩阵坐标下，连续球能量的指数速率由固定维行列式最大化问题决定，其极值点为正四面体的Gram矩阵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加性能量是加法组合学和解析数论中的核心量，衡量集合中方程a1+a2=a3+a4的解数。理解高维整数格点球内集合的加性能量及其与连续版本的比值，对加法组合学的极值问题具有重要意义。Shao提出了关于特定高维集合加性能量增长率的问题，本文旨在通过构造显式例子回答该问题。

Method: 作者建立离散球能量与连续球能量之间的比较估计（uniform in dimension），利用高维变量趋于无穷时的极限分析。关键技术是将归一化能量提升到1/d次方后取极限，通过Gram矩阵坐标变换将连续能量问题转化为固定维数的行列式最大化问题，并证明该最优化问题的极值点对应正四面体的Gram矩阵。

Result: （1）对R_d/√d→∞，归一化离散球能量的1/d次方极限为4√3/9；（2）构造出的集合A_n满足log E(A_n)=3log|A_n| - d log(3√3/4)+o(d)；（3）取d=⌊(log n)^{1/2}⌋时给出显式构造回答了Shao的问题；（4）Gram矩阵坐标下连续球能量指数速率由正四面体Gram矩阵实现。

Conclusion: 本文建立了高维离散与连续球加性能量之间的精确渐近关系，给出了均匀于维度的比较估计，并通过构造显式例子回答了Shao的问题。内蕴的几何洞察是将能量问题归结为Gram矩阵的行列式最大化，揭示了该组合学问题与高维几何（正四面体）之间的深刻联系。

Abstract: For a finite set $A$ in an abelian group, let \[
  E(A)=\#\{(a_1,a_2,a_3,a_4)\in A^4:a_1+a_2=a_3+a_4\}. \]
  We obtain an estimate uniform in $d$ that compares the normalized additive energy of $\mathbb{Z}^d \cap B_d(R)$ with the continuous energy of $B_d(R)$ .
  If $R_d/\sqrt d\to\infty$, then \[
  \lim_{d\to\infty}
  \left(
  \frac{E\bigl(\mathbb{Z}^d\cap B_d(R_d)\bigr)}
  {\lvert \mathbb{Z}^d\cap B_d(R_d)\rvert^3}
  \right)^{1/d}
  =\frac{4\sqrt{3}}{9}. \] As an application, consider \[
  A_n
  =
  R_n\mathbf{1}_d
  +
  \bigl(\mathbb{Z}^d\cap B_d(R_n)\bigr), \] where $d=d(n)\to\infty$ satisfy $\log d=o(\log n)$, and $R_n=\lfloor(n-1)/2\rfloor$. Then $A_n\subset\{0,1,\ldots,n-1\}^d$ and \[
  \log E(A_n)
  =3\log|A_n|-d\log\frac{3\sqrt3}{4}+o(d). \] In particular, taking $d=\lfloor(\log n)^{1/2}\rfloor$ gives an explicit construction answering a question of Shao \cite{Shao2026}. We also prove that in Gram-matrix coordinates, the exponential rate of the continuous ball energy is determined by a fixed dimensional determinant maximization whose extremizer is the Gram matrix of a regular tetrahedron.

</details>


### [95] [Measurable Matroids: Foundations and Min--Max Theorems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16464)
*Kristóf Bérczi,András Imolay,Ádám Schweitzer*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文在标准无原子测度空间上建立了可测拟阵论（matroid theory）的测度论类比，证明了可测拟阵可由独立集、基、秩函数和闭包算子等价刻画，并建立了截断、伸长、直和、对偶和子式等可测类比。文章证明了一般可测版本的Edmonds拟阵交定理和Edmonds–Fulkerson拟阵并定理，应用于可测匹配、二部图群（bipartite graphings）中的匹配问题及容量约束传输等优化问题，并推广了Nash-Williams–Tutte定理到图群上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在子模集函数的商收敛框架（quotient-convergence framework）驱动下，有限拟阵理论需要合适的极限侧可测对象。本文旨在识别这些可测对象并发展其基本结构与优化理论，从而将经典的拟阵理论与测度论及分析相结合，为图群（graphings）等无限结构上的组合优化问题提供理论工具。

Method: 本文采用测度论方法，在标准无原子测度空间上定义可测拟阵，并证明其多个等价刻画（独立集、基、秩函数、闭包算子）。在此基础上构造截断、伸长、直和、对偶与子式等运算的可测版本，并利用这些工具证明可测Edmonds交定理和Edmonds–Fulkerson并定理，以及公共基的存在性定理。此外，通过秩扩张条件处理上确界可达性问题，并将结果应用于二部图群上的匹配问题与Nash-Williams–Tutte型定理。

Result: 建立了可测拟阵的完整理论基础，证明其与有限拟阵的多种等价描述一致，并涵盖了归一化有限拟阵、图群的圈拟阵、以及划分、嵌套、格路径、横截和匹配拟阵的可测类比。得到了可测版本的Edmonds交/并定理（极大值在一般情况下为上确界），以及当秩扩张条件满足时可达性结果。应用方面获得了二部图群可测匹配的精确Hall亏缺公式、连续二部b-匹配的极大极小定理、容量约束运输与指定截面的应用，以及图群上的可测Nash-Williams–Tutte定理。

Conclusion: 本文成功建立了可测拟阵理论这一新框架，不仅统一了已有的一些结果（如Lyons–Nazarov可测完美匹配定理），还开拓了拟阵优化与测度论相结合的新方向。通过可测交/并定理，文章建立了图群上组合优化问题的统一处理框架，证明了超有限本质生成森林的近似覆盖与打包定理，并在加强秩不等式下获得精确分解结果。

Abstract: We develop a measure-theoretic analogue of matroid theory on standard atomless measure spaces. Motivated by the quotient-convergence framework for submodular set functions, our aim is to identify suitable measurable objects on the limit side of finite matroid theory and to develop their basic structural and optimization theory. We prove that measurable matroids admit equivalent descriptions by independent sets, bases, rank functions, and closure operators. The class includes normalized finite matroids, cycle matroids of graphings, and measurable analogues of partition, nested, lattice path, transversal, and matching matroids. We establish measurable analogues of truncation, elongation, direct sum, duality, and minors.
  We prove measurable versions of Edmonds' matroid intersection theorem and the Edmonds--Fulkerson matroid union theorem, together with an attainment theorem for common bases. The intersection theorem retains the classical min--max form, with the maximum replaced in general by a supremum. Applied to partition matroids, it gives an exact Hall-deficiency formula for measurable matchings in bipartite graphings and a min--max theorem for continuous bipartite $b$-matchings, with applications to capacity-constrained transport and prescribed cross-sections. We give a rank-expansion criterion under which the supremum is attained. For bipartite graphings, this criterion specializes to the measurable perfect matching theorem of Lyons and Nazarov.
  For measurable union, the Edmonds--Fulkerson rank formula remains valid. As a main application of measurable union and its attainment theorem, we prove measurable Nash-Williams--Tutte theorems for graphings, characterizing approximate coverings and packings by hyperfinite essential spanning forests and obtaining exact decompositions under strengthened rank inequalities.

</details>


### [96] [A Family of Simultaneously Cospectral Trees for Degree-Distance Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16497)
*Limeng Lin,Quanyu Tang,Kehua Wang,Wei Wang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文否定了Alfaro和Zapata(2024)提出的猜想，即所有树均可由Ddegp和Ddeg的谱唯一确定。作者构造了一个无穷族非同构树对，使得每对树在六个矩阵(A, L, Q, D, Ddegp, Ddeg)下同时共谱，并进一步指出对于二部图，Atrs和Atrsp通过对角{±1}矩阵相似，具有相同的Smith标准型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 谱图理论中，图的谱刻画是核心问题。Alfaro和Zapata基于计算证据提出了关于树可由度-距离矩阵谱确定的猜想，需要验证或推翻这些猜想。

Method: 采用r-正则化叶扩展和等划分约简技术构造反例；利用符号翻转观察证明二部图中Atrs与Atrsp的相似性。

Result: 构造了无穷族树对(每对17r-15个顶点，r≥3)，这些树对在六个矩阵下同时共谱，推翻了原猜想；同时证明了二部图中Atrs和Atrsp相似且具有相同Smith标准型。

Conclusion: Alfaro和Zapata关于Ddegp和Ddeg谱确定树的猜想是错误的；对于树而言，Atrs和Atrsp的谱问题及Smith标准型问题等价，简化了后续研究。

Abstract: Spectral characterization of graphs for various graph matrices constitutes a central topic in spectral graph theory. Let $G$ be a graph with adjacency matrix $A(G)$, diagonal degree matrix $\Deg(G)$, distance matrix $D(G)$, and transmission matrix \(\Trs(G)\), respectively. Recently, Alfaro and Zapata (2024) introduced the degree-distance matrices \(\Ddegp(G)=\Deg(G)+D(G)\) and \(\Ddeg(G)=\Deg(G)-D(G)\), together with the transmission-adjacency matrices \(\Atrsp(G)=\Trs(G)+A(G)\) and \(\Atrs(G)=\Trs(G)-A(G)\). Based on computational evidence for trees on at most \(20\) vertices, they conjectured that all trees are determined by the spectra of \(\Ddegp\) as well as \(\Ddeg\).
  In this paper, we disprove these conjectures by constructing an infinite family of pairs of non-isomorphic trees. More precisely, for each integer \(r\ge 3\), we construct a pair of trees on \(17r-15\) vertices which are simultaneously cospectral with respect to the following six matrices \[
  A,\quad L,\quad Q,\quad D,\quad \Ddegp,\quad \Ddeg . \] The construction is based on an \(r\)-regularized leaf extension and an equitable-partition reduction. We also record a simple sign-switching observation for transmission-adjacency matrices: if \(G\) is bipartite, then \(\Atrs(G)\) and \(\Atrsp(G)\) are similar via a diagonal \(\{\pm1\}\)-matrix and have the same Smith normal form. Consequently, for trees, the spectral and Smith normal form problems for \(\Atrs\) and \(\Atrsp\) are equivalent.

</details>


### [97] [Three Infinite Families Separating Schur Positivity, the Strongly Nice Property, and the Nice Property](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16613)
*Kai Zhang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文构造了三族无限图来分离Schur正性、强nice性和nice性这三个逐渐放宽的性质。具体包括：连通且强nice但不Schur正的F_t族，不连通且强nice但不Schur正的M_t族，以及连通且nice但不强nice的N_r族。此外还引入了level-k nice性质并计算了N_r的level深度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在图的对称函数X_G的研究中，已知Schur正性蕴含强nice性，而强nice性蕴含nice性，但之前不清楚这些蕴含关系是否严格。构造反例来分离这些性质是理解它们之间深层关系的关键。

Method: 本文通过三族显式构造的图来分离三种性质。首先构造连通族F_t(t≥6)为强nice但不Schur正。其次证明齐次强nice对称函数在非负单项式系数下对乘法封闭，从而强nice图对不交并封闭，由此构造M_t=H⊔K_t(H=K_{3,3}-e)为不连通的强nice但不Schur正族。最后定义N_r=K_r∨(K_2⊔2K_1)并证明其连通、nice但不强nice，且构造了level-k nice性质并计算其level深度为4r!。

Result: 成功构造了三族无限图严格分离Schur正性、强nice性和nice性这三个性质：F_t证明强nice不蕴含Schur正性，M_t证明不连通情形下也能分离这两种性质，N_r证明nice不蕴含强nice。同时证明了强nice对称函数在特定条件下的乘法封闭性，并给出N_r的level深度为4r!的精确计算。

Conclusion: 本文通过显式构造证明了Schur正性、强nice性和nice性之间的蕴含关系是严格递进的，它们形成了真正的三层嵌套。这为图的对称函数的性质研究提供了清晰的反例框架，也引出了level-k nice这一新的可研究的性质度量。

Abstract: For a graph $G$, Schur positivity of $X_G$ implies that $G$ is strongly nice, and every strongly nice graph is nice. We construct three infinite families separating these properties. We first give a connected family $F_t$, $t\ge6$, that is strongly nice but not Schur positive. We then prove that homogeneous strongly nice symmetric functions with nonnegative monomial coefficients are closed under multiplication, and hence that strongly nice graphs are closed under disjoint union. As an application, for $H=K_{3,3}-e$, the graphs \[ M_t=H\sqcup K_t,\qquad t\ge3, \] form a disconnected family that is strongly nice but not Schur positive. Finally, we define \[ N_r=K_r\vee(K_2\sqcup2K_1),\qquad r\ge2, \] and prove that every $N_r$ is connected and nice but not strongly nice. We also introduce the level-$k$ nice property and show that the level depth of $N_r$ is $4r!$.

</details>


### [98] [The maximum number of maximal dissociation sets in trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16639)
*Meiqin Wang,Min Xu,Ning Zhang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了树的极大解离集（maximal dissociation set）的最大数量问题。给定阶数为 n 的树，本文证明了极大解离集数量的最大值是一个分段函数 g(n)，并对所有 n≥3 的情形给出了精确上界和达成该上界的极值树刻画。这解决了 Wang 等人在 2025 年提出的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解离集（dissociation set）是 Yannakakis 于 1981 年提出的概念，指诱导子图中每个顶点的度数至多为 1 的顶点集合。Wang 等人在 2025 年已证明对于阶数 n≥4 的树，极大解离集的数量不超过 3^((n-1)/3) + (n-1)/3，并刻画了达到该上界的极值树，同时提出了关于极大解离集数量上界的猜想。本文的动机是验证并解决这一猜想。

Method: 本文针对 Wang 等人提出的猜想，对所有 n≥3 的树进行系统分析。通过将树按阶数 n 模 3 的余数分类（n≡0,1,2 mod 3），分别推导出极大解离集数量的最优上界 g(n)，并构造性地刻画达到各上界的极值树结构（包括特殊情况 n=9 和 n=21 的单独讨论）。

Result: 本文证明了：对于阶数 n≥3 的任意树，极大解离集的最大数量为 g(n)，其中 g(n) 是一个分段函数：当 n=3,4,5,6 时 g(n)=n；当 n≡1 (mod 3) 且 n≥7 时为 3^((n-1)/3) + (n-1)/3；当 n≡2 (mod 3) 且 n≥8 时为 4·3^((n-5)/3) + n-5；当 n≡0 (mod 3) 时，n=9 时为 19，n=21 时为 1349，其余 n≥12 的情形为 16·3^((n-9)/3) + 3n-25。同时给出了达成这些上界的极值树的完整刻画。

Conclusion: 本文完整解决了 Wang 等人提出的关于树中极大解离集最大数量的猜想，给出了对所有 n≥3 的树精确的极大解离集数量上界，并刻画了相应的极值树。这一结果推广和深化了 2025 年 Wang 等人的工作，为图论中解离集问题的研究提供了重要进展。

Abstract: Let $G$ be a simple graph. A dissociation set of $G$ proposed by Yannakakis in $1981$ is defined as a set of vertices that induces a subgraph in which every vertex has a degree of at most $1$. A dissociation set is maximal if it is not contained as a proper subset in any other dissociation set. In $2025$, Wang et al.\cite{ZiyuanWang} established that for any tree $T$ of order $n\geq 4$, the number of maximal dissociation sets in $T$ is at most $3^{\frac{n-1}{3}}+\frac{n-1}{3}$ and characterized the extremal trees attaining the upper bound. They also proposed a conjecture about the upper bound of the maximal dissociation set. In this paper, we consider this conjecture and show that the maximum number of maximal dissociation sets in a tree of order $n(n\geq 3)$ is $g(n)$, where \[ g(n) = \begin{cases} n, & n=3,4,5,6,\\ 3^{\frac{n-1}{3}}+\frac{n-1}{3}, & n \equiv 1 \pmod{3},~n\geq7,\\ 4\cdot 3^{\frac{n-5}{3}}+n-5, & n \equiv 2 \pmod{3},~n\geq8, \\ 16\cdot 3^{\frac{n-9}{3}}+3n-25, & n \equiv 0 \pmod{3},~n\geq12~\text{and }~n\neq21, \\ 19, & n=9, \\ 1349, & n=21. \end{cases} \] We also characterize the extremal trees with the maximum number of maximal dissociation sets.

</details>


### [99] [Nearly permanental cospectral graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16676)
*Weifang Lv,Quanyu Tang,Wei Wang,Hao Zhang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究图的近共谱问题（nearly cospectral graphs）。Lv等人此前对行列式意义下的近共谱图问题在模4情况下给出了部分结果，本文将对应结果推广到永久（permanent）情形，并进一步对任意不可约特征标对应的immanantal多项式，在有限域F_2[x]上建立了近immanantal共谱图的类似结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近共谱图问题是图论与代数组合中的有趣问题。此前Lv等人已在行列式（特征多项式）框架下引入了近共谱图问题并在模4情形给出了部分结论，但永久（permanental）情形及更一般的immanant情形尚未被研究。由于行列式和永久分别是S_n的两个不可约特征标对应的immanant的特例，将结果推广到永久以及所有immanant情景是自然的。

Method: 本文通过对图的邻接矩阵的行列式与永久多项式的结构性质进行分析，借助行列式与永久在模4（以及F_2[x]）意义下的代数性质，将Lv等人在行列式情形的结果平行推广到永久情形。进一步地，利用immanant的定义以及对称群S_n不可约特征标的性质，在有限域F_2[x]上对所有不可约特征标χ_λ建立了近immanantal共谱图的类似结论。

Result: 证明了行列式情形下Lv等人的近共谱图结果对于永久情形同样成立；并进一步在F_2[x]上对所有不可约特征标χ_λ建立了近immanantal共谱图的类似结果，将行列式与永久两种特殊情况统一到一个更一般的框架中。

Conclusion: 本文成功将近共谱图问题从行列式推广到永久，并进一步推广到一般immanant情形，在F_2[x]上对所有不可约特征标给出了统一的结果，丰富了近共谱图理论的研究。

Abstract: Let $G$ be a simple graph of order $n$ with adjacency matrix $A= (a_{ij})$. The \emph{determinant} and the \emph{permanen}t of the matrix $A$ are defined as \[\mathrm{det}A= \sum_{σ\in S_n}\mathrm{sgn}(σ) \prod_{i=1}^n a_{iσ(i)}\quad\text{and}\quad\mathrm{per}A= \sum_{σ\in S_n} \prod_{i=1}^n a_{iσ(i)},\]respectively. The polynomials $φ(G;x) =\mathrm{det}(xI-A(G))$ and $π(G;x) =\mathrm{per}(xI-A(G))$ are called the \emph{characteristic polynomial} and the \emph{permanental polynomial} of $G$, respectively. Two graphs are said to be \emph{nearly cospectral} with respect to the determinant (resp. permanent) if the difference of their characteristic (resp. permanental) polynomials is a constant. Lv et al. introduced the nearly cospectral graphs problem with respect to the determinant, and provided partial results in the case modulo 4. In this paper, we mainly prove that the corresponding results also hold for the nearly cospectral graphs problem with respect to the permanent. The determinant and permanent are the immanants corresponding to the irreducible characters $(1^n)$ and $(n)$ of the symmetric group $ S_n $, respectively. Here, the \emph{immanant} $d_λ(A)$ of $A$ is defined as \[d_λ(A) = \sum_{σ\in S_n} χ_λ(σ) \prod_{i=1}^n a_{iσ(i)},\] where $χ_λ$ is the irreducible character of $ S_n $ indexed by the partition $ λ$. The immanantal polynomial of $G$ associated with $ χ_λ$ is given by $ φ_λ(G;x)=d_λ(xI-A) $. In this paper, we also establish a similar result for nearly immanantal cospectral graphs in $\mathbb{F}_2[x]$ for all irreducible characters $χ_λ$.

</details>


### [100] [The resonance graphs of coronoid systems and nanotubes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16687)
*Lingmei Liang,Heping Zhang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文使用纯图论方法，给出了冠状系统（带孔六角系统）的完美匹配在共振图中处于同一连通分量的判据，该判据基于孔洞/外表面之间切割上的简单不变量——流。作为推论，得到了共振图连通性的判据，并构造了一个反例，推翻Tratnik等人提出的关于纳米管共振图连通性的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 六角系统的共振图是连通的，但带孔的冠状系统的共振图不一定连通。Saldanha等人使用同调与上同调理论给出了平面二次区域两个拼块属于同一翻转图连通分量的三种判据版本，本文希望用纯图论方法给出类似的组合判据。

Method: 采用纯图论（graph-theoretical）方法，通过定义孔洞/外表面之间切割上的简单不变量"流"（flow），刻画冠状系统两个完美匹配在同一共振图连通分量中的充要条件；并进一步将其推广讨论到纳米管情形。

Result: 给出了冠状系统两个完美匹配在同一共振图连通分量的基于"流"的判据，并由此得到共振图连通的判据。同时构造了一个共振图连通的纳米管，反证了Tratnik等人提出的猜想。

Conclusion: 本文以纯图论方式复现并简化了此前基于同调理论的判据，拓展了共振图连通性理论的应用范围，并纠正了文献中关于纳米管共振图连通性的一个错误猜想。

Abstract: The resonance graph of a hexagonal system is connected, which shows that a perfect matching can be transformed into any other perfect matchings by a series of flips along hexagons. However, the resonance graph of a coronoid system (with holes) is not necessarily connected. Saldanha et al. (Discrete Comput. Geom. 14 (1995) 207-233) used homology and cohomology theory to obtain three versions of criteria for two tilings of a quadriculated region in the plane to be in the same connected component of the flip graph. Inspiblack by the combinatorial version, in this paper we use a purely graph-theoretical approach to give a criterion in terms of simple invariant\textcolor{black}{---flow} across cuts between holes/exterior face for two perfect matchings of a coronoid system $G$ to be in the same connected component of its resonance graph. As a corollary we obtain a criterion for the resonance graph of a coronoid system to be connected. We also discuss whether such \textcolor{black}{criteria} are applicable to nanotubes, and construct a nanotube whose resonance graph is connected, which disproves a conjecture proposed by Tratnik et al. (MATCH Commun. Math. Comput. Chem. 74 (2015) 175-186).

</details>


### [101] [Codegree Thresholds for $λ$-Choosability of Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16740)
*Chunqiu Fang,Rongxing Xu*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究图的λ-可选择性（λ-choosability）问题。对于每个固定划分λ，引入最小q-码度（minimum q-codegree）替代最小度来刻画图不可λ-可选的条件，给出了阈值f(λ)的上下界，并在均分划分情形下精确确定了渐近阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Zhu提出的λ-赋色将普通着色和列表着色统一到同一框架中。Alon定理表明最小度为d的图的选择数至少为(1/2-o(1))log₂d，Saxton和Thomason用超图容器方法将其改进到最优常数1。自然的问题是：对任意固定划分λ，是否也存在类似的阈值现象？由于平衡完全二分图具有任意大的最小度但始终是{1,1}-可选的，最小度并不充分，需要寻找合适的替代量（即最小q-码度）。

Method: 作者定义最小q-码度δ_q(G)作为最小度的推广替代，证明了对于每个划分λ，当δ_q(G)足够大时图必不是λ-可选的，并定义阈值f(λ)。利用概率方法和容器方法等技术，给出了f(λ)的上界2^{(2q+o(1))|λ|}和下界(q+1)^{-1}(1+1/q)^{|λ|}。对于q个相等部分{k,...,k}的划分，通过精确分析确定了渐近阈值ρ_q^{-(1+o(1))k}，其中ρ_q是方程x=(1-x)^q的唯一解。

Result: 得到了如下主要结果：(1) 对任意固定q，f(λ)≤2^{(2q+o(1))|λ|}（|λ|→∞）；(2) 对所有λ，f(λ)≥(q+1)^{-1}(1+1/q)^{|λ|}；(3) 对q个相等部分的划分{k,...,k}，f({k,...,k})=ρ_q^{-(1+o(1))k}，确定渐近阈值；(4) 当q=1时，蕴含ch(G)≥(1-o(1))log₂δ(G)，恢复了Saxton-Thomason的常数1结果。

Conclusion: 本文表明最小q-码度是刻画λ-不可选择性阈值的正确参数，将Alon定理和Saxton-Thomason定理推广到了任意划分λ的框架下，并在均分情形给出了精确渐近阈值，完成了从常数1/2到1再到一般划分的系统性推广。

Abstract: Let $λ=\{k_1,\ldots,k_q\}$ be a partition, and let $|λ|=k_1+\cdots+k_q$. A $|λ|$-list assignment $L$ of a graph $G$ is a $λ$-assignment if its color set can be partitioned into $q$ disjoint sets $X_1,\ldots,X_q$ such that $|L(v)\cap X_i|=k_i$ for every vertex $v$ and every $i\in[q]$. This notion, introduced by Zhu [J. Combin. Theory Ser. B, 2020], puts ordinary coloring and list coloring in the same framework. A theorem of Alon [Random Structures Algorithms, 2000] states that every graph with minimum degree $d$ has choice number at least $(1/2-o(1))\log_2d$. Saxton and Thomason [Invent. Math., 2015] later used the hypergraph container method to replace $1/2$ by the sharp constant $1$. It is natural to ask whether a similar phenomenon holds for every fixed partition $λ$. Minimum degree alone is not sufficient: balanced complete bipartite graphs have arbitrarily large minimum degree but are always $\{1,1\}$-choosable. We show that the appropriate replacement is the minimum $q$-codegree, defined for $|V(G)|\geq q$ by $δ_q(G)=\min\{|N_G(S)|:S\subseteq V(G),\,|S|=q\}$.
  More precisely, for every partition $λ$ there exists an integer $d$ such that every graph $G$ with $δ_q(G)\geq d$ is not $λ$-choosable. Let $f(λ)$ be the least such $d$. For every fixed $q$, we prove $f(λ)\leq2^{(2q+o(1))|λ|}$ as $|λ|\to\infty$, while $f(λ)\geq(q+1)^{-1}(1+1/q)^{|λ|}$ for every $λ$. For the partition $\{k,\ldots,k\}$ with $q$ equal parts, we determine the threshold asymptotically: $f(\{k,\ldots,k\})=ρ_q^{-(1+o(1))k}$ as $k\to\infty$, where $ρ_q$ is the unique $x\in(0,1)$ satisfying $x=(1-x)^q$. When $q=1$, our result implies $\operatorname{ch}(G)\geq(1-o(1))\log_2δ(G)$.

</details>


### [102] [Explicit characterization of $\widehat{\mathfrak{g}}$-dominant tableaux for $n\le 4$ via $1$-$0$-slack recording tableaux in the quantum Littlewood-Richardson rule and other bijections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16800)
*Olga Azenhas*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文利用1-0-slack记录表通过promotion算子的复合定义Naito-Suzuki-Watanabe双射，对n≤4情形显式刻画了量子Littlewood-Richardson规则中由1-0-slack记录表产生的$\widehat{\mathfrak{g}}$-支配表，并将其与Littlewood-Richardson-Sundaram表的自然双射相联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前作者已用线性不等式显式刻画了量子LR规则中由1-0-slack记录表产生的$\mathfrak{k}$-最高权表，本文旨在对偶地显式刻画$\widehat{\mathfrak{g}}$-支配表，并建立与Naito-Sagaki猜想相关双射之间的联系。

Method: 利用promotion算子的复合来构造Naito-Suzuki-Watanabe双射，通过线性不等式对n≤4情形下由1-0-slack记录表产生的$\widehat{\mathfrak{g}}$-支配表进行显式刻画，并借助记录表与Littlewood-Richardson-Sundaram表的自然双射来关联其他双射结果。

Result: 对n≤4的情形，成功用特定的线性不等式显式刻画了量子LR规则中由1-0-slack记录表产生的$\widehat{\mathfrak{g}}$-支配表，并将这些结果与其他Naito-Sagaki猜想的双射建立了联系。

Conclusion: 本文完成了对$\widehat{\mathfrak{g}}$-支配表的显式线性不等式刻画，补充了对偶方向的量子LR规则理解，并通过LR-Sundaram表的自然双射统一了相关结论。

Abstract: Previously we have explicitly characterized by certain linear inequalities the ${\mathfrak{k}}$-highest weight tableaux in the quantum Littlewood-Richardson (LR) rule produced by $1$-$0$-slack recording tableaux. Using the composition of promotion operators to defining the Naito-Suzuki-Watanabe bijection between ${\mathfrak{k}}$-highest weight tableaux and $\widehat{\mathfrak{g}}$-dominant tableaux, we now explicitly characterize by certain linear inequalities the $\widehat{\mathfrak{g}}$-dominant tableaux for $n\le 4$ produced by $1$-$0$-slack recording tableaux in the quantum Littlewood-Richardson rule. Since recording tableaux in the quantum Littlewood-Richardson rule are in natural bijection with Littlewood-Richardson-Sundaram tableaux, we relate our results with other bijections for the Naito-Sagaki conjecture.

</details>


### [103] [A Local Central Limit Theorem for Clique Counts in Sparse Random Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16882)
*Asaf Cohen Antonir,Ilay Hoshen,Maksim Zhukovskii*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了对于任意固定阶数 r≥3 的团 K_r，在随机图 G_{n,p} 中其拷贝数 X_H 在 n^{-1/m(H)} << p ≤ 1/2 范围内满足局部中心极限定理（LCLT），从而基本解决了 Gilmer 和 Kopparty 关于团的一个猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gilmer 和 Kopparty 猜想：对于连通的固定图 H，当 p >> n^{-1/m(H)} 且 n^2(1-p) >> 1 时，X_H 满足局部中心极限定理。此前 Berkowitz、Sah 和 Sawhney 仅对常数 p 情形确认了该猜想，而 p = o(1) 的情形此前仅有三角形 K_3 被 Araújo 和 Mattos 解决。本文旨在将该结果推广到任意团 K_r。

Method: 本文通过推广 Araújo 和 Mattos 处理三角形的技术，针对一般团 K_r 建立了局部中心极限定理。方法涉及对随机图 G_{n,p} 中团拷贝数的高阶累积量的精细控制，以及利用图论中的最大密度 m(H) 来确定有效的 p 下界，并结合 Röllin 和 Ross 的一般性结果作为补充。

Result: 在 n^{-1/m(H)} << p ≤ 1/2 的范围内，对于任意固定的 r ≥ 3，K_r 的拷贝数满足局部中心极限定理。该结果将此前仅对三角形成立的结果推广到了任意团，基本解决了 Gilmer 和 Kopparty 猜想中关于团的情形。

Conclusion: 本文实质性地推进了 Gilmer-Kopparty 猜想的解决，将 LCLT 结果从三角形推广到任意规模的团，覆盖了此前长期未解决的 p = o(1) 区域。这为将来证明更一般图 H 的该猜想提供了重要的技术基础。

Abstract: Let $X_H$ denote the number of copies of a fixed graph $H$ in $G_{n, p}$. Gilmer and Kopparty conjectured that $X_H$ satisfies a local central limit theorem (LCLT) provided that $H$ is connected, $p \gg n^{-1/m(H)}$, and $n^2 (1-p) \gg 1$, where $m(H)$ is the maximum density.
  Following the work of Berkowitz, Sah and Sawhney confirmed this conjecture for every constant $p$, leaving the regime where $p=o(1)$ open. In this regime, the only case addressed in the literature is when $H=K_3$, where, in a recent paper, Araújo and Mattos confirmed the conjecture for $p \in (4n^{-1/2}, 1/2)$. This, together with a general result of Röllin and Ross, essentially settles the conjecture for the triangle. We generalise these results by showing that an LCLT holds for $H = K_r$ (for any fixed $r \ge 3$) in the regime $n^{-1/m(H)}\ll p\leq 1/2$, essentially settling the conjecture for cliques.

</details>


### [104] [Cubes in the Torus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16883)
*Douglas Barnes,Sean Jaffe*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了用\{0,1,...,p-1\}^n立方体的平移覆盖n维环面(ℤ/qℤ)^n所需的最小数量T(n,q,p)，证明了对于每个q存在常数Λ_q(1≤Λ_q≤2)，使得T(n,q,2)=(Λ_q+o(1))(q/2)^n。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定用低维立方体的平移覆盖环面所需的最小覆盖数是一个基本的组合几何问题，对于q>p的一般情形，精确确定T(n,q,p)的渐进行为具有重要意义。

Method: 作者通过构造上界和下界来证明T(n,q,2)的渐近公式，引入常数Λ_q并证明其取值范围为[1,2]，说明覆盖数的主项是以(q/2)^n为尺度、乘以一个仅依赖于q的常数因子。

Result: 建立了渐近公式T(n,q,2)=(Λ_q+o(1))(q/2)^n，其中Λ_q是仅依赖于q的常数，满足1≤Λ_q≤2。

Conclusion: 对于q>p的一般情况，当p=2时，覆盖数T(n,q,2)的渐近行为已被完全刻画，其主阶为(q/2)^n乘以一个与q相关的常数Λ_q。

Abstract: For $q> p$, let $T(n,q,p)$ be the minimum number of translates of the cube \(\{0,1,\dots,p-1\}^n\) required to cover the $n$-dimensional torus $(\mathbb{Z}/q\mathbb{Z})^n$. We show that for each $q$ there exists a constant $1\le Λ_q \le 2$ such that $T(n,q,2)=(Λ_q + o(1))(q/2)^n$.

</details>


<div id='math.FA'></div>

# math.FA [[Back]](#toc)

### [105] [Commutator estimates for functions of noncommuting self-adjoint operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16731)
*Vladimir Peller*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文研究了自伴算子对(A,B)在交换子[A,B]属于Schatten-von Neumann类S_p条件下的函数演算φ↦φ(A,B)的性质。研究发现，当p≤2时，定义在Besov类B^1_{∞,1}(R²)上的该演算满足交换子Lipschitz估计，且模S_p具有乘法性；而当p>2时不存在交换子Lipschitz估计，算子范数意义下同样不存在该估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究自伴算子对(A,B)的双变量函数演算的性质，特别是在交换子属于Schatten-von Neumann类S_p的约束下，探究该演算是否满足交换子Lipschitz估计及乘法性，以推广和深化双变量算子演算理论。

Method: 本文针对自伴算子对(A,B)定义演算φ↦φ(A,B)，其中[A,B]∈S_p。作者分析了该演算在Besov空间B^1_{∞,1}(R²)上的性质，分别研究p≤2与p>2两种情形下的交换子Lipschitz估计是否存在，并考察其模S_p的乘法性质。

Result: 当p≤2时，该演算在Besov类B^1_{∞,1}(R²)上满足交换子Lipschitz估计，并且模S_p具有乘法性。然而当p>2时，不存在交换子Lipschitz估计；在算子范数意义下也不存在交换子Lipschitz估计。

Conclusion: 双变量函数演算的交换子Lipschitz性质在p≤2的Schatten-von Neumann类框架下成立，但该性质在p>2时以及算子范数意义下均不成立，表明该演算的Lipschitz估计存在阈值界限（p=2），其适用范围受限于特定的Schatten类条件。

Abstract: We study properties of the calculus $\varphi\mapsto\varphi(A,B)$ for self-adjoint operators with commutator $[A,B]$ in the Schaten--von Neumann class $\boldsymbol{S}_p$. It turns out that this calculus defined on the Besov class $B_{\infty,1}^1({\Bbb R}^2)$ in the case $p\le2$ admits a commutator Lipschitz estimate and is multiplicative modulo $\boldsymbol{S}_p$. On the other hand in the case $p>2$ there is no commutator Lipschitz estimate. There is no commutator Lipschitz estimate in the operator norm as well.

</details>


### [106] [The König constant is one](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14817)
*Xinyuan Xie,Haonan Zhang*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文证明König双线性形式常数𝔎_K=1，通过在任意高维空间中构造布尔函数对(f,g)达到该上界，从而否定了BMMN论文中对高维情形下Grothendieck常数问题的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: König双线性形式源于确定Grothendieck常数精确值的研究。König猜想其最优值由一维半空间给出，但BMMN在二维中否定了该猜想并提出高维中交替舍入方案的问题。确定𝔎_K的高维行为对于理解Grothendieck常数至关重要。

Method: 通过基本Fourier论证已知𝔎_K≤1且对任意有限维不取等号。本文构造一族高维布尔函数对(f,g)，使得König双线性形式的值趋近于1，从而证明上界1可实现。

Result: 证明了𝔎_K=1，即存在高维布尔函数对使König双线性形式达到其上界1，对BMMN中关于高维Grothendieck常数问题的期望给出了否定回答。

Conclusion: 本文的核心结论是𝔎_K=1，表明König双线性形式在高维中的最大值可以达到平凡Fourier上界1，这排除了通过该形式在高维中改进Grothendieck常数下界的可能性。

Abstract: For each $N\geq1$, consider the normalized König bilinear form $B_{\mathrm K}:L_\infty(\mathbb R^N)\times L_\infty(\mathbb R^N)\to\mathbb R$ given by \[ B_{\mathrm K}(f,g):=\frac{1}{(\sqrt{2}π)^N} \iint_{\mathbb R^N\times\mathbb R^N} f(x)g(y)e^{-(\lVert x\rVert^2+\lVert y\rVert^2)/2} \sin\langle x,y\rangle\,\mathrm d x\,\mathrm d y, \] We define the König constant by \[ \mathfrak K_{\mathrm K}:=\sup_{N\geq1}\sup_{\substack{f,g:\mathbb R^N\to\{\pm1\}\\ f,g\ \mathrm{measurable}}}B_{\mathrm K}(f,g). \]
  The study of this bilinear form arose from efforts to determine the exact value of the Grothendieck constant. König~\cite{KONIG} conjectured that the sharp value should instead be given by the one-dimensional half-spaces $B_{\mathrm K}(\operatorname{sgn}(x_1),\operatorname{sgn}(x_1))=\frac{2}π\log(1+\sqrt{2})$. A positive answer to this conjecture, together with a classical upper bound of Krivine \cite{KRIVINE}, would determine the exact value of the Grothendieck constant. In a breakthrough~\cite{BMMN}, Braverman, Makarychev, Makarychev, and Naor disproved König's conjecture already in dimension two and used their counterexamples to obtain the first strict improvement over Krivine's bound. One question in \cite{BMMN} attempts to determine the Grothendieck constant through alternating Krivine rounding schemes arising from König's bilinear form in high dimension. More recently, Li et al.~\cite{LISK} constructed high-dimensional examples showing that $\mathfrak K_{\mathrm K}\ge 0.59357$.
  An elementary Fourier argument gives $\mathfrak K_{\mathrm K}\le 1$ and excludes equality for every finite-dimension. In this paper, we prove that $\mathfrak K_{\mathrm K}=1$ by constructing a family of Boolean pairs in high dimensions. In particular, this gives a negative answer to the high-dimensional aspect of the question in \cite{BMMN}.

</details>


### [107] [Rigidity of isometries on vector and normed lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14884)
*Noel Murasko,Tomasz Szczepanski*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文研究向量格和赋范格上由等距、保序和保模条件引发的刚性（rigidity）现象。首先发展了中点单射函数理论并推广了Baker定理；然后证明每个模等距（modulus isometry）都是仿射的且线性部分保不交性；最后证明了一个非线性序等距刚性定理，即保序范数等距保持确界上下限、保不交性且不交可加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在向量格和赋范格理论中，理解由等距、保序和保模等自然条件所隐含的结构刚性是核心问题之一。作者旨在推广Baker关于Mazur-Ulam定理的非满射版本，并系统性地研究模等距（modulus isometry）和序等距（order isometry）的刚性性质，揭示这些映射在何种条件下必然是仿射的、保不交性的或具有格同态的线性部分。

Method: 本文采用理论推导的方法：(1) 建立中点单射（midpoint injective）函数的理论框架，并研究其与凸性和单调性的联系，以此推广Baker定理；(2) 利用推广后的Baker定理，证明模等距在向量格和赋范格设定下的仿射性和线性部分的结构性质；(3) 对于序等距，通过平移归零、结合严格单调范数条件，证明其保持确界、保不交性和不交可加性，并构造显式非线性等距例子说明结论不蕴含线性。

Result: (1) 建立了中点单射函数理论并推广了Baker定理；(2) 证明模等距一定是仿射的且线性部分保不交性，当定义域为理想时线性部分是自身的双射且为对合；(3) 证明赋范格中的正范数等距（余域严格凸）是仿射的且线性部分为格同态；(4) 证明保序范数等距（余域有严格单调范数）保持上下确界、保不交性且不交可加，并通过L_1[0,1]上的显式非线性等距族说明这些结论不蕴含线性。

Conclusion: 本文系统地揭示了向量格和赋范格中等距、保序和保模条件下映射的刚性结构。模等距必然是仿射的且线性部分保不交性；在严格凸或严格单调范数条件下，等距还具有更强的结构性质（如格同态、保持确界等）。但这些刚性结论并不足以迫使映射线性化，存在本质上非线性的等距，说明刚性理论有其精确的边界。

Abstract: We study rigidity phenomena for maps on vector and normed lattices arising from isometric, order-theoretic, and modulus-preserving conditions. We first investigate the role of strict convexity in Baker's nonsurjective version of the Mazur-Ulam theorem. In the process, we develop a theory of midpoint injective functions and investigate their connections with convexity and monotonicity. Using this framework, we prove a generalization of Baker's theorem.
  As our first main application, we show that every modulus isometry, i.e., a map $T:X\to Y$, where $X$ is a sublattice of a vector lattice $Y$, satisfying $$|T(x)-T(y)|=|x-y|, \qquad x,y\in X,$$ is affine and has a disjointness-preserving linear part. When $X$ is an ideal, the linear part is moreover a bijection of $X$ onto itself and an involution. In the normed lattice setting, we also show that every positive norm isometry into a strictly convex normed lattice is not only affine, but has a linear part that is a lattice homomorphism.
  Finally, we prove a nonlinear order-isometric rigidity theorem. After shifting to the origin, every order-preserving norm isometry between normed lattices whose codomain has a strictly monotone norm, preserves suprema and infima and is both disjointness preserving and disjointly additive. These conclusions do not force linearity, as shown by an explicit family of nonlinear isometries on $L_1[0,1]$.

</details>


### [108] [Monomial bases for holomorphic functions on Banach spaces with an unconditional basis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14926)
*Thiago Grando*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文证明了：对于具有无条件Schauder基的复Banach空间X，与基相关的单项式（每个齐次度上按平方序排列，整体上按任意相容序排列）构成了空间(H(X),τ₀)（X上全体整函数空间配备紧开拓扑）的一个Schauder基。证明依赖于两个关键思想：坐标尾部条件给出紧致固体子集的基本系统，以及Fourier投影给出基常数上界c_{K,n}≤n+1。该结果可应用于Banach序列格、向量值E-和以及Lorentz预对偶d_*(w,1)等。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在有限维情形，整函数空间的单项式基是一个经典结果。然而在无限维Banach空间上，将单项式构造为Schauder基是一个长期未解决的问题。作者希望将该经典理论推广到具有无条件Schauder基的无限维复Banach空间，建立紧开拓扑下整函数空间的单项式Schauder基理论。

Method: 证明包含两个核心步骤：(1) 利用坐标尾部条件（coordinate-tail conditions）构造X中紧致固体子集（compact solid subsets）的基本系统，为紧开拓扑提供适当的局部基；(2) 通过坐标上的Fourier投影，在每个齐次度上建立单项式关于紧集K上一致范数的基常数估计c_{K,n}≤n+1。通过将单项式按平方序（square order）排列在齐次度内、按相容序排列在整体上，构造出(H(X),τ₀)的Schauder基。

Result: 成功证明了在具有无条件Schauder基的复Banach空间X上，与基相容的单项式族构成(H(X),τ₀)的Schauder基。该结果覆盖了丰富的具体应用：经典序列空间、Lorentz空间、Orlicz心、Schreier型序列空间，以及c₀和ℓ_r混合的有限维ℓ_p空间和（向量值）E-和，包括Lorentz预对偶d_*(w,1)。

Conclusion: 该工作将有限维整函数空间的单项式基理论成功推广到一类广泛的无限维Banach空间（具有无条件Schauder基的空间），并获得了关于基常数的统一估计c_{K,n}≤n+1。这为无限维复分析中整函数空间的基理论提供了新的工具和广泛的应用场景。

Abstract: Let $X$ be a complex Banach space with an unconditional Schauder basis. We prove that the monomials associated with this basis, endowed in each homogeneous degree with the square order and globally with any compatible ordering, form a Schauder basis for the space $(\calH(X),τ_0)$ of entire holomorphic functions on $X$ with the compact-open topology. The proof relies on two main ideas. First, coordinate-tail conditions yield a fundamental system of compact solid subsets of $X$. Second, Fourier projections in the coordinates give the uniform estimate $c_{K,n}\leq n+1$ for the basis constant of the degree-$n$ monomials with respect to the supremum seminorm on each such compact set $K$. We derive applications to Banach sequence lattices with dense $c_{00}$, including classical, Lorentz, Orlicz-heart and Schreier-type sequence spaces, and to general vector-valued $E$-sums. In particular, the result covers mixed $c_0$- and $\ell_r$-sums of finite-dimensional $\ell_p$ spaces and the Lorentz predual $d_*(w,1)$.

</details>


### [109] [Stability of Individually Eventually Positive Semigroups on $L^p$-Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15038)
*Loris Arnold*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文证明了在$L^p$-空间（$1<p<\infty$）上，个体最终正$C_0$-半群的增长界与其生成子的谱界相等，回答了Vogt提出的一个问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vogt提出了一个开放问题：个体最终正半群的增长界是否等于谱界。此前该结论仅在一致最终正的情形下成立，个体情形需要新的工具来弥合个体性与均匀性之间的差距。

Method: 论文沿用了Vogt在一致最终正情况下的证明策略，关键的新工具是将Arora和Glück的算子值域均匀化原理推广到时间相关的算子值域情形。将该原理应用于主理想时，可将个体最终正性转化为Vogt稳定性论证所需的均匀支配估计。

Result: 成功证明了在$L^p$-空间（$1<p<\infty$）上，个体最终正$C_0$-半群的增长界与生成子的谱界重合，从而回答了Vogt提出的问题。

Conclusion: 个体最终正性在$L^p$-空间上足以保证增长界与谱界的一致性，这推广了之前仅在一致最终正情况下已知的结果，并展示了时间相关算子值域均匀化原理在正半群理论中的有效性。

Abstract: We answer a question raised by Vogt by proving that the growth bound of an individually eventually positive $C_0$-semigroup on an $L^p$-space ($1 < p < \infty$) coincides with the spectral bound of its generator. Our proof follows the general strategy of Vogt's argument for the uniformly eventually positive case. The key new ingredient is a variant of an operator-range uniformisation principle of Arora and Glück, adapted here to time-dependent operator ranges. When applied to principal ideals, this principle turns individual eventual positivity into the uniform domination estimate required for Vogt's stability argument.

</details>


### [110] [New perspectives on operator radius bounds in $A-$weighted frameworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15059)
*Bikram Das,Chandal Nahak*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文利用有界线性算子的Moore-Penrose逆，在半希尔伯特空间中推导了算子之和的数值半径和算子范数的若干界，推广并改进了经典不等式；建立了关于n×n算子矩阵的A-Davis-Wielandt半径的新不等式，并探讨了其推论（特别针对2×2算子矩阵），最终给出了涉及算子交换子的A-数值半径的改进上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在半希尔伯特空间中，算子不等式与数值半径的估计是算子理论的重要课题。已有经典界在一般半希尔伯特空间中不够精细，需要借助Moore-Penrose逆这一工具来推广和优化。

Method: 利用Moore-Penrose逆刻画有界线性算子，推导算子之和的数值半径与算子范数的上界；通过A-Davis-Wielandt半径研究n×n算子矩阵并推广到2×2情形；最后利用所得结果改进交换子算子的A-数值半径不等式。

Result: 获得了推广并改进经典界的算子数值半径和范数不等式；建立了A-Davis-Wielandt半径的新不等式，特别是对2×2算子矩阵给出了具体界；得到了交换子算子的改进A-数值半径上界。

Conclusion: 论文在半希尔伯特空间框架下，以Moore-Penrose逆为关键工具，系统推广了数值半径、Davis-Wielandt半径及交换子不等式，所得结果比经典界更优。

Abstract: By employing the Moore$-$Penrose inverse of a bounded linear operator, we derive several bounds for the numerical radius and operator norms of the sum of operators in semi$-$Hilbertian space that generalize and improve the classical bounds. We establish novel inequalities pertaining to the $\mathbb{A}$$-$Davis$-$Wielandt radius for $n \times n$ operator matrices and further explore their ramifications, particularly concerning $\mathbb{A}$$-$Davis$-$Wielandt radius bounds for $2 \times 2$ operator matrices, where diagonal operator matrix $\mathbb{A}$ contains positive bounded operator $A.$ Ultimately, we get an improved upper bound for the $A$$-$numerical radius inequalities relating to the commutators of operators.

</details>


### [111] [Entrywise Loewner Preservers on Min and Max Matrix Cones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15125)
*Wei Xie*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文研究了由实数序列生成的Min矩阵和Max矩阵，给出了保正半定、保全非负、保Loewner序和保Loewner凸性的逐元素映射的精确刻画。核心结果包括：保Loewner序等价于函数非降且凸（无需连续性假设）；在Min和Max锥上，Loewner凸性不等式在任意对上成立时强制函数为仿射函数；若限制在Loewner可比对上，则自动得到f∈C¹且等价于f和f'均为凸。所有条件在二维情况下即可检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Min矩阵和Max矩阵是两类具有特殊锥结构的矩阵族，在组合数学、图论和矩阵理论中具有重要应用。研究这些矩阵族上的逐元素函数保持性（如保正半定性、保Loewner序等）有助于理解函数作用于矩阵时的结构保持规律，并为Matrix Analysis中的Schur变换提供新的理论支撑。

Method: 利用Min和Max矩阵的经典锥参数化方法，将矩阵锥的逐元素函数保持性问题转化为对实数序列和函数性质的分析。通过二维情形的检测，将高维条件归结为函数的单调性、凸性等基本分析性质。同时涉及幂函数值域、Loewner序自同构以及严格Min/Max类的刻画，并与逆M-矩阵和振荡矩阵建立联系。

Result: (1) 保Loewner序逐元素等价于f非降且凸（无需连续或可微假设）；(2) 在任意对上要求Loewner凸性不等式会强制f仿射；在Loewner可比对上则等价于f与f'均凸，且自动推出f∈C¹；(3) 保正半定等价于f非负且非降，该条件同样刻画全非负保持；(4) 确定了幂函数值域、Loewner序自同构及严格Min/Max锥的逐元素保持者。

Conclusion: 本文完整刻画了Min和Max矩阵族上的逐元素保持问题，揭示了Loewner序保持与函数凸单调性之间的深刻联系。所有保持性条件均在二维即可检测，简化了验证难度。严格Min/Max类与逆M-矩阵、振荡矩阵的关系为这些矩阵类的进一步应用提供了新视角。

Abstract: Let \[ A_{\min}(x)=\bigl(x_{\min(i,j)}\bigr)_{i,j=1}^n, \qquad A_{\max}(x)=\bigl(x_{\max(i,j)}\bigr)_{i,j=1}^n \] be the Min and Max matrices generated by a real sequence \(x=(x_1,\ldots,x_n)\). Using their classical cone parametrizations, we give exact characterizations of entrywise maps preserving positive semidefiniteness, total nonnegativity, Loewner order, and Loewner convexity.
  Our main results concern the Loewner structure.Without assuming continuity or differentiability, entrywise Loewner-order preservation is equivalent to \(f\) being nondecreasing and convex. On the Min and Max cones, requiring the Loewner-convexity inequality on arbitrary pairs is rigid and forces \(f\) to be affine. Under the standard convention of restricting the inequality to Loewner-comparable pairs, the condition automatically forces \(f\in C^1([0,\infty))\) and is equivalent to convexity of both \(f\) and \(f'\). The analogous statement holds for Loewner concavity. In each case, the condition is already detected in dimension two.
  We also show that \(f:[0,\infty)\to\mathbb R\) preserves positive semidefiniteness entrywise on all positive semidefinite Min or Max matrices if and only if \(f\) is nonnegative and nondecreasing; the same condition characterizes total-nonnegativity preservation. Finally, we determine the power-function ranges and Loewner-order automorphisms, characterize the entrywise preservers of the strict Min and Max classes, and relate these strict cones to inverse \(M\)-matrices and oscillatory matrices.

</details>


### [112] [A Continuous digit projector from binary representations of numbers onto $A_2$-representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15158)
*O. O. Nikorak,S. P. Ratushniak*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文引入了一个连续函数作为经典二进制数字投影到A₂-连分数表示（零冗余）数字的模拟，证明了该函数是良定义、连续且单调的，并通过Lebesgue定理确立了其奇异性质，同时建立了该函数与右移位算子及逆映射之间的关系，进而证明了逆映射的奇异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: A₂-连分数表示的数字投影是不连续函数，因为该表示在拓扑上不等价于经典二进制表示。因此需要引入一个连续的模拟函数，以便更好地理解A₂-连分数表示的结构性。

Method: 构造一个从二进制数字序列到A₂-连分数表示数字序列的映射函数f，形式为f(Δ²_{α₁α₂...α₂ₙ₋₁α₂ₙ...}) = Δ^{A₂}_{(1/2)^{1-α₁}(1/2)^{α₂}...(1/2)^{1-α₂ₙ₋₁}(1/2)^{α₂ₙ}...}。利用二进制表示的数的正规性质以及Lebesgue定理（连续单调函数几乎处处具有有限导数）来建立f的奇异性，并通过建立f与右移位算子及逆映射之间的关系来确立逆映射的奇异性。

Result: 证明了该函数f是良定义、连续且单调的，并且由于与其相关的数字序列的二进制的正规性，函数f是奇异的（即导数几乎处处为零或无穷）。同时通过该函数与右移位算子和连分数逆映射的关系，确立了逆映射的奇异性。

Conclusion: 本文成功构造了一个连续单调的奇异函数，模拟了二进制表示与A₂-连分数表示之间的数字转换关系，并利用这一工具进一步揭示了A₂-连分数表示的逆映射具有奇异性质。

Abstract: As is known, the $A_2$-continued representation of numbers is not topologically equivalent to the classical binary representation; therefore, the digit projector of such representations is a discontinuous function. In this paper, we introduce a continuous function that serves as an analogue of the digit projector of the classical binary representation of numbers into the digits of the $A_2$-continued representation with zero redundancy, namely a function of the form \[f(Δ^2_{α_1α_2...α_{2n-1}α_{2n}...})= Δ^{A_2}_{(\frac{1}{2})^{1-α_1}(\frac{1}{2})^{α_2}... (\frac{1}{2})^{1-α_{2n-1}}(\frac{1}{2})^{α_{2n}}...}, α_n\in \{0,1\}.\] It is proved that the function $f$ is well-defined, continuous, and monotone. Using the normal properties of numbers with respect to their binary representation and Lebesgue's theorem asserting the existence of a finite derivative for a continuous monotone function almost everywhere, the singularity of the function $f$ is established.
  The paper also establishes a relationship between the considered function, the right-shift operator on digits, and the inversor of the continued representation of numbers. This relationship is then used to establish the singularity of the inversor.

</details>


### [113] [Operator Inequalities and Several Characterizations of the $λ$-Mean Transform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15180)
*Bikram Das,Goutam Biswas,Chandal Nahak*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文推广了Buzano型不等式，为AXB型算子提供新的数值半径上界，并对λ-均值变换M_λ(T)的数值半径性质进行了深入探讨，包括反例、不可比较性分析、σ-类的引入、张量积性质及范数保持条件的等价刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展已有的Buzano型不等式结果（由Sababheh等人获得），使其适用于更一般的AXB型算子；同时深入理解λ-均值变换M_λ(T)在数值半径、范数保持等方面的性质，填补该领域的研究空白。

Method: (1) 将Buzano型不等式推广到AXB型算子以获得新颖的数值半径界；(2) 通过构造反例证明r_σ(M_λ(T))≤r_σ(T)对λ∈(0,1)一般不成立；(3) 建立新的σ-类并证明M_λ(T^*)=(M_λ(T))^*成立的充要条件；(4) 研究算子T与张量积T⊗S的变换性质，细化Zamani不等式，统一算子模界|T̃|≤|T̂|≤|T|；(5) 将2×2非对角块算子矩阵在λ-均值变换下的范数估计精确化。

Result: 成功推广了Buzano型不等式至AXB型算子；给出了反例表明r_σ(M_λ(T))≤r_σ(T)在λ∈(0,1)时一般不成立；证明了(r_ω(M_λ(T)))^n与r_ω(T^n)对n≥2通常不可比较；确立M_λ(T^*)=(M_λ(T))^*对λ∈[0,1)成立当且仅当T属于新定义的σ-类；证明了范数保持条件‖T̃‖=‖T‖与‖M_λ(T)‖=‖T‖等价于‖T²‖=‖T‖²；提供了2×2非对角块算子矩阵在λ-均值变换下的精确范数估计。

Conclusion: 本文在算子数值半径理论方面取得系统性进展，不仅推广了Buzano型不等式和统一了算子模界，还深刻揭示了λ-均值变换在数值半径和范数保持方面的本质性质，并通过σ-类的引入建立了伴随算子与λ-均值变换相容性的完整刻画。

Abstract: We broaden Buzano-type inequalities to provide novel numerical radius bounds for operators of the type $AXB$, thereby generalizing the results obtained by Sababheh et al. For the $λ$-mean transform $M_λ(T)$, we provide a counterexample demonstrating that $r_σ(M_λ(T)) \le r_σ(T)$ fails to hold in general for $λ\in (0, 1)$, establish that $(r_ω(M_λ(T)))^n$ and $r_ω(T^n)$ are typically incomparable for $n \ge 2$, and confirm that $M_λ(T^*) = (M_λ(T))^*$ is valid for $λ\in [0, 1)$ if and only if $T$ is a member of a newly established $σ$-class. Furthermore, we examine the transformation characteristics of $T$ and the tensor products $T \otimes S$, refine Zamani's inequalities, and unify operator modulus bounds $|\widetilde{T}| \le |\widehat{T}| \le |T|$. In this application, we demonstrate that the conditions for norm preservation, $\|\widetilde{T}\| = \|T\|$ and $\|M_λ(T)\| = \|T\|$, are equivalent to the statement $\|T^2\| = \|T\|^2$, and we offer precise norm estimates for $2 \times 2$ off-diagonal block operator matrices under $λ$-mean transformation.

</details>


### [114] [Near-Parseval orbit frames for irreducible unitary representations: from mixing and expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15182)
*Vignon Oussa*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文建立了投影C0酉表示的一个抽象存储定理，该定理保证在具有共轭逃逸对的条件下，存在单个轨道使得其采样集相对于投影核是相对分离的，且帧界逼近(1-ε)²和(1+ε)²。由此导出两个应用：非交换指数李群的左正则表示具有近Parseval平移帧，以及指数李群的无限维不可约酉表示具有近Parseval离散轨道帧；当有效投影商为交换时，轨道可选取为从Weyl格搬运的正交规范基。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究投影C0酉表示的存储与采样理论，旨在为更广泛的一类群表示（特别是李群表示）建立近Parseval帧的构造机制，从而推广已有的采样定理并解决指数李群上是否存在高质量离散轨道帧的问题。

Method: 通过引入"共轭逃逸"条件（一对单参数子群），证明一个抽象的存储定理，确保存在单个轨道使采样集相对分离且帧界任意接近1。该机制统一处理两个应用场景：对非交换指数李群的左正则表示，以及对无限维不可约酉表示，利用指数李群的投影商结构，将问题约化到有效投影商（交换情形对应Weyl格）来处理。

Result: 1) 每个非交换指数李群的左正则表示存在近Parseval左平移帧；2) 正维指数李群是FT群当且仅当它是非交换的，特别地三维Heisenberg群存在此类帧；3) 指数李群的每个无限维不可约酉表示存在近Parseval离散轨道帧；4) 当有效投影商为交换时，轨道可为从Weyl格搬运得到的正交规范基。

Conclusion: 该抽象存储定理为投影酉表示的离散轨道帧提供了统一且近乎最优的构造方法，同时解决并部分完成了FT群的刻画问题，展示了从交换到非交换情形下采样理论的自然推广。

Abstract: We establish an abstract storage theorem for projectively $C_0$ unitary representations admitting a pair of one-parameter subgroups with conjugation escape. It produces, for every $0<\varepsilon<1$, a single orbit whose sampling set is relatively separated modulo the projective kernel and whose frame bounds are $(1-\varepsilon)^2$ and $(1+\varepsilon)^2$. Two applications are obtained from the same mechanism. First, every nonabelian exponential Lie group admits a near-Parseval frame of left translates for its left regular representation. Consequently, a positive-dimensional exponential Lie group is an FT group if and only if it is nonabelian; in particular, the three-dimensional Heisenberg group admits such a frame. Second, every infinite-dimensional irreducible unitary representation of an exponential Lie group admits a near-Parseval discrete orbit frame. When the effective projective quotient is abelian, the orbit may be chosen to be an orthonormal basis arising from a transported Weyl lattice.

</details>


### [115] [Banach Spaces Generated by Finite-Valued Functions: Superreflexive Rigidity, Hankel Operators, and Universality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15185)
*Miroslav Hristov,Atanas Ilchev,Diana Nedelcheva,Boyan Zlatanov*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文研究由取值于有限集合的一致有界函数族生成的Banach空间，证明此类空间超自反当且仅当生成族有限（等价于空间有限维）。核心工具是有限范围蔓延模型障碍。将该原理应用于由形式语言左导数的特征函数生成的语言空间，得到正则语言的几何刻画（有限维性、超自反性、等价一致凸范数的存在性），并揭示正则与非正则情形之间的鲜明对比，包括一个能等距嵌入所有可分实Banach空间的二元语言构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Banach空间几何性质与生成函数族基数之间的内在关系，特别是将形式语言理论与Banach空间几何建立联系，希望从几何视角给出正则语言的新刻画，并理解非正则语言对应的空间复杂性。

Method: 提出有限范围蔓延模型（finite-range spreading-model）障碍技术，证明无限个有限值函数族在一致范数下无法生成超自反空间。利用左导数特征函数构造语言空间，通过紧移位轨道闭包上的连续函数空间进行坐标函数表示，并分析对应的Hankel算子。

Result: 证明了有限生成族等价于超自反性等价于有限维性。语言空间有限维/超自反/具有等价一致凸范数均等价于语言为正则语言。正则语言的Hankel算子紧致，非正则情形下确定了该算子与紧算子及有限秩算子的精确距离并计算了所有非平凡逼近数。构造了一个二元语言，其Banach空间等距包含所有可分实Banach空间。

Conclusion: 正则语言对应Banach空间具有强几何刚性（有限维性），而非正则语言中可能存在极端普适性（等距嵌入所有可分空间），形成鲜明对比，揭示了形式语言复杂度与Banach空间几何之间的深刻联系。

Abstract: We investigate Banach spaces generated by uniformly bounded families of functions taking values in a fixed finite set and establish a rigidity principle connecting the cardinality of the generating family with the geometry of its closed linear span. We prove that such a space is superreflexive precisely when the generating family is finite, or equivalently, when the resulting Banach space is finite-dimensional. The main ingredient is a finite-range spreading-model obstruction showing that an infinite family of finite-valued functions cannot generate a superreflexive space under the supremum norm. This principle is applied to Banach spaces generated by the characteristic functions of the left derivatives of formal languages. It yields geometric characterizations of regular languages in terms of finite dimensionality, superreflexivity, and the existence of an equivalent uniformly convex norm. We also obtain a canonical representation of the associated language space as a coordinate-function subspace of a space of continuous functions on a compact shift-orbit closure. The corresponding language Hankel operator is shown to be compact exactly for regular languages. In the nonregular case, we determine its exact distance from both the compact and finite-rank operators and compute all its nontrivial approximation numbers. Finally, we construct a single binary language whose associated Banach space contains an isometric copy of every separable real Banach space. These results reveal a sharp contrast between the geometric rigidity associated with regular languages and the universality that may occur in the nonregular setting.

</details>


### [116] [Vector-Valued Hardy--Littlewood Maximal Inequalities on Ball Banach Function Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15511)
*Yoshihiro Sawano,Mitsuo Izuki,Takahiro Noi*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文研究了非中心Hardy-Littlewood极大算子在球Banach函数空间及其Köthe对偶空间上的有界性，通过向量值极大不等式来刻画这一性质，补充了Nieraeth近期的工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在调和分析和函数空间理论中，极大算子的有界性是核心问题。已有的中心化极大算子在ball Banach函数空间上的有界性已有研究，但非中心化情形的刻画尚不完整。

Method: 利用向量值极大不等式作为工具，将非中心Hardy-Littlewood极大算子在球Banach函数空间及其Köthe对偶空间上的有界性进行特征化刻画。

Result: 给出了非中心Hardy-Littlewood极大算子有界性的充分必要条件，以向量值极大不等式的形式呈现，补充并拓展了Nieraeth的相关结果。

Conclusion: 向量值极大不等式为描述非中心极大算子在球Banach函数空间上的有界性提供了统一的刻画框架，深化了对该问题的理解。

Abstract: The goal of this paper is to characterize the boundedness property of the uncentered Hardy--Littlewood maximal operator on ball Banach function spaces and their Köthe associate spaces in terms of the vector-valued maximal inequality. The main result of this paper supplements the recent results by Nieraeth.

</details>


### [117] [The unit ball of quadratic forms on an octagonal sector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15644)
*Manwook Han,Sun Kwang Kim,Gustavo A. Muñoz-Fernández,Juan B. Seoane--Sepúlveda*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文研究了正则八边形第一象限扇区上二次型P(x,y)=ax²+bxy+cy²的supremum范数，给出了完整的五区域公式，证明了单位球在ac平面上的投影恰为[-1,1]²，并刻画了单位球的所有极点，将其归结为四条单参数曲线和有限个孤立多项式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 这是关于凸几何与分析学交叉领域的研究。作者意识到在该多项式空间上的后续凸极值问题需要对其单位球结构有充分理解，而目前缺少对八边形扇区上二次型范数的完整显式描述，因此开展这项系统性刻画工作。

Method: 研究者首先将凸区域定义为第一象限扇区O={x+y≤√2}，并基于范数接触点所处位置（端点或三条径向边内部的某一处）将问题划分为五种情形，推导出完整的五区域范数公式。随后通过投影分析证实单位球在ac平面上的投影恰为[-1,1]²，计算每个竖直截面的两个端点，进而参数化整个单位球面。最后对单位球的极点进行了完整刻画。

Result: 获得了:(1) 五区域范数公式的完整显式表达;(2) 单位球在ac平面上的投影恰好是[-1,1]²;(3) 每个竖直截面的两端点均可计算，从而单位球面可完全参数化;(4) 单位球的极端点由四条显式曲线、它们的相反曲线以及四对孤立点构成。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了完全显式的描述，将这一多项式空间上的后续凸极值问题简化为一族单参数优化问题和有限多个孤立多项式的检查，为后续在此空间上进行优化求解奠定了完备的几何基础。

Abstract: Let \[\mathfrak O=\{(x,y)\in[0,1]^2:x+y\le \sqrt2\} \] be the first-quadrant sector of a regular octagon. For quadratic forms \(P(x,y)=ax^2+bxy+cy^2\), we study the supremum norm over \(\mathfrak O\). We obtain a complete five-region formula for the norm, according to whether the norming contact occurs at an endpoint or in the interior of one of the three radial sides. We then prove that the projection of the unit ball onto the \(ac\)-plane is exactly \([-1,1]^2\), compute both endpoints of every vertical section, and thereby parametrize the entire unit sphere. Finally, we characterize the extreme points of the unit ball as four explicit curves, their negatives, and four pairs of isolated points. The resulting description is fully explicit and reduces subsequent convex extremal problems on this polynomial space to four one-parameter families and finitely many isolated polynomials.

</details>


### [118] [Uniform sine-kernel determinant asymptotics and prolate eigenvalue bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15808)
*Ahmadreza Azimifard*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文研究一维sinc核浓度算子S_c在低半区间的特征值计数下界，证明了一个关于D(δ,c)的均匀下界估计，主要分析工具是一个新的带增长参数的sine-kernel行列式渐近公式（γ<0情形），并通过IIKS最速下降法直接证明，还推导出头分位数的均匀公式及相关推论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: sine-kernel浓度算子的特征值分布（即Slepian问题的高维推广）在时间-频率分析、信号处理及随机矩阵理论中具有重要地位。下半个特征值计数D(δ,c)的精确估计此前缺乏系统性结果，研究者需要建立一个新的分析框架来填补这一空白，特别是针对γ<0的参数区间（此前Bothner-Deift-Its-Krasovsky仅处理了γ>0情形）。

Method: 核心方法包括：(1) 通过直接的IIKS（Its-Izergin-Korepin-Slavnov）最速下降分析证明带增长参数的sine-kernel行列式渐近公式，而非通过参数延拓；(2) 利用正定性保证可解性，从Kummer函数推导confluent-hypergeometric模型，并在Stokes扇区相消后估计端点匹配；(3) 利用酉等价S_c≃K_{πc/2}、Barnes G函数下界以及双向行列式约化来建立桥估计。

Result: 证明了对固定α≥4及充分大的c，D(δ,c)≥(1/32π²)L ln(αc/L)在c^{-α}≤δ≤δ₀上均匀成立；建立了γ<0情形的sine-kernel行列式渐近公式；证明了头分位数N_{1-ε}(c)的均匀公式及其上半区、张量积和联合范围推论。

Conclusion: 该工作为sine-kernel浓度算子的特征值在低半区间的计数问题提供了精确的均匀下界，补充了γ<0情形的行列式渐近理论，完善了对Slepian特征值分布的整体理解。深区间的互补结果由Kulikov和Larsen的定理给出，两者结合形成了对该问题较完整的刻画。

Abstract: Let $S_c = P_{(0,c)} Q P_{(0,c)}$ be the one-dimensional sinc-kernel concentration operator, with eigenvalues $1 > λ_0(c) \ge λ_1(c) \ge \cdots > 0$. We study the lower-half count $D(δ,c) = \#\{n : δ< λ_n(c) \le 1/2\}$ and prove, for a fixed $α\ge 4$ and all sufficiently large $c$, that $D(δ,c) \ge (1/32π^2) L \ln(αc / L)$ with $L = \ln(1/δ)$, uniformly on $c^{-α} \le δ\le δ_0$. The complementary deep range follows from the theorem of Kulikov and Larsen.
  The main analytic input is a signed, growing-parameter sine-kernel determinant asymptotic: for each fixed $A > 0$, uniformly for $0 \le ω\le A \ln s$,
  $\ln \det(I + (e^{2ω}-1) K_s) = 4ωs/π+ (2ω^2/π^2) \ln(4s) + 2 \ln |G(1 + iω/π)|^2 + O_A((1+ω)^4 \ln^2 s / s)$, where $G$ is the Barnes $G$-function. This is the $γ< 0$ counterpart of the theorem of Bothner, Deift, Its and Krasovsky. It is proved by a direct IIKS steepest-descent analysis rather than by continuation in the parameter: positivity gives solvability, the confluent-hypergeometric model is derived from its defining Kummer functions, and the endpoint matching is estimated after the Stokes-sector cancellations. The unitary equivalence $S_c \simeq K_{πc/2}$, a Barnes-$G$ lower bound, and a two-way determinant reduction then yield the bridge estimate. We also prove the uniform head quantile formula $N_{1-\varepsilon}(c) = c - π^{-2} \bar{L} \ln(4π^2 c / \bar{L}) + O(\ln c + \bar{L})$ for $6 \le \bar{L} = \ln((1-\varepsilon)/\varepsilon) \le c^{1/3}$, together with its upper-half, tensor-product, and joint-range consequences.

</details>


### [119] [Fredholm $Δ$-Filtration of Fredholm Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15823)
*Ahmad Reza Haj Saaedi Sadegh,Jody Trout*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文证明无限维Fredholm算子群中若干经典滤过实际上是Fredholm Δ-滤过。以可分无限维实Hilbert空间上的一般线性群GLK(E)为例，展示其有限维一般线性群的并集滤过是一个Fredholm Δ-滤过，其维度序列为Δ(n)=n²，突破了经典文献中维度每次仅增加1的刚性约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的Fredholm流形文献中对滤过维度有刚性限制——维度序列每次只能递增1，这排除了许多自然的滤过结构。作者希望放宽这一约束，证明更多自然的滤过（如GL(n)的并集滤过，维度以n²增长）也构成Fredholm滤过，从而拓展Fredholm流形理论的适用范围。

Method: 作者引入Fredholm Δ-滤过的定义（见文中定义5），放宽了经典Fredholm滤过中维度每次只增加1的限制，允许更一般的维度序列。随后通过利用正交基构造GL(n)到GLK(E)的典范包含，分析其维度序列Δ(n)=n²，并验证该滤过满足Fredholm Δ-滤过的条件。

Result: 证明了GL(n)的并集滤过GL(1)⊂⋯⊂GL(n)⊂⋯⊂GLK(E)构成Fredholm流形GLK(E)上的Fredholm Δ-滤过，其维度序列为Δ(n)=dim(GL(n))=n²，该结果在经典Fredholm流形文献中因维度递增限制而未得到讨论。

Conclusion: 通过放宽经典Fredholm滤过的维度约束，可以证明更多与Fredholm算子相关的自然滤过（如GLK(E)上的滤过）是Fredholm Δ-滤过，这为Fredholm流形理论提供了更广泛的框架。

Abstract: In this short note, we show that some well-known filtrations of infinite dimensional groups of Fredholm operators associated to certain perturbation classes are, in fact, Fredholm $Δ$-filtrations (see Definition 5 below.) For example, let $\E$ be a separable infinite dimensional real Hilbert space. The group $\GLK(\E)$ of all invertible operators on $\E$ which are compact perturbations of the identity is the structure group for Hilbert Fredholm manifolds and bundles modeled on $\E$ \cite{ElwTr, Ksch, Mkhr}. Using an orthonormal basis, there are canonical inclusions of general linear groups: $$\GL(1) \subset \cdots \subset \GL(n) \subset \GL(n+1) \subset \cdots \subset \GL(\infty) = \varinjlim \GL(n) \subset \GLK(\E).$$ We show this is a Fredholm $Δ$-filtration of the Fredholm manifold $\GLK(\E)$ with dimension sequence $Δ(n) = \dim(\GL(n)) = n^2$, which was not discussed in the classical Fredholm manifold literature because of the rigid constraint that the dimensions of a filtration increase only by one.

</details>


### [120] [The nonlinear Hausdorff-Young inequality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15895)
*Durvudkhan Suragan*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文证明了常数为1的离散非线性Hausdorff-Young不等式，并通过离散到连续的极限论证，得到了非线性Fourier变换透射系数a_f的对数形式的L^{p'}范数估计，解决了Muscalu-Tao-Thiele均匀性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性Hausdorff-Young不等式是经典Hausdorff-Young不等式在非线性Fourier变换框架下的推广，其最优常数问题（即Muscalu-Tao-Thiele均匀性问题）长期悬而未决。本文旨在给出该问题的最优常数结果。

Method: 作者首先建立了常数为1的离散非线性Hausdorff-Young不等式，然后通过离散到连续的极限论证将其推广到连续情形。证明的关键在于发现了一个隐藏的Hilbert空间结构，该结构将非线性不等式约化为经典的插值问题。

Result: 获得了对于所有1≤p<2，满足 ||(log|a_f|^2)^{1/2}||_{L^{p'}(R)} ≤ ||f||_{L^p(R)} 的估计，其常数为1（最优），从而解决了Muscalu-Tao-Thiele均匀性问题。

Conclusion: 该结果揭示了非线性Fourier变换与经典插值理论之间的深刻联系，通过Hilbert空间结构将非线性问题化归为线性工具可处理的情形，给出了均匀性问题的最优常数。

Abstract: We prove the constant-one discrete nonlinear Hausdorff-Young inequality. As a consequence, by a discrete-to-continuous limiting argument, we obtain $$ \|(\log|a_f|^{2})^{1/2}\|_{L^{p'}(\mathbb{R})}
  \le \|f\|_{L^{p}(\mathbb{R})},\quad 1\le p<2, $$ for all $f\in L^{p}(\mathbb{R})$, where $a_f$ denotes the transmission coefficient of the nonlinear Fourier transform. In particular, this resolves the Muscalu-Tao-Thiele uniformity problem. The proof uncovers a hidden Hilbert-space structure that reduces the nonlinear inequality to classical interpolation.

</details>


### [121] [Unified growth rates for operator semigroups under generalized Kreiss conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16217)
*Filippo Dell'Oro,Alberto Farina,Vittorino Pata*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文为Hilbert空间上满足广义Kreiss预解条件的C₀-半群的算子范数建立了统一的增长率上界，该界限包含并改进了文献中多个已知估计，特别是捕捉了不同超线性增长行为之间的过渡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有文献中对满足广义Kreiss预解条件的C₀-半群算子范数的增长估计是分散且不统一的，不同条件下给出的增长率各不相同，缺乏一个能够涵盖并改进这些结果的统一框架。

Method: 在Hilbert空间框架下，对生成元满足广义Kreiss预解条件的C₀-半群进行算子范数增长率的统一估计，通过数学分析推导出一个包含并改进多个已知结果的统一上界。

Result: 得到了一个统一的增长率界限，该界限不仅包含了文献中已有的多个估计，还在某些情形下对其进行了改进，并准确刻画了不同超线性增长率之间的过渡行为。

Conclusion: 该统一增长率界限为满足广义Kreiss条件的C₀-半群的增长行为提供了更完整和更精确的刻画，统一并改进了以往分散的结果。

Abstract: In this note we establish a unified growth rate for the operator norm of $C_0$-semigroups on Hilbert spaces whose generators satisfy the generalized Kreiss resolvent condition. Our bound contains and improves several known estimates in the literature. In particular, it captures the transition between different super-linear growth behaviors.

</details>


### [122] [On quasipolynomial upper bounds for complex polynomial Bohnenblust--Hille constants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16584)
*Daniel M. Pellegrino,Eduardo V. Teixeira*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文研究了复多项式Bohnenblust-Hille不等式中的最优常数D_m，给出了准多项式上界limsup log D_m/(log m)^2 ≤ 5/[8 log(4/(1+√5))]，将此前已知的exp(O(√(m log m)))界改进为exp(O((log m)^2))；同时通过张量化内函数和熵-径向控制得到下界，证明D_m的下极限>1.27，表明新的准多项式上界与持久非收缩间隙共存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bohnenblust-Hille不等式在多元复分析、调和分析及量子信息理论中具有重要地位，其维数无关常数D_m的最优增长速率是长期悬而未决的核心问题。此前最好上界为exp(O(√(m log m)))，作者希望进一步改善这一指数型增长估计，并同时探明常数是否存在收缩（即趋于1）的下界行为。

Method: 采用固定比例的次数约化（fixed-ratio degree reduction）策略。具体包含三个关键组分：(1) 相位保持分裂（phase-preserving splitting）以精确追踪每个系数的血统；(2) 分数两块估计（fractional two-block estimate）控制两个产生的次数尺度；(3) 在宏观分离的次数之间建立递推关系，迭代后得到二次对数上界。下界部分使用张量化内函数与熵-径向控制技术，并结合一个经过验证的二元有理内种子函数。

Result: 上界方面，证明了limsup_{m→∞} log D_m/(log m)^2 ≤ 5/[8 log(4/(1+√5))]，从而将常数阶界从exp(O(√(m log m)))改进为exp(O((log m)^2))。下界方面，通过张量化内函数和熵-径向控制证明了liminf_{m→∞} D_m > 1.27，表明最优常数不会趋于1。

Conclusion: 本文同时改进了Bohnenblust-Hille常数D_m的上下界：上界实现了从亚指数到准多项式的本质性突破，而下界证明常数存在持久性的非收缩间隙（大于1.27）。这一结果深化了对复多项式Bohnenblust-Hille不等式最优常数的认识，但其精确渐近行为仍然开放。

Abstract: For an m-homogeneous polynomial on C^n, let D_{m,n} denote the optimal constant in the complex polynomial Bohnenblust--Hille inequality, and set D_m = sup_n D_{m,n}. Our principal result gives a quasipolynomial upper bound for the dimension-free constants:
  limsup_{m->infinity} log D_m / (log m)^2 <= 5 / [8 log(4/(1+sqrt(5)))].
  Thus the previously known exp(O(sqrt(m log m))) bound can be replaced by exp(O((log m)^2)). The proof uses a fixed-ratio degree reduction. A phase-preserving splitting retains the exact ancestry of every coefficient, and a fractional two-block estimate controls the two resulting degree scales. This gives a recurrence between macroscopically separated degrees whose iteration produces the quadratic logarithm above.
  We also obtain lower bounds from tensorized inner functions and entropy--radial control. This gives localized estimates and, for a certified bivariate rational-inner seed,
  liminf_{m->infinity} D_m > 1.27.
  Thus the new quasipolynomial upper bound coexists with a persistent noncontractive gap.

</details>
