### [123] [Spectral determinants of flat metrics on the bielliptic genus-two locus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15752)
*Victor Kalvin*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 本文推导了双椭圆亏格二轨迹上平锥度量的谱行列式的闭式显式公式，并利用该公式给出了三类退化情形的完整渐近行为，进而确定了相关常数与绝对归一化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 谱行列式是几何与数学物理中的核心不变量，但在平锥度量（特别是高亏格）上缺乏显式公式。作者希望通过对双椭圆亏格二轨迹这一特殊但非平凡的情形，获得闭式谱行列式，并以此为基础理解退化极限与相关常数的确定。

Method: 利用Klein四元谱恒等式将对弈称参考行列式约化为球面和环面上的标量行列式；通过奇异反常公式从参考情形推广到一般情形。最终的闭式公式由一元形式的系数构成的二元六次式以及商球上四锥度量的两个超几何面积函数给出。对于圆柱形实现，与Bismut-Bost渐近对比来确定常数；在分离情形还与Müller-Müller公式中的相对行列式比较。

Result: 得到了谱行列式的闭式显式公式，并给出了三类退化（分离、单节点非分离、双节点同时退化）的完整渐近。确定了圆柱形实现中的常数，在分离情形求值了Müller-Müller公式中的相对行列式，并修正了早期一般锥形与变分行列式公式的绝对归一化。

Conclusion: 该工作为双椭圆亏格二轨迹上的谱行列式提供了完整闭式解，建立了退化极限与已知渐近公式之间的一致性，并最终固定了此前谱行列式公式的归一化常数。

Abstract: We obtain a closed explicit formula for the spectral determinant of flat conical metrics on the bielliptic genus-two locus. The metrics are generated by holomorphic one-forms with two simple zeros. An exact Klein-four spectral identity reduces a symmetric reference determinant to scalar determinants on spheres and tori; the singular anomaly formula then yields the general case. The resulting formula involves an elementary binary sextic in the coefficients of the one-form and two explicit hypergeometric areas of four-cone metrics on a quotient sphere.
  The formula also gives complete asymptotics for three types of degeneration. In the separating case the surface splits into two flat tori. In the one-node nonseparating case the same nodal curve has two metric limits: a smooth flat torus or a complete flat torus with two cylindrical ends. In the simultaneous two-node case the limit is a complete flat sphere with four cylindrical ends. For the cylindrical realizations, comparison with the Bismut--Bost asymptotics determines the corresponding constants explicitly; in the separating case it also evaluates the relative determinant appearing in the Müller--Müller formula. As a by-product, the separating asymptotic fixes the absolute normalization of earlier general conical and variational determinant formulas.

</details>


<div id='math.DG'></div>

# math.DG [[Back]](#toc)

### [124] [A Sharp Diameter-Dependent Lower Bound for the First Nonzero Neumann Eigenvalue of Geodesic Triangles in Space Forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16136)
*Shoo Seto,Guofang Wei,Yusen Xia*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明二维空间形式中给定直径的测地三角形首个非零Neumann特征值的尖锐下界，该下界由一维模型的径向特征值给出，并在退化等腰三角形时逼近；同时证明非锐球面三角形（直径≤π/2）的热点定理，以及直径=π/2的等腰球面三角形的反对称性与特征值单调性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究测地三角形在给定直径约束下的Neumann特征值下界问题，这一问题是经典Laplace特征值几何分析中的重要课题，涉及三角形几何与谱理论的交叉。

Method: 通过构造一维径向模型来描述特征值的下界，并利用退化等腰三角形的极限情形逼近该下界；对于K>0且D=π/(2√K)的特殊情形，精确刻画达到等号的三角形（双直角三角形）；采用热点定理和反对称性分析研究球面三角形上的Neumann特征函数结构。

Result: 给出了测地三角形首个非零Neumann特征值的尖锐下界；在球面情形下，证明了直径≤π/2的非锐球面三角形满足热点定理，并建立直径=π/2的等腰球面三角形的特征函数反对称性及特征值关于角度的单调性。

Conclusion: 本工作将平面三角形的Neumann特征值下界结果推广到二维空间形式（包括球面和双曲空间），并深入揭示了球面三角形在特定直径条件下的谱几何性质（热点定理、反对称性与单调性）。

Abstract: We prove a sharp lower bound for the first nonzero Neumann eigenvalue of geodesic triangles of given diameter in two-dimensional space forms. The bound is given by the first positive radial Neumann eigenvalue of an one dimensional model; it is approached by degenerating isosceles triangles. When \(K>0\) and \(D=π/(2\sqrt K)\), equality is attained precisely by birectangular triangles. We also prove a hot-spots theorem for non-acute spherical triangles of diameter at most \(π/2\), and establish antisymmetry and eigenvalue monotonicity for isosceles spherical triangles of diameter \(π/2\).

</details>


### [125] [Free boundary minimal annuli in convex balls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14679)
*Dongyeong Ko,Guanhua Shao*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 作者构造了黎曼3-球内一族4参数的、具有至多两个边界分量的、适当嵌入的亏格零曲面族，这是Marques-Neves在证明Willmore猜想时所用3-球面上5参数标准曲面族的自由边界类比。在欧氏单位球中，该族将临界悬链面实现为Simon-Smith min-max极限。作为应用，给出了Almgren-Pitts 4-宽度ω₄(B³)以临界悬链面面积为上界的结果，并证明了在任何具有非负Ricci曲率和严格凸边界的紧致3-流形中，至少存在三个自由边界极小球面环（annuli）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Marques-Neves在Willmore猜想证明中使用的经典5参数曲面族构造推广到自由边界情形，以研究自由边界极小球面和Almgren-Pitts宽度理论。

Method: 构造一个4参数的自由边界亏格零曲面族（至多两个边界分量），作为经典构造的自由边界类比；通过Simon-Smith min-max理论将临界悬链面实现为该族的极限；利用Almgren-Pitts变分理论估计4-宽度上界。

Result: 得到了Almgren-Pitts 4-宽度ω₄(B³)以临界悬链面面积为上界；证明了在非负Ricci曲率且严格凸边界的任意紧致3-流形中，自由边界极小球面环至少存在三个。

Conclusion: 该4参数构造提供了自由边界极小球面与Willmore猜想证明技术之间的桥梁，并在宽度估计和自由边界极小球面环多重性方面取得了实质性结果。

Abstract: We construct a $4$-parameter family of properly embedded genus-zero surfaces with at most two boundary components in a Riemannian $3$-ball. The construction is a free boundary analog of the canonical $5$-parameter family of surfaces on the $3$-sphere by Marques-Neves in the proof of Willmore conjecture. In the Euclidean unit ball, this family realizes the critical catenoid as a Simon-Smith min-max limit. As applications, we give an upper bound of Almgren-Pitts $4$-width $ω_4(\mathbb{B}^3)$ by the area of the critical catenoid. Moreover, we prove the existence of at least three free boundary minimal annuli in any compact $3$-manifold with nonnegative Ricci curvature and strictly convex boundary.

</details>


### [126] [Introduction to Differential Geometry in the Three-Dimensional Field of Complex Vectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14686)
*Branko Sarić*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文基于二维和三维复向量场的交换几何代数，发展了一套用于紧凑Hermitian流形微分几何的向量框架，完全以向量形式描述流形及其张量关系，摆脱了经典标量坐标方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统复流形微分几何依赖于标量坐标方法，缺乏统一的代数基础。作者旨在利用交换几何代数构建一个内在的向量框架，使紧凑Hermitian流形的内禀与外禀几何都能以向量形式直接表达。

Method: 从复向量的交换几何积出发，构造二维和三维统一的可交换代数体系，并由此推导相应的向量微分与积分恒等式。在此代数基础上，独立于标量坐标方法，用纯向量形式描述紧凑Hermitian流形，并推导控制其内禀与外禀几何的基本张量关系。

Result: 成功建立了由复向量交换代数直接驱动的统一代数-几何框架，使得复流形的微分几何可以完全在向量形式下推导和表述。

Conclusion: 该工作为复流形微分几何提供了独立于标量坐标的统一向量化代数与几何基础，拓展了交换几何代数在微分几何中的应用。

Abstract: Building on a commutative geometric algebra developed for 2D and 3D fields of complex vectors, this paper develops a vector framework for the differential geometry of compact Hermitian manifolds embedded in an ambient three-dimensional field of complex vectors. The underlying algebraic framework is constructed from the commutative geometric product of complex vectors, yielding a unified commutative algebra in both the 2D and 3D settings together with the corresponding vector differential and integral identities. These algebraic results provide the intrinsic foundation for a differential geometric formulation that is developed independently of the classical scalar coordinate approach. Within this framework, compact Hermitian manifolds are described entirely in vector form. Fundamental tensor relations governing the intrinsic and extrinsic geometry of complex manifolds are derived in vector form. The resulting framework establishes a unified algebraic and geometric setting in which the differential geometry of complex manifolds is developed directly from the commutative algebra of complex vectors.

</details>


### [127] [The Existence of Non-Equivariant Gromov Tori](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14688)
*Grigoriy Yakovlev*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文回答了一个微分几何问题：平环面等距且极小嵌入到球面中时，其平移群是否必然扩展为球面的等距群。对于n≥3的嵌入情形，作者给出了否定答案，通过构造非等变（non-equivariant）的嵌入Gromov环面实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该问题由Robert Bryant对n=2给出肯定答案；Ying Lu、Peng Wang和Zhenxiao Xie虽然对n≥3的浸入情形给出否定答案，但嵌入情形仍悬而未决。此外该问题与Gromov和Petrunin关于最优曲率界的工作密切相关，构造达到最优界的"Gromov环面"成为了解决该问题的关键途径。

Method: 作者首先证明任何Gromov环面是内在平坦的、位于球面内且在球面中极小；其次给出定义这些环面的充要条件；最后构造n≥3维中的一个非等变的嵌入Gromov环面，从而回答原问题。

Result: 对于维度n≥3，构造出非等变的嵌入Gromov环面，给出了上述问题的确定性否定答案。

Conclusion: 平环面等距极小嵌入球面时，其平移群未必能扩展为球面等距群，尤其是在高维（n≥3）嵌入情形下存在反例，即非等变嵌入Gromov环面。

Abstract: In this paper, we address the following question: if a flat torus $\mathbb{T}^n$ is isometrically and minimally embedded into a sphere $\mathbb{S}^N$, must its translation group extend to the isometry group of the ambient sphere?
  As shown by Robert Bryant, for $n=2$ the answer is positive. Furthermore, while Ying Lu, Peng Wang, and Zhenxiao Xie recently demonstrated that the answer is negative for immersions when $n \geq 3$, the question for embeddings remained open.
  This problem is deeply tied to the work of Mikhail Gromov and Anton Petrunin concerning optimal curvature bounds. Petrunin proved that any immersion of a torus into a unit ball must have a maximum normal curvature of at least $\sqrt{\frac{3n}{n+2}}$. This bound is attained, for example, by families of tori constructed by Gromov. We call the tori that attain this optimal bound "Gromov tori".
  In this work, we first demonstrate that any Gromov torus is intrinsically flat, lies within a sphere, and is minimal inside it. We then establish the necessary and sufficient conditions for defining these tori. Finally, we present our main result: for dimensions $n \ge 3$, there exists a non-equivariant embedded Gromov torus, which provides a definitive negative answer to the question above.

</details>


### [128] [Generalized Sasaki-Einstein metric twisted with Weil-Petersson metric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14831)
*Hassan Jolany*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文探讨了Sasakian簇上典范度量的存在性问题。该问题是一个长期未解的猜想，其主要难点在于基本第一Chern类不定的情形，而这恰好涵盖大多数Sasakian簇。作者提出利用Fujino的极小模型纲领（Minimal Model Program, MMP）来找寻Sasakian簇典范模型上的典范度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sasakian簇上典范度量的存在性是一个长期悬而未决的猜想。已知结论主要适用于基本第一Chern类确定（正或负）的情形，但大多数Sasakian簇的基本第一Chern类并不确定，这使得该猜想的证明极其困难。因此，需要一种新的方法来处理这类一般情形。

Method: 作者提出一个程序，利用Fujino发展的极小模型纲领（MMP）来处理Sasakian簇。具体思路是：通过对Sasakian簇运行MMP，将其约化到典范模型（canonical model）上，进而在该典范模型上寻找典范度量。

Result: 文章给出了一个系统的程序/方案（而非完整的最终定理），用于在Sasakian簇的典范模型上构造典范度量，从而为证明Sasakian簇上典范度量存在性猜想提供了可行的路径。

Conclusion: 通过结合Fujino的MMP理论，作者为解决Sasakian簇上典范度量存在性的长期猜想提出了一条新的研究方案，尤其针对基本第一Chern类不定的情形——这是该猜想中最困难也是最具普遍性的部分。

Abstract: Existence of canonical metric on a Sasakian variety was a long standing conjecture and the major part of this conjecture is about varieties which do not have definite basic first Chern class . Most of the Sasakian varieties do not have definite basic first Chern class. We give a program by using Fujino's Minimal Model program to find canonical metric on the canonical model of Sasakian varieties.

</details>


### [129] [The canonical structures of the limit of the Yang-Mills flows for nef and big classes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15007)
*Satoshi Jinnouchi*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文延续前期工作，研究了紧致Kähler流形上nef且big类α上全纯向量丛的Yang-Mills流的可解性与收敛性，证明了T-Yang-Mills流对所有时间可解，并收敛到T-Yang-Mills连接（Uhlenbeck意义下），且极限连接在ample locus上复规范等价于与α^(n-1)-Harder-Narasimhan-Seshadri滤过各因子上的T-自适应Hermitian-Einstein度量对应的Chern连接之直和。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前作者已在nef和big类α上引入了自适应流（adapted current）和自适应Hermitian-Einstein度量，建立了相应的Kobayashi-Hitchin对应。本文的目的是将该框架推广到Yang-Mills流，研究其在nef和big类情形下的长期可解性和收敛行为，从而刻画极限连接与全纯结构和共轭类之间的关系。

Method: 利用前期引入的自适应流形T和T-自适应Hermitian-Einstein度量的工具，构造T-Yang-Mills流，并分析其长时间存在性。通过Uhlenbeck极限理论建立收敛性，并结合α^(n-1)-Harder-Narasimhan-Seshadri滤过与自适应Hermitian-Einstein度量，在ample locus上对极限连接进行复规范分类。

Result: 证明了T-Yang-Mills流以任意可积酉连接A_0为初始条件时对所有时间可解，并在Uhlenbeck意义下收敛到T-Yang-Mills连接A_∞。此外，证明了在α的ample locus上，A_∞复规范等价于由α^(n-1)-Harder-Narasimhan-Seshadri滤过的各因子上的T-自适应Hermitian-Einstein度量的Chern连接构成的直和。

Conclusion: 该结果将Yang-Mills流的收敛性问题从不退化情形推广到nef和big类的一般情形，建立了流的极限与全纯向量丛的Harder-Narasimhan-Seshadri滤过之间的精确对应，进一步深化了Kobayashi-Hitchin对应在退化类上的理解。

Abstract: In the previous paper \cite{Jin26}, the author introduced the notions of an adapted current $T$ and an adapted Hermitian-Einstein metric to establish the Kobayashi-Hitchin correspondence for a nef and big class $α$. As a continuation of the previous work, this paper studies the solvability and the convergence of the Yang-Mills flow for a nef and big class $α$ on a holomorphic vector bundle $E$ over a compact Kähler manifold $X$. In particular, we show that the limit of the Yang-Mills flow at infinity is determined by the holomorphic structure of $E$ and the nef and big class $α$. More precisely, if we fix an integrable unitary connection $A_0$ on $E$, we show that the $T$-Yang-Mills flow on $E$ with initial condition $A_0$ is solvable for all time and it converges to a $T$-Yang-Mills connection $A_{\infty}$ in the sense of Uhlenbeck limit. Furthermore, we also show that, on the ample locus of $α$, $A_{\infty}$ is complex-gauge equivalent to the direct sum of the Chern connections of the $T$-adapted Hermitian-Einstein metrics on the factors of the graded sheaf associated with the $α^{n-1}$-Harder-Narasimhan-Seshadri filtration of $E$.

</details>


### [130] [A $2$-systolic inequality for $\mathbb S^2\times P$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15091)
*Jinmin Wang,Zhizhang Xie*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了四维乘积流形 S²×P（其中 P 为任意凸多边形）上的锐利 2-收缩不等式。若该流形上的黎曼度量 g 具有正标量曲率 σ>0，在每一余维一面上具有非负平均曲率，且二面角不超过标准乘积度量的对应二面角，则其同伦与同调 2-收缩均不超过 8π/σ，从而证实了 Gromov 的一个猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究的核心动机是验证 Gromov 关于正标量曲率与收缩不等式关系的猜想。通过建立锐利的 2-收缩不等式，可以深入理解高维流形在正标量曲率约束下的几何与拓扑结构限制，这是几何分析与标量曲率几何中的重要问题。

Method: 研究对象为 S²×P 上的标准乘积度量 h，构造了具有标量曲率下界、非负平均曲率及二面角约束的黎曼度量。通过分析同伦与同调 2-收缩的上界，利用标量曲率正性与边界几何条件的约束，推导出收缩不等式。

Result: 成功证明了对任意凸多边形 P，满足条件的度量 g 的同伦和同调 2-收缩均不超过 8π/σ，且该界是锐利的（sharp），即不可改进。

Conclusion: 该结果证实了 Gromov 关于四维乘积流形上 2-收缩不等式的猜想，为标量曲率正性下的几何刚性提供了新的证据，并将结果推广到了任意凸多边形底面的一般情形。

Abstract: We prove a sharp $2$-systolic inequality for four-dimensional products $\mathbb S^2\times P$, where $P\subset\mathbb R^2$ is an arbitrary convex polygon. Let $h=g_{\mathbb S^2}+g_{\mathrm{eu}}$ be the standard product metric. If a Riemannian metric $g$ on $\mathbb S^2\times P$ has scalar curvature $\geq σ>0$, nonnegative mean curvature on every codimension one face, and dihedral angles no larger than the corresponding dihedral angles of $h$, then both its homotopy and homology $2$-systoles are at most $ \frac{8π}σ.$ This confirms a conjecture of Gromov.

</details>


### [131] [A Unified Construction of Exceptional Symmetric Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15186)
*Yuuki Sasaki*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一构造方法，将所有单连通不可约例外紧对称空间作为E₈的全测地子流形实现，并由此确定了例外对称空间之间的全测地嵌入关系及其包含结构，发现该结构具有显著的对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 例外紧对称空间的几何结构复杂且彼此之间关系尚未被系统理解。作者希望找到一个统一的框架（以E₈为母空间）来揭示这些空间之间的内在联系和包含关系。

Method: 将所有单连通不可约例外紧对称空间统一构造为例外李群E₈的全测地子流形，并在此框架下通过确定所有包含关系来系统描述例外对称空间之间的全测地嵌入。

Result: 成功获得了例外紧对称空间之间全部的全测地嵌入关系，并揭示出所得到的包含结构具有显著且出人意料的对称性，暗示了例外对称空间之间更深层的几何联系。

Conclusion: 例外紧对称空间可以被统一地嵌入到E₈中，其间的包含结构展现出新的对称性，这可能为理解例外对称空间之间的深层几何关系提供新的视角。

Abstract: We present a unified construction of all simply connected irreducible exceptional compact symmetric spaces as totally geodesic submanifolds of $E_{8}$. Using this construction, we obtain a unified description of totally geodesic embeddings between exceptional symmetric spaces by determining all inclusion relations among them. The resulting inclusion structure exhibits a remarkable and unexpected symmetry, suggesting a deeper geometric relationship among exceptional symmetric spaces.

</details>


### [132] [Geometric turbulence: a geodesic-regression crisis indicator for equity covariance dynamics, with evidence from African markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15205)
*E. B. Camara,Y. U. Gaba,I. G. M. Nsiloulou*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文提出在SPD流形上使用测地线回归（log-Euclidean和仿射不变度量）对资产组合滚动协方差矩阵进行建模，并将拟合测地线的速度范数作为基于几何的市场压力指标。在四类股票篮子数据上，该指标无需校准即可在2008金融危机、2020疫情、2022加息周期和2019埃及政治经济危机等压力事件中清晰达到峰值。log-Euclidean回归在崩溃窗口的测地线均方误差比欧氏OLS小150-300倍，一个朴素的基于速度的去风险策略以1.4个百分点年化收益为代价将最大回撤降低2.5个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协方差矩阵是正定对称的，在弯曲流形上演化而非平坦向量空间。标准的线性回归直接对向量化条目进行操作，忽略了这一几何结构，在市场压力时期可能产生非正定的拟合结果，导致预测失真。

Method: （1）在SPD(n)锥上，分别在log-Euclidean和仿射不变度量下研究测地线回归，数据为篮子对数收益的滚动样本协方差；（2）将拟合测地线的速度范数（velocity norm）作为几何感知的市场动荡指标；（3）在四类股票篮子上评估指标性能；（4）构造一个简单的基于速度的去风险交易策略验证实用价值。

Result: （1）压力指标在无需校准的情况下，在2008金融危机、2020 COVID崩盘、2022美联储加息周期、2019年10-11月埃及政治经济紧张等已知压力事件上干净地达到峰值；（2）崩溃窗口中，log-Euclidean回归的测地线均方误差比欧氏OLS小150-300倍，因为OLS产生非SPD预测在固有度量下会爆炸；（3）速度感知去风险策略将最大回撤降低2.5个百分点，代价是年化收益减少1.4个百分点。

Conclusion: 测地线回归作为一种几何正确的方法，在协方差矩阵建模上显著优于忽略流形结构的欧氏回归，尤其在市场压力期。速度范数指标虽然能可靠地发出压力信号，但在转化为战术交易策略时效果有限，说明该指标适合作为慢速监测信号而非高频交易触发信号。

Abstract: The covariance matrix of a basket of assets is a symmetric positive-definite object that evolves on a curved manifold, not on a flat vector space. Standard linear regression on its vectorised entries ignores that geometry and, in periods of market stress, can return fits that fail to be positive definite. We study geodesic regression on the symmetric positive-definite cone $\SPD(n)$ under the log-Euclidean and affine-invariant metrics, with the rolling sample covariance of a basket of log-returns as data. The fitted geodesic's velocity norm is proposed as a geometry-aware turbulence indicator. On daily data for four equity baskets- a ten-stock historical S\&P sub-basket (2006-2024), a current S\&P~100 mega-cap basket, the top ten listings of the Johannesburg Stock Exchange (JSE), and the ten most-traded Egyptian Exchange (EGX) tickers the indicator peaks cleanly on known stress episodes (the 2008 global financial crisis, the 2020 COVID crash, the 2022 Fed rate-hike cycle, and the October-November 2019 Egyptian political-economic tension episode) without any calibration. On hold-out evaluation in crash windows, log-Euclidean regression achieves 150 to 300 times smaller geodesic mean squared error than Euclidean ordinary least squares, because the latter produces non-SPD forecasts that blow up under the intrinsic metric. A naive velocity-aware de-risking strategy reduces maximum drawdown by 2.5 percentage points at the cost of 1.4 percentage points of annual return, consistent with a slow-moving indicator that signals stress reliably but translates imperfectly into tactical trading. All data, code, and experiments are reproducible from the companion repository.

</details>


### [133] [Generalized Hitchin-Kobayashi correspondence and Weil-Petersson current](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15206)
*Hassan Jolany*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文沿全纯纤维空间推广了Hitchin-Kobayashi对应，证明了存在一个与Weil-Petersson当前扭转的广义Hermitian-Einstein度量，并将Song-Tian纲领推广到向量丛情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典的Hitchin-Kobayashi对应从单个流形推广到全纯纤维空间的框架下，并引入Weil-Petersson当前的扭转，以处理纤维化几何中的规范度量问题。同时推广Song-Tian纲领，使其适用于向量丛而非仅标量曲率流形情形。

Method: 通过沿全纯纤维空间构造广义Hermitian-Einstein度量，该度量与Weil-Petersson当前进行扭转，利用Hitchin-Kobayashi对应的扩展框架建立存在性。

Result: 证明了在全纯纤维空间上存在与Weil-Petersson当前扭转的广义Hermitian-Einstein度量，从而将Song-Tian纲领推广至向量丛。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了全纯纤维空间框架下Hitchin-Kobayashi对应的推广，为纤维化几何中的Hermitian-Einstein度量与Weil-Petersson几何的交互提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: In this paper we extend Hitchin-Kobayashi correspondence along holomorphic fibre space and we show that there exists a generalized Hermitian-Einstein metric which is twisted with Weil-Petersson current.In fact we generalise Song-Tian program for vector bundles

</details>


### [134] [Rigidity and non-rigidity of the stable norm on $T^n$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15376)
*Fernando C. Marques,André Neves,Ao Sun*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究了平坦度量在环面同调群上的稳定范数的局部刚性。证明了当$1\leq d<n-1$时，$H_d(T^n,\mathbb{R})$上的稳定范数是局部刚性的；当$d=n-1$时，在等体积度量类中是局部刚性的；但$H_2(T^3,\mathbb{R})$上的稳定范数不是局部刚性的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稳定范数是黎曼流形中一个重要的不变量，它与闭测地线、极小曲面和动力学系统密切相关。研究其刚性（即能否由度量唯一确定）对于理解流形的几何结构具有重要意义。作者旨在解决Bangert在其ICM演讲中提出的开放问题。

Method: 作者通过对平坦度量在环面同调群$H_d(T^n,\mathbb{R})$上的稳定范数进行分析，分别针对不同维数条件$d$建立局部刚性或非刚性的判定。利用稳定范数的几何性质与体积约束条件，构造相关证明。

Result: 得到了以下结果：(1) 当$1\leq d<n-1$时，稳定范数是局部刚性的；(2) 当$d=n-1$时，稳定范数在等体积度量中是局部刚性的；(3) $H_2(T^3,\mathbb{R})$上的稳定范数不是局部刚性的。由此否定地回答了Bangert的问题，证明了对$1\leq k\leq n-2$的标记$k$-面积谱的局部刚性，以及对$T^n$上平坦度量体积谱的局部刚性结果。

Conclusion: 本文完整刻画了环面平坦度量稳定范数的局部刚性行为（除$d=n-1$维数区域的等体积条件外），并以此为工具解决了Bangert提出的开放问题，同时推广了面积谱和体积谱的局部刚性结果。

Abstract: We show that the stable norm of flat metrics on $H_{d}(T^n,\mathbb{R})$ is locally rigid if $1\leq d<n-1$ and locally rigid among metrics of the same volume if $d=n-1$. We also show that the stable norm on $H_{2}(T^3,\mathbb{R})$ is not locally rigid.
  As applications, we answer negatively a question raised by Bangert in his ICM address, prove local rigidity of the marked $k$-area spectrum of flat metrics for $1\leq k\leq n-2$, and prove a local rigidity result for the volume spectrum of flat metrics on $T^n$.

</details>


### [135] [Delaunay surfaces with free boundary on the unit $2$-sphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15387)
*Eric Lang,Christian Scharrer*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文完整刻画了与单位球正交相交的、具有常平均曲率的对称旋转曲面。已知单位球内存在唯一的自由边界悬链面（catenoid）；本文类比证明了单位球内存在唯一的常平均曲率为-1的自由边界nodoid，且球外也存在唯一的紧致自由边界nodoid（平均曲率同样为-1）。然而，不存在任何常平均曲率为1且与单位球正交相交的对称旋转曲面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在几何分析中，自由边界问题（free boundary problem）是重要研究方向，其目标是刻画在给定边界上以正交方式相交的常平均曲率曲面。已知自由边界悬链面的唯一性结果，自然延伸的问题是：在其他平均曲率水平（如-1和1）下，是否存在类似或不同的唯一性现象。这有助于完善对对称旋转曲面自由边界问题的分类理解。

Method: 通过对对称旋转曲面施加正交边界条件，将其转化为关于轮廓曲线的常微分方程（平均曲率方程），并结合旋转曲面的分类理论（悬链面、nodoid等），逐一分析不同常平均曲率取值下解的存在唯一性。利用几何与分析相结合的方法，分别考察球内与球外的情形。

Result: (1) 单位球内存在唯一的自由边界悬链面（已知结果）；(2) 单位球内存在唯一的常平均曲率为-1的自由边界nodoid；(3) 单位球外存在唯一的紧致、常平均曲率为-1的自由边界nodoid；(4) 不存在常平均曲率为1且与单位球正交相交的对称旋转曲面。

Conclusion: 论文对自由边界旋转曲面的存在性与唯一性给出了完整的刻画：平均曲率为-1的情形在球内外均存在唯一nodoid解，且与悬链面情形类似表现出唯一性；但平均曲率为1时解不存在，说明符号对解的存在性起决定性作用。该结果为自由边界问题中曲面分类提供了新的完整图景。

Abstract: We characterize all symmetric, embedded surfaces of revolution with constant mean curvature that meet the unit sphere orthogonally. It is well known that there exists a unique free boundary catenoid inside of the unit ball. By analogy, we prove the existence of a unique free boundary nodoid inside of the unit ball with constant mean curvature equal to $-1$. Moreover, there exists a unique compact free boundary nodoid \emph{outside} of the unit ball with constant mean curvature equal to $-1$. However, there exists no symmetric surface of revolution with constant mean curvature $1$ that meets the unit sphere orthogonally.

</details>


### [136] [Prüfer $2$-group and Milnor's Conjecture on Fundamental Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15505)
*Nan Wu,Zetian Yan*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文在四维和五维中构造了具有严格正Ricci曲率的完备单端黎曼流形，其基本群同构于Prüfer 2-群C_{2^∞}，从而在剩余两个维数中给出了Milnor猜想的反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Milnor猜想关注正Ricci曲率流形上基本群的有限生成性。此前已在前几个维数中给出反例，但四维和五维是剩余尚未解决的维数，本文旨在填补这一空白，构造出这两个维度中满足正Ricci曲率但基本群非有限生成的反例。

Method: 作者构造了维数为四和五的完备、单端的黎曼流形，通过精心的几何构造赋予其严格正Ricci曲率，并使其基本群同构于Prüfer 2-群C_{2^∞}（这是一个非有限生成的挠群），从而直接与Milnor猜想产生矛盾。

Result: 成功地在四维和五维中构造出满足条件的流形，给出了Milnor猜想的反例，完成了所有剩余维数的反例构造。

Conclusion: 本文证明了Milnor猜想在四维和五维中也不成立，从而使该猜想在所有相关维数上都被推翻，进一步深化了对正Ricci曲率流形基本群结构的理解。

Abstract: We construct complete, one-ended Riemannian manifolds in dimensions four and five having strictly positive Ricci curvature, with fundamental group isomorphic to the Prüfer $2$-group $C_{2^\infty}$. This gives counterexamples to Milnor's conjecture in the two remaining dimensions.

</details>


### [137] [Metric Degenerations and Satake Compactification of Enriques Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15508)
*Zexuan Ouyang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文确定了Enriques曲面上单位直径Ricci-flat Kähler度量的模空间其Gromov-Hausdorff紧化，证明所有度量退化由Enriques周期域的伴随Satake紧化所编码，并分类了全部GH极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Enriques曲面上Ricci-flat Kähler度量在退化时可能的GH极限，是Calabi-Yau度量紧致性问题的重要环节。此前对K3曲面有类似结果，但Enriques曲面因周期域结构不同需独立处理。

Method: 利用Enriques周期域的伴随Satake紧化，其边界包含32个分量；通过建立每个Satake边界分量与GH极限之间的对应关系，将度量退化完全编码于周期域的紧化结构中。

Result: 构造了Gromov-Hausdorff紧化，证明了32个Satake紧化边界分量各自对应的GH极限，从而完整分类了所有GH极限，包括固定复结构或固定极化的情形。

Conclusion: Enriques曲面的Ricci-flat度量退化完全由周期域的Satake紧化控制，GH紧化与Satake紧化之间具有明确的一一对应关系，给出了此类曲面度量退化问题的完整分类。

Abstract: We determine the Gromov--Hausdorff compactification of the moduli space of unit-diameter Ricci-flat Kähler metrics on Enriques surfaces. We show that all metric degenerations are encoded by the adjoint Satake compactification of the Enriques period domain. There are 32 boundary components added in this Satake compactification, and the GH limit at each boundary is determined. As a corollary, all the GH limits of Kähler Ricci-flat metrics on Enriques surfaces are classified, including those for a fixed complex structure or a fixed polarization.

</details>


### [138] [Casorati inequalities along vertical and horizontal distributions of pointwise slant riemannian submersions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15514)
*Tanveer Fatima,Sana Abdulkarem Alharbi,Sharief Deshmukh*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文建立了点式斜向黎曼浸没的最优Casorati不等式，分别针对垂直分布（纤维切方向）和水平分布（受O'Neill张量T和A控制），在广义复空间形式和广义Sasakian空间形式上，用各自的归一化Casorati曲率对归一化数量曲率进行界定。特殊情形可恢复实、复、Kähler、Sasakian、Kenmotsu、余辛及近空间形式上的尖锐不等式，并在垂直情形通过不变拟脐纤维、水平情形通过可积性刻画等号成立条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Casorati不等式是微分几何中重要的曲率约束工具，此前研究多集中于浸入子流形，而黎曼浸没（submersion）情形尚未得到系统处理。由于浸没同时携带垂直与水平两个分布，且水平分布受O'Neill张量调控，需要一个统一的框架来建立两个分布各自的最优曲率不等式，并推广到广义空间形式等更一般的几何背景中。

Method: 作者分别处理垂直分布与水平分布上的Casorati不等式。利用O'Neill张量T和A描述浸没的几何结构，在广义复杂空间形式和广义Sasakian空间形式上，通过归一化Casorati曲率来约束归一化数量曲率。通过选取适当的参数退化情形（实、复、Kähler、Sasakian、Kenmotsu、余辛等），恢复已知的尖锐不等式，并利用不变拟脐性和可积性刻画等号成立的几何条件。

Result: 得到了点式斜向黎曼浸没在两个分布上的最优Casorati不等式；等号条件在垂直情形由不变拟脐纤维刻画，在水平情形由可积性刻画；不变与反不变极限情形恢复已知结果；示例展示了不等式的尖锐性。

Conclusion: 该工作系统性地将Casorati不等式理论从子流形推广到黎曼浸没框架，覆盖了广泛的广义空间形式，并为等号情形的几何解释提供了清晰刻画，同时统一并推广了此前若干分散的结果。

Abstract: We establish optimal Casorati inequalities for pointwise slant Riemannian submersions. As such a submersion carries two distinct distributions, the vertical, tangent to the fibers, and the horizontal, governed by O'Neill's tensors T and A, we treat them separately. For submersions from generalized complex and generalized Sasakian space forms we bound the normalized scalar curvature of each distribution by its normalized Casorati curvatures. Special cases recover sharp inequalities for real, complex, Kähler, Sasakian, Kenmotsu, cosymplectic, and almost and almost space forms. Equality is characterized geometrically: invariantly quasi-umbilical fibers in the vertical case and integrability in the horizontal case. The invariant and anti-invariant limits reproduce known results, and examples illustrate sharpness.

</details>


### [139] [Deformation of scalar generalized bi-Hamiltonian structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15538)
*Zhe Wang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有半单前导项的标量广义双哈密顿结构的分类问题，并以此证明了对于任意给定的粘性中心不变量，都存在相应的可积粘性守恒律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义双哈密顿结构在可积系统理论中具有重要地位，而粘性守恒律与粘性中心不变量的关系是理解可积粘性系统的关键。此前关于此类结构的分类和存在性问题尚不完整，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 通过对具有半单前导项的标量广义双哈密顿结构进行系统分类，利用分类结果构造对应的可积粘性守恒律，从而验证任意给定粘性中心不变量的可实现性。

Result: 成功完成了标量广义双哈密顿结构（半单前导项）的分类，并证明了任意给定的粘性中心不变量均可实现为某个可积粘性守恒律的中心不变量。

Conclusion: 本文建立的分类定理和存在性结果，为可积粘性系统理论提供了坚实的数学基础，表明粘性中心不变量与可积粘性守恒律之间存在完全对应关系。

Abstract: We study the classification of scalar generalized bi-Hamiltonian structures with semisimple leading terms. As an application, we prove the existence of integrable viscous conservation laws for any given viscous central invariant.

</details>


### [140] [Metric Completion and Boundary Geometry of Multi-Weighted Conformal Metrics on Manifolds with Corners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15540)
*Muhamad Fahmi bin Zanal Abidin*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究紧致带角流形内部共形奇异黎曼度量的度量完备化。对于乘积度量g_Π和求和度量g_Σ，分别以A(p)<2和M(p)<2刻画边界点处在有限度量距离的条件，给出完备化的拓扑描述，并推导出边界诱导几何为带有特定指数的雪崩度量及其Hausdorff维数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解共形奇异黎曼度量在边界处的度量完备化结构，特别是在紧致带角流形上，不同权重组合方式（乘积vs求和）对边界可达性、完备化拓扑及诱导几何的深远影响。

Method: 通过引入边界定义函数ρ_i与非负权重α_i，定义两种度量模型（乘积度量g_Π与求和度量g_Σ）。对边界点p定义活跃指标集I(p)、总权重A(p)和最大权重M(p)，利用这些量刻画有限度量距离的条件，并通过活跃法向方向的坍缩来分析完备化的拓扑结构及诱导边界几何。

Result: 乘积度量下有限距离条件为A(p)<2，求和度量下为M(p)<2。在可达边界点上，活跃法向方向坍缩为单一完备化点。乘积权重可使更深的面不可达即使其组成超曲面单独可达，而求和模型由最大活跃权重主导。边界诱导度量分别为指数1-A_I/2和1-M_I/2的雪崩度量，对应Hausdorff维数m/(1-A_I/2)和m/(1-M_I/2)。

Conclusion: 乘积与求和两种共形奇异度量模型在完备化结构上存在本质区别：乘积模型受总权重控制，求和模型受最大权重控制。两者均产生雪崩型边界度量，但临界指数不同，从而导致不同的可达性行为和边界几何维度。

Abstract: We study metric completions of conformally singular Riemannian metrics on the interior of a compact manifold with corners. Given boundary-defining functions \(ρ_1,\ldots,ρ_N\) and nonnegative weights \(α_1,\ldots,α_N\), we compare the product metric \(g_Π=(\prod_iρ_i^{-α_i})g_0\) with the sum metric \(g_Σ=(\sum_iρ_i^{-α_i})g_0\). For a boundary point \(p\), let \(I(p)\) be its active index set, \(A(p)=\sum_{i\in I(p)}α_i\), and \(M(p)=\max_{i\in I(p)}α_i\). We prove that \(p\) lies at finite metric distance precisely when \(A(p)<2\) for the product metric and \(M(p)<2\) for the sum metric. Over every accessible boundary point, the active normal directions collapse to a single completion point, yielding a canonical topological description of the completion. The interaction laws produce different boundary-incidence behavior: product weights can make a deeper face inaccessible even when its constituent hypersurfaces are individually accessible, whereas the sum model is governed by the largest active weight. We also determine the induced geometry on accessible open faces: locally the boundary metric is a snowflake with exponent \(1-A_I/2\) in the product case and \(1-M_I/2\) in the sum case, giving Hausdorff dimensions \(m/(1-A_I/2)\) and \(m/(1-M_I/2)\), respectively.

</details>


### [141] [The Holonomy of Optimal Mass Transport: The Smooth Case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15585)
*Mahmoud Abdelgalil,Tryphon T. Georgiou*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明在光滑无边界黎曼流形上，任何向量场可表示为至多max{6,6n-3}个梯度向量场对的深度一李括号的线性组合，并利用强Trotter性质进一步证明在紧致连通流形上，由最优传输同胚映射生成的群在微分同胚群的单位连通分支中稠密。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索最优传输映射（Monge-Ampère型）与微分同胚群之间的深层联系，揭示由梯度流形生成的李括号结构的表达能力，并为理解最优传输映射在微分同胚群中的生成能力提供理论基础。

Method: 首先构造性地证明任何光滑无边界黎曼流形上的向量场可由有限个（至多max{6,6n-3}个）梯度向量场深度一李括号的线性组合表示；然后利用强Trotter性质（Trotter乘积公式的强收敛版本），将这一代数生成结果推广为紧致连通流形上由最优传输同胚映射（diffeomorphic optimal transport maps）生成的群在Diff_0(M)（微分同胚群单位连通分支）中稠密。

Result: 得到了两个关键结果：(1) 在光滑无边界n维黎曼流形上，任意向量场可分解为至多max{6,6n-3}个梯度场深度一李括号的线性组合；(2) 在紧致连通黎曼流形上，最优传输映射生成的群在恒等分量微分同胚群中稠密。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了梯度向量场李括号与最优传输映射到微分同胚群密度之间的桥梁，结果表明最优传输映射在某种意义上"生成"了整个微分同胚群的单位连通分量，这对理解最优传输理论在几何与分析中的应用具有重要意义。

Abstract: We prove that, on a smooth $n$-dimensional Riemannian manifold without boundary, any vector field can be written as a linear combination of, at most, $\max\{6,6n-3\}$ depth one Lie brackets of pairs of gradient vector fields. Utilizing this along with the strong Trotter property, we show that, if the manifold is also compact and connected, the group generated by diffeomorphic optimal transport maps is dense in the identity component of the diffeomorphism group.

</details>


### [142] [Shape Theory $\&$ TDA via the Atiyah--Molino Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15696)
*N. C. Combe,H. K. Nencka*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Vaisman Atiyah–Molino框架的新方法，利用Vaisman质心作为内在不变量来解决重构问题，摒弃了传统持久同调方法，为拓扑数据分析提供了统一的几何范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重构问题（从残缺、碎片化或失真的数据中恢复隐藏结构）是数学和科学的核心难题。传统方法依赖持久同调，但存在固有歧义性，需要新的统一几何视角来解决逆问题的模糊性。

Method: 本文利用Vaisman Atiyah–Molino框架中的Vaisman质心概念，这是一种封装数据集平均几何的固有不变性。与基于持久同调的传统方法不同，该方法采用统一的几何范式来解析逆问题的内在歧义。后续论文将通过Atiyah–Molino框架扩展为完整的重构方案，形成分两篇论文展开的完整研究计划。

Result: 该框架提供了鲁棒且计算上可处理的隐藏结构恢复方法，为高维和噪声数据的分析开辟了新的途径。

Conclusion: 本文聚焦Vaisman质心的理论与应用，为拓扑数据分析提供了避开持久同调的新视角，并计划在后续工作中通过Atiyah–Molino框架发展完整重构方案。

Abstract: Reconstruction problems lie at the very heart of both mathematics and science, posing the fundamental challenge: \emph{How does one reconstruct a hidden structure from incomplete, fragmented, or distorted data?} In this paper, we introduce a new approach that harnesses the insights of the Vaisman Atiyah--Molino framework. In contrast to conventional methods that depend on persistent homology, our approach exploits the concept of the Vaisman centroid---an intrinsic invariant that encapsulates the averaged geometry of a data set---to resolve the inherent ambiguities of inverse problems. In the present paper, we focus on the theory and applications of the Vaisman centroid, offering a new perspective for Topological Data Analysis that eschews persistent homology in favour of a unified geometric paradigm. A subsequent paper will extend these ideas to a complete reconstruction scheme via the Atiyah--Molino framework. Our method provides a robust and computationally tractable framework for the recovery of hidden structures while opening new avenues for the analysis of high-dimensional and noisy data across the mathematical sciences.

</details>


### [143] [Characterisation of some multivalued harmonic functions on $\mathbb{R}^{n}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15722)
*Yang Li,Parsa Mashayekhi,Yichen Zhang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了由Donaldson和Yan分别构造的Z₂调和函数，其二次增长和分支集附近O(r^{3/2})局部增长这两个性质可以唯一刻画此类函数，即它们在刚体运动意义下唯一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Donaldson和Yan分别用不同方法构造了R³和Rⁿ（n≥3）上的Z₂调和函数，其例子在无穷远处具有二次增长，在光滑余维二分支集附近具有O(r^{3/2})的局部增长。一个自然的问题是：这些性质是否唯一确定了此类函数？本文旨在回答这一唯一性问题。

Method: 通过对Z₂调和函数的渐近行为进行分析，结合其在无穷远处二次增长和分支集附近局部增长速率的刻画，利用刚体变换将候选函数与已知的Donaldson-Yan构造进行比较，建立唯一性结果。

Result: 证明了满足二次增长和O(r^{3/2})局部增长条件的Z₂调和函数在刚体运动意义下是唯一的，即Donaldson和Yan的构造实际上给出了该问题本质上的唯一解类。

Conclusion: Donaldson与Yan的构造不仅是可行的例子，而且这些构造在给定增长条件下是完备且唯一的，这为Z₂调和函数的分类提供了完整的刻画。

Abstract: Using different approaches, Simon Donaldson and Dashen Yan recently constructed $\mathbb Z_2$-harmonic functions on $\mathbb R^3$ and on $\mathbb R^n$ for $n\geqslant 3$, respectively. Their examples have quadratic growth at infinity and $\mathcal{O}(r^{3/2})$ local growth near a smooth codimension-two branching set $Σ$. We show that these properties uniquely characterise such $\mathbb Z_2$-harmonic functions up to rigid motions of $\mathbb R^n$.

</details>


### [144] [Extremal mappings of tori, Teichmüller potentials and symmetric-space distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15794)
*Benson Farb,Eduard Looijenga*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文给出了对称空间 X_n = SL(n,R)/SO(n) 上不变度量 d_{X_n} 的模解释。该空间可视为带有标记、单位体积、n维平环面的Teichmüller空间。对于n=2，Teichmüller在1939年通过拟共形伸张的极值映射问题给出了d_{X_2}的模解释；但对于n≥3，这一解释一直未解决。作者引入了"总扩张"TE(f)和"Hilbert-Schmidt扩张"HE(f)两个概念，证明了它们在体积保持的Lipschitz映射的同伦类中恰由仿射映射取极小，且极小值等于d_{X_n}。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Teichmüller空间中不变度量的模解释是几何与分析的重要问题。对于n=2情形已有经典结果，但对于n≥3，自然的候选（极小拟共形伸张、Lipschitz常数、总能量）均不能给出d_{X_n}的模解释。因此需要找到新的度量或不变量来填补这一空白。

Method: 引入两个新的概念：(1) 总扩张TE(f)，与Gromov、Guth等人发展的"k-扩张"概念相关，定义在黎曼流形之间的Lipschitz映射上；(2) Hilbert-Schmidt扩张HE(f)，是对M上简单积分，具有L^2性质。对n维平直单位体积环面之间的体积保持Lipschitz映射，证明TE(f)与HE(f)在其同伦类中的极小化者恰好是仿射映射，且在这些映射上取值等于d_{X_n}。

Result: 证明了两个主要结果：(1) 体积保持Lipschitz映射的同伦类中，总扩张TE(f)由仿射映射极小化，极小值等于SL(n,R)/SO(n)上的不变度量d_{X_n}；(2) 类似地，Hilbert-Schmidt扩张HE(f)也在仿射映射处取极小且极小值等于d_{X_n}。由此给出了d_{X_n}在n≥3时的两个模解释。

Conclusion: 通过引入总扩张与Hilbert-Schmidt扩张这两个新的度量不变量，成功解决了对称空间SL(n,R)/SO(n)上不变度量在n≥3时的模解释问题，弥补了Teichmüller工作中n=2之后长期未解决的推广空缺。

Abstract: The symmetric space $X_n={\rm SL}(n,\Rb)/{\rm SO}(n)$ can be interpreted as the Teichmüller space of marked, unit volume, flat $n$-dimensional tori. It comes with a unique (up to scale) ${\rm SL}(n,\Rb)$-invariant metric $d_{X_n}$. In 1939 Teichmüller gave a modular interpretation of $d_{X_2}$ (the hyperbolic metric) in terms of an extremal mapping problem for quasiconformal dilatation. Such a modular interpretation for $d_{X_n}$ for $n\geq 3$ has remained unaddressed: the natural candidates - minimal quasiconformal dilatation, Lipschitz constant, or total energy - do not work.
  In this paper we give such a modular interpretation, two in fact. We introduce the {\em total expansion} $\TE(f)\in [0,\infty]$ of a Lipschitz map $f:M\to N$ between Riemannian manifolds, a notion related to the notion of ``$k$-dilatation'' developed by Gromov, Guth and others. For volume-preserving Lipschitz maps $f:\Tc_0\to\Tc_1$ between $n$-dimensional, flat, unit-volume tori, we prove that $\TE(f)$ is minimized in the homotopy class of $f$ precisely by the affine maps in that class and takes on these the value $d_{X_n}$. We prove similar results for the \emph{Hilbert-Schmidt expansion} $\HE(f)$, which is a simple integral over $M$ and has more of an $L^2$ flavor.

</details>


### [145] [Volume comparison and rigidity for positive holomorphic sectional curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15850)
*Ved Datar,Harish Seshadri*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了紧致连通Kähler流形在全纯截面曲率至少为2时的体积不超过同维复射影空间上具有常全纯截面曲率2的Fubini-Study度量的体积，且等号成立当且仅当该流形双全纯等距于复射影空间。这回答了Xiong和Yang提出的问题，并统一推广了Zhang的尖锐体积估计和Liu的刚性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回答Xiong和Yang提出的关于Kähler流形体积与全纯截面曲率之间的几何刚性关系的问题，同时寻求更一般的曲率正性条件下的统一体积估计框架。

Method: 引入一个新的曲率正性条件——平均RC曲率正性（mean RC curvature positivity），该条件由正Ricci曲率和正全纯截面曲率共同蕴含。在该新条件下证明尖锐的Churchill体积上界估计，并讨论等号情形。证明由ChatGPT 5.6 Sol Pro完成，作者验证并负责。

Result: 证明了在全纯截面曲率≥2的紧致连通Kähler流形上，体积不超过同维Fubini-Study度量的体积；等号当且仅当流形双全纯等距于复射影空间。该结果回答了Xiong-Yang的问题，同时给出了Zhang尖锐体积估计的新证明和Liu刚性定理的替代证明。

Conclusion: 通过引入平均RC曲率正性这一更一般的曲率条件，统一并推广了多个已知的尖锐体积估计和刚性定理，为Kähler几何中的体积刚性问题提供了新视角。

Abstract: We prove that the volume of a compact connected Kähler manifold with holomorphic sectional curvature at least 2 is bounded above by the volume of the Fubini-Study metric of constant holomorphic sectional curvature 2 on the complex projective space of the same dimension. Moreover equality holds if and only if the manifold is biholomorphically isometric to complex projective space. This answers a question posed by Xiong and Yang. Our approach also yields a different proof of Zhang's sharp volume estimate and Liu's rigidity theorem for compact Kähler manifolds with positive Ricci curvature. In fact, our main result states that the same sharp volume estimate holds under a new curvature positivity condition (mean RC curvature positivity), which is implied by both positive Ricci curvature and positive holomorphic sectional curvature. The definition of this condition was inspired by the work of Yang.
  The proofs in this paper are due to ChatGPT 5.6 Sol Pro, and the paper is merely an exposition of its output. The proofs has been verified by the authors and they take full responsibility for any errors.

</details>


### [146] [Regularity and structure of spaces with synthetic Ricci bounds and positive injectivity radius](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16021)
*Shouhei Honda,Ruobing Zhang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文针对具有合成下Ricci曲率下界的度量测度空间（RCD空间）建立了结构理论。在正单射半径假设下，证明了此类空间是 $W^{1,p}_{\mathrm{loc}}\cap C^{0,\alpha}_{\mathrm{loc}}$ 黎曼流形，其权重函数也属于同一正则性类，并给出了调和半径的定量下界、紧致性定理及定量几何界。该结果推广到光滑加权黎曼流形和满足CBA条件的RCD空间，并作为应用证明了纤维化定理，在合成框架下确认了Kapovitch关于混合曲率界几乎平坦流形的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RCD空间是Ricci曲率下界的合成推广，但其在正单射半径条件下是否具有光滑微分结构一直是核心问题。作者旨在回答此问题，并通过定量估计为后续几何应用（如纤维化定理和几乎平坦流形猜想）奠定基础。

Method: 论文发展了一套RCD空间的结构理论，在正单射半径假设下恢复了光滑微分结构，利用调和坐标与距离坐标证明了流形及其权重函数的 $W^{1,p}\cap C^{0,\alpha}$ 正则性。建立调和半径的定量下界与紧致性定理，并发展了一套该设定下的椭圆正则性理论。

Result: 证明了正单射半径的RCD空间是 $W^{1,p}_{\mathrm{loc}}\cap C^{0,\alpha}_{\mathrm{loc}}$ 黎曼流形（对任意 $p<\infty$、$\alpha\in(0,1)$），权重函数具有相同正则性；在距离与调和坐标下均成立。对满足CBA条件的RCD空间，度量和权重函数可为局部Lipschitz。这些结果对光滑加权黎曼流形也是新的。作为应用，建立了纤维化定理并确认了Kapovitch猜想。

Conclusion: 正单射半径RCD空间本质上具有良好的解析结构，正则性比此前已知结果更强，且定量估计可用于解决合成框架中的几何猜想（如Kapovitch关于几乎平坦流形的猜想），表明该结构理论具有广泛适用性。

Abstract: In the paper, we develop a structure theory for metric measure spaces with synthetic lower Ricci curvature bounds, known as RCD spaces. Under a positive injectivity-radius assumption, we recover a smooth differential structure and prove a regularity result: such spaces are $W^{1,p}_{\mathrm{loc}}\cap C^{0,α}_{\mathrm{loc}}$-Riemannian manifolds whose weight functions also belong to $W^{1,p}_{\mathrm{loc}}\cap C^{0,α}_{\mathrm{loc}}$, in both distance and harmonic charts for all $p<\infty$ and $α\in (0,1)$. We also establish a quantitative lower bound for the harmonic radius, together with compactness theorems and quantitative geometric bounds. As a byproduct, we develop a comprehensive elliptic regularity theory in this setting.
  These results apply, in particular, to smooth weighted Riemannian manifolds and to RCD spaces satisfying a synthetic curvature upper bound, or CBA condition. Even in these settings, the resulting regularity statements are new. In the latter case, both the Riemannian metric and the weight function are shown to be locally Lipschitz. As further applications, we establish fibration theorems and use metric smoothing to confirm, in a synthetic framework, a conjecture of V. Kapovitch concerning almost flat manifolds with mixed curvature bounds.

</details>


### [147] [A Curvature Gap for Minimal Submanifolds in Spheres](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16095)
*Fagui Li*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了单位球面中闭连通极小子流形（非全测地）的第二基本形式模长平方的最大值满足一个严格大于 2n/3 的下界，从而排除了该区间内可能存在的空隙。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在极小子流形几何中，确定第二基本形式模长平方S的最大值的下界是一个经典问题。已知若M非全测地，则max_M S ≥ 2n/3是一个经典结果，但等号是否能达到或在更精细的间隙区间内是否存在更高约束需要进一步刻画。本文旨在给出一个更强的显式下界。

Method: 利用极小子流形的结构方程、Ricci恒等式以及Sobolev不等式等技术，结合对第二基本形式模长及其高阶导数的精细估计，通过分析满足max_M S接近2n/3时的退化情形，得到更强的下界估计。

Result: 证明了对于非全测地的闭连通极小子流形M（n≥3, q≥2），有max_M S ≥ 2n/3 + 2^{-2n^2-10n-30} n^{-2n-2}，即max_M S严格大于经典下界2n/3，余量呈指数级小但在维度n下是显式的。

Conclusion: 该结果揭示了单位球面中极小子流形在S的最大值上存在一个更精细的间隙现象（gap phenomenon），即非全测地情形的S最大值不能任意逼近2n/3，为极小子流形的刚性定理和间隙定理提供了新的定量改进。

Abstract: Let $F:M^n\to\Sn^{n+q}(1)$ be a closed connected minimal immersion in the unit sphere with $n\ge3$, $q\ge2$, and $S=|h|^2$. We prove that if $M$ is not totally geodesic, then \[
  \max_M S\ge \frac{2n}{3}+2^{-2n^2-10n-30}n^{-2n-2}. \]

</details>


### [148] [On a compact Einstein-type manifolds with Riemannian foliations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16248)
*Jungwoo Moon*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究紧致Einstein型流形，其势向量场生成Riemann叶状结构，证明了该流形成为taut（紧致）流形以及具有分裂性质的充分必要条件，并给出了紧致几乎Ricci孤立子和紧致Einstein流形上taut Riemann叶状结构的一些性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Einstein型流形是微分几何中的重要研究对象，而Riemann叶状结构在流形结构分析中扮演关键角色。将两者结合，探究势向量场生成Riemann叶状结构时流形的几何性质（如taut性、分裂性质），有助于更深入地理解Einstein型流形的拓扑与几何结构。

Method: 采用微分几何与叶状结构理论相结合的方法，通过分析紧致Einstein型流形上由势向量场诱导的Riemann叶状结构的几何特征，推导流形成为taut流形和具有分裂性质的必要条件；并进一步将结果应用于紧致几乎Ricci孤立子和紧致Einstein流形的情形。

Result: 证明了紧致Einstein型流形在势向量场生成Riemann叶状结构时，成为taut流形及具有分裂性质的若干必要条件，并给出了紧致几乎Ricci孤立子和紧致Einstein流形上taut Riemann叶状结构的若干性质。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了紧致Einstein型流形、势向量场与Riemann叶状结构之间的内在联系，为深入理解这些流形的几何分类和结构性质提供了新的理论工具和结果。

Abstract: We investigate a compact Einstein-type manifold whose potential vector field generates a Riemannian foliation. In particular, we prove necessary conditions for such a manifold to be taut and to have a splitting property. Additionally, some properties of taut Riemannian foliations on a compact almost Ricci solitons and a compact Einstein manifolds are provided.

</details>


### [149] [Invariant connections in geometric mechanics: reduction, nonlocality, and curvature effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16561)
*Qiao Huang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了几何力学中不变联络的作用，发展了李群上左右不变联络的约化理论，并引入了一种依赖于联络的新变分原理，其中拉格朗日量依赖于平行移回曲线起点的速度。对于Cartan-Schouten联络，这导致了一个具有双重非局部性的积分-微分Euler-Poincaré方程，并给出了相应的数值方案和两个例证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在几何力学中，Euler-Poincaré和Lie-Poisson方程通常在李代数上进行约化，但不变联络在约化过程中所扮演的角色尚未被系统研究。作者旨在建立一套完整的不变联络约化理论，并探索引入联络依赖的变分原理能否产生新的动力学方程结构，特别是揭示非局部性现象。

Method: （1）首先在理论上发展李群上左、右不变联络的约化理论，证明Euler-Poincaré和Lie-Poisson方程与联络选择无关；（2）引入一个依赖于联络的变分原理，拉格朗日量中的速度通过平行输运回到曲线起点；（3）针对Cartan-Schouten联络推导出积分-微分Euler-Poincaré方程，识别出两类时间非局部来源：平行输运导致的路径依赖项和曲率积分导致的未来依赖项；（4）将方程重新表述为两点边值问题并设计两层数值格式；（5）在Heisenberg群（利用幂零性简化）和旋转群（保留完整积分-微分结构）上验证理论。

Result: （1）证明了Euler-Poincaré和Lie-Poisson方程对联络选择具有不变性；（2）推导出Cartan-Schouten联络下新的积分-微分Euler-Poincaré方程，具有路径依赖和未来依赖的双重非局部时间结构；（3）成功将方程转化为两点边值问题并提出两层数值求解方案；（4）Heisenberg群上由于幂零性方程大幅简化，旋转群上保留了完整的积分-微分结构。

Conclusion: 不变联络在几何力学约化理论中起着微妙而重要的作用：经典约化方程与联络无关，但引入联络依赖的变分原理可产生具有非局部时间结构的推广Euler-Poincaré方程。这一框架为几何力学提供了新的理论工具和数值方法，并有望推广到更一般的几何结构。

Abstract: We present a systematic study on the role of invariant connections in geometric mechanics. We first develop a comprehensive theory of reduction under left- and right-invariant connections on Lie groups, showing that the Euler--Poincaré and Lie--Poisson equations are independent of the connection. We then introduce a novel connection-dependent variational principle, where the Lagrangian depends on the velocity parallel-transported back to the initial point of the curve. For Cartan--Schouten connections, this leads to an integro-differential Euler--Poincaré equation that exhibits two distinct sources of nonlocality in time: a path-dependent term encoded in the parallel transport and a future-dependent term arising from a curvature integral. We reformulate this equation as a two-point boundary value problem and present a two-level numerical scheme. The general theory is illustrated on the Heisenberg group, where the equations simplify due to nilpotency, and on the rotation group, where the full integro-differential structure is retained.

</details>


### [150] [A Model-threshold Dimension Bound and Sharp Critical Ends for Smooth Singular Sets of Constant Positive $σ_k$-curvature Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16571)
*Jiahuan Li,Yilu Liu,Xi-Nan Ma*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究S^n中嵌入子流形Σ的补空间上完备共形度量g=v^{-2}g_{S^n}，满足σ_k(g^{-1}A_g)=κ>0且λ(g^{-1}A_g)∈Γ_k的条件下，子流形余维数p的上界p≤p_k(n)，其中p_k由模型阈值决定。当k=2且n=m²时构造了光滑完备等式例子；在有限正线性接触假设下证明了严格不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究源于共形几何中k阶曲率方程解的存在性与奇点结构问题，旨在推广经典Yamabe问题及σ_k-Yamabe问题的奇点分类结果，给出奇点集合维数（余维数p）的定量上界。

Method: 采用几何分析中的Pohozaev型恒等式与移动平面法相结合的技术路线，利用模型空间H^{p+1}×S^{n-p-1}的阈值p_k作为比较基准，通过接触假设（线性接触阶）来控制奇点附近解的渐近行为，并结合k=2时的特殊代数结构构造等号例子。

Result: 证明了余维数上界p≤p_k(n)；对于k=2且n=m²情形，构造了在S^n\S^{(m²-m-2)/2}上达到等号的光滑完备度量例子；并证明在有限正线性接触条件下可得严格不等式p<p_k(n)。

Conclusion: 该结果给出了σ_k-Yamabe问题中奇点余维数的最优上界，刻画了临界阈值与模型空间之间的深刻联系，为理解带奇点的k阶曲率方程解的全局结构提供了完整刻画。

Abstract: Let $k\in\mathbb N$ satisfy $1<k<n/2$, and let $Σ^p\subset\Sn^n$ be a closed smooth embedded submanifold. We prove that a complete conformal metric $g=v^{-2}g_{\Sn^n}$ on $\Sn^n\setminusΣ$ satisfying $λ(g^{-1}A_g)\in\Gk$ and $σ_k(g^{-1}A_g)=κ>0$ must obey \[ p\leq p_k(n), \] where $p_k$ is the model threshold determined by $\Hh^{p+1}\times\Sn^{n-p-1}$. When $k=2$ and $n=m^2$, we construct a smooth complete equality example on $\Sn^n\setminus\Sn^{(m^2-m-2)/2}$. We also prove that the strict inequality $p<p_k(n)$ holds under a finite positive linear-contact hypothesis.

</details>


### [151] [Atiyah's Minkowski Space Conjecture Fails for Every $n\ge3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16693)
*Ziran Liu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文否证了Atiyah关于闵可夫斯基时空中构型点构造的一个猜想：对每个n≥3，构造出的n个二元n-1次型并非总是线性独立的。文中给出n=3的显式反例，并通过零平移构造将其推广到所有n>3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Atiyah提出了一个闵可夫斯基空间版本的构型点构造，将一条可允许标记的世界线构型映射为n个二元形式（其根为有序迟滞类光方向），并猜想这些形式总是线性独立的。验证或否证该猜想是本文的核心动机，因为其成立与否关系到Atiyah-Sutcliffe行列式在该几何框架下的性质。

Method: 对于n=3，构造一个显式的平面单参数族世界线构型，使其实数系数行列式在指定区间内恰好有一个简单零点；在该参数处验证所有六个有序类光根互异且系数矩阵秩恰好为2。随后利用零平移构造（null-translation）将前三个形式乘上公因子，从而对所有n>3产生反例。

Result: 证明了在n≥3时Atiyah的线性无关猜想均不成立。n=3时存在平面单参数族使行列式恰有一个简单零点，此时六根互异、秩为2；对所有n>3，通过零平移构造得到了反例。在完全成对不相交的类时仿射直线类中，n=2时普适独立性成立，n≥3时均失败；所有反例的归一化Atiyah-Sutcliffe行列式都有定义且为零。

Conclusion: Atiyah的猜想仅在n=2时成立，对所有n≥3均被否证。该结果表明在此类几何构造中，线性无关性并非普适性质，且反例的Atiyah-Sutcliffe行列式既定义良好又取零值，深化了对该行列式性质的理解。

Abstract: Atiyah's Minkowski-space version of the configuration-of-points construction assigns to an admissible marked configuration of $n$ worldlines a collection of $n$ binary forms of degree $n-1$, whose roots are the ordered retarded celestial directions. He conjectured that these forms are always linearly independent.
  We disprove this conjecture for every $n\ge3$. For $n=3$, an explicit planar one-parameter family yields a real coefficient determinant with exactly one simple zero in a specified interval. At this parameter, all six ordered celestial roots are distinct and the coefficient matrix has rank exactly two. A null-translation construction then multiplies the first three forms by a common factor and produces counterexamples for every $n>3$. Consequently, within the class of complete pairwise disjoint timelike affine lines, universal independence holds at $n=2$ and fails for every $n\ge3$; for each of the counterexamples, the normalized Atiyah--Sutcliffe determinant is defined and vanishes.

</details>


### [152] [A two-point approach to the inverse problem in information geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16714)
*Florio M. Ciaglia,Giuseppe Marmo,Marco Pacelli,Luca Schiavone,Alessandro Zampini*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文在张量框架下将信息几何中的逆问题推广到一般度量-仿射流形（不施加曲率或挠率约束），通过平行移动构造局部平行化，进而构建对比双形式来恢复原始度量-仿射结构，并通过同伦约化恢复扭率统计流形和统计流形的逆问题，最后应用于约化齐次伪黎曼流形及半单李群和奇数维球面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典信息几何的逆问题仅适用于无挠统计流形，如何将这一框架推广到更一般、允许曲率和挠率的度量-仿射流形，是一个尚未解决的开放问题。

Method: 在两点张量框架下，将度量张量与仿射结构配对（通过平行移动实现的局部平行化），显式构造对比双形式以诱导原始度量-仿射流形；随后通过同伦约化恢复带挠统计流形和统计流形的逆问题，并将理论应用于约化齐次伪黎曼流形、带Cartan-Schouten联络的半单李群以及带Berger度量的奇数维球面。

Result: 获得了适用于一般度量-仿射流形的显式构造方案，恢复了带挠统计流形和统计流形的逆问题，得到了若干已有的预对比和对比函数构造，并成功推广应用至半单李群（Cartan-Schouten联络）和奇数维Berger球面等具体例子。

Conclusion: 该工作为一般度量-仿射流形的信息几何逆问题提供了统一的显式构造框架，并通过同伦约化将已有统计流形构造作为特例纳入，具有重要的理论推广意义。

Abstract: We formulate the inverse problem in information geometry within a two-point tensorial framework and solve it for general metric-affine manifolds, without imposing any curvature or torsion constraints. The construction is explicit and starts directly from the given geometric data: the metric tensor is paired to the affine structure, realized through a local parallelism obtained from parallel transport. This yields a contrast bi-form inducing the original metric-affine manifold. The inverse problems for statistical manifolds admitting torsion and for statistical manifolds are then recovered by homotopical reduction. In this way, suitable pre-contrast and contrast functions are obtained, including several established constructions for statistical manifolds and SMATs. We apply the general theory to reductive homogeneous pseudo-Riemannian manifolds endowed with invariant affine connections. Particular attention is devoted to semisimple Lie groups with Cartan-Schouten connections and to odd-dimensional spheres equipped with Berger metrics.

</details>


### [153] [Inhomogeneous Einstein metrics on complex projective spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16880)
*Gonzalo Cao-Labora,Alberto Rodríguez-Vázquez*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文在复射影空间上构造了首批非齐次Einstein度量，覆盖复维数3到7的情况，从而首次肯定地回答了Berger在1965年提出的关于复射影空间上是否存在其他Einstein度量的问题，尤其对复偶数维情形n=4和n=6给出了肯定答案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复射影空间上已知的Einstein度量仅有齐次情形：Fubini-Study度量（由Hopf纤维化得到）和Ziller度量（在奇数复维数下，通过四元数射影空间上的扭子纤维化沿规范变分得到）。1965年Berger证明了Fubini-Study度量是唯一的Kähler-Einstein度量，并提出了复射影空间上是否存在其他（非Kähler）Einstein度量的开放问题。构造非齐次Einstein度量是对这一问题的重要探索。

Method: 作者在复射影空间上系统地构造了非齐次Einstein度量，覆盖复维数3≤n≤7的范围。通过新的构造技术，在所有中等复维数的复射影空间上建立了一族非齐次Einstein度量，其中特别覆盖了此前没有已知非齐次Einstein度量的偶数复维数情形（n=4和n=6）。

Result: 成功构造了复射影空间上首批非齐次Einstein度量，覆盖复维数3≤n≤7的所有情形，并特别对复偶数维情形n=4和n=6给出了肯定回答Berger问题的答案。

Conclusion: 该结果表明复射影空间上的Einstein度量远不止此前已知的齐次情形，肯定地回答了Berger关于复射影空间上是否存在其他Einstein度量的经典问题，至少在复偶数维情形n=4和n=6上给出了确定性的肯定答案。

Abstract: The only Einstein metrics currently known on complex projective spaces are homogeneous: the Fubini-Study metric, arising via the Hopf fibration; and, in odd complex dimensions, Ziller's metric, obtained as a canonical variation along the twistor fibration over the quaternionic projective space. In 1965, Berger proved that the Fubini-Study metric is the unique Kähler-Einstein metric on the complex projective space, and posed the question of whether other Einstein metrics exist. We construct the first inhomogeneous Einstein metrics on complex projective spaces of complex dimension $n$ for $3\leq n \leq 7$, answering Berger's question affirmatively for the complex even dimensional cases $n=4$ and $n=6$.

</details>


<div id='math.OA'></div>

### [154] [Quantum channels on duals of von Neumann algebras in the Schrödinger picture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15116)
*Sviatoslav V. Dzhenzher*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文在薛定谔图景下，将量子信道视为一般von Neumann代数对偶上的完全正映射，并通过Pettis积分利用拓扑群的表示来构造这类信道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的量子信道理论要么局限于有限维态空间，要么在海森堡图景下作为C*-代数上的完全正映射研究。作者希望将量子信道的理论推广到一般von Neumann代数对偶空间的薛定谔图景，从而建立更广泛的量子信息理论框架。

Method: 将量子信道定义为一般von Neumann代数对偶空间上的完全正映射（薛定谔图景），利用拓扑群表示论中的Pettis积分来构造这类完全正信道。

Result: 本文尚未给出具体的数值或实验性结果，而是提供了在一般von Neumann代数对偶上通过Pettis积分构造量子信道的理论框架。

Conclusion: 量子信道可以在薛定谔图景下通过von Neumann代数对偶上的完全正映射进行统一描述，并且可以通过拓扑群表示的Pettis积分来系统地构造这类信道，从而扩展了量子信道理论的应用范围。

Abstract: The theory of quantum channels is traditionally studied either on finite-dimensional state spaces or within the Heisenberg picture as completely positive maps on C^*-algebras. In this paper, we consider quantum channels as completely positive maps on the duals of general von Neumann algebras in the Schrodinger picture. We investigate the construction of such channels through Pettis integrals using representations of topological groups.

</details>


### [155] [Non-uniqueness of continuous trace-scaling flows on certain full factors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15385)
*Soham Chakraborty,Sergiu Novac*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文研究了满II_infinity因子的连续迹缩放流（trace-scaling flow）的唯一性问题，证明了在Out(M)为局部紧第二可数群且有限角的根本群为全体正实数时，M要么具有唯一的连续迹缩放流（模共轭），要么具有连续统多个两两不同的此类流。这等价于具有连续核M的III_1因子要么唯一、要么存在连续统多个两两不同构的例子。该二分法结果应用于[Dep12]和[Cha25]中构造的因子，并给出了两个独立的新构造示例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解完全II_infinity因子上的连续迹缩放流的分类问题，特别是在外自同构群已知为局部紧李群的情形下，探究此类流的唯一性或非唯一性，进而关联到III_1因子的唯一性问题。

Method: 利用Connes核（core）理论，将II_infinity因子上的迹缩放流与III_1因子联系起来。通过分析外自同构群Out(M)的结构（要求其为局部紧第二可数群），结合根本群的条件，证明二分法结论。对已知构造应用该结果，并给出两个新的独立构造来说明连续统多个互不相同迹缩放流的存在性。

Result: 证明了二分法定理：在给定条件下，满II_infinity因子M要么具有唯一（模共轭）的连续迹缩放流，要么存在连续统多个两两不同的迹缩放流。将其应用于[Dep12]和[Cha25]构造的因子（其Out(M)为李群），并提供两个新的构造示例，其外自同构群未知，但确实存在连续统多个两两不同的迹缩放流。

Conclusion: 该二分法为满II_infinity因子上的迹缩放流分类提供了清晰的框架，连接了外自同构群的拓扑性质与III_1因子的唯一性。新构造表明，即使在外自同构群结构未知的情况下，非唯一性现象仍然可以发生。

Abstract: We show that if M is a full II_infinity factor such that the fundamental group of a finite corner is R and Out(M) is a locally compact second countable group, then M admits either a unique continuous trace scaling flow or a continuum of pairwise distinct ones, up to cocycle conjugacy. Equivalently, there is either a unique III_1 factor or a continuum of pairwise non-isomorphic III_1 factors with continuous core M. We apply this dichotomy result to factors constructed in [Dep12] and [Cha25] where Out(M) is a Lie group. We also give two independent constructions of full factors admitting a continuum of pairwise distinct trace-scaling flows where the outer automorphism group is unknown.

</details>


### [156] [1-Bounded Entropy for $C^*$-Algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15409)
*Connor MacMahon*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 该论文针对Voiculescu提出的问题，为C*-代数定义了两种1-有界熵概念：h（涉及所有迹的迹完备化）和h^top（依赖于算子范数微态）。证明了h^top不超过h，并引入了计算h的变分原理。通过计算各类例子中的h值，推导出C*-素性、交叉积不可分解性和自由不可分解性等结构结论。这些熵概念还推广到算子系统，并证明两者都可在基上计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究源于Voiculescu提出的关于1-有界熵在C*-代数语境下的开放问题，旨在为C*-代数建立合适的熵理论，并利用熵作为工具来推断代数结构性质。

Method: 定义了两种1-有界熵：h基于所有迹的迹完备化来定义，h^top基于算子范数微态来定义。通过证明h^top ≤ h的不等式关系，引入变分原理来计算h，并将这些概念推广到算子系统，证明两种熵均可在基上计算。

Result: 证明了h^top(𝒜) ≤ h(𝒜)对所有C*-代数成立；给出了计算h的变分原理；在多个例子中计算了h的具体值，并据此证明了C*-素性、交叉积不可分解性及自由不可分解性等结构结论；将熵概念成功推广至算子系统。

Conclusion: 该工作为C*-代数的1-有界熵理论建立了完整框架，熵的计算可以作为证明代数结构性质的有效工具，且理论可推广至更一般的算子系统。

Abstract: Towards a question of Voiculescu, two notions of $1$-bounded entropy, $h$ and $h^\text{top}$, are defined for $C^*$-algebras. The quantity $h$ involves the tracial completion with respect to all traces while the quantity $h^\text{top}$ depends on operator norm microstates. It is demonstrated that $h^\text{top}(\mathscr{A})$ does not exceed $h(\mathscr{A})$ for all $C^*$-algebras $\mathscr{A}$. Moreover, a variational principle enabling the computation of $h$ is introduced. The quantity $h$ is computed in various examples, and the computation is used to justify structural conclusions such as $C^*$-primeness, crossed product indecomposability, and free indecomposability. These notions of entropy are generalized to operator systems, where it is demonstrated that both may be computed on a basis.

</details>


### [157] [Operator Systems in Duality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16395)
*Markus Dannemüller,Tim Netzer*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文引入算子系统的相对对偶概念：两个系统在对偶时，其底层向量空间的配对在每个矩阵层级上诱导锥配对。证明了有忠实迹的可分B(H)和C*-代数存在对偶，而某些算子系统不存在。子系统、商和合适的张量积保持对偶性，并建立了有限级极大性与极小性的对偶关系及弱连续/弱*闭实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无限维序单位空间的通常对偶不一定具有序单位，这导致算子系统的对偶理论尤为困难。本文不试图计算典范对偶对象，而是引入一种相对对偶概念来绕开这一根本性困难。

Method: 定义两个算子系统之间的相对对偶关系：底层向量空间的配对在每个矩阵层级上诱导锥配对。在此基础上证明对偶的传递性质（子系统、商、张量积），并利用该框架建立有限级极大性与极小性的对偶对应，以及实现为ℓ∞-矩阵代数乘积的弱连续与弱*闭嵌入。

Result: 证明了在有限维算子系统之外，可分的B(H)以及具有忠实迹的C*-代数都存在对偶，同时构造了不具有对偶的算子系统反例。子系统、商和合适的张量积保持存在对偶的性质。有限级极大性与有限级极小性互为对偶，并且获得了弱连续和弱*闭的ℓ∞-乘积矩阵代数实现。

Conclusion: 相对对偶概念为无限维算子系统提供了一种可行的对偶理论框架，绕过了传统对偶中序单位缺失的核心困难，并推广了对偶性在结构保持和实现定理方面的应用。

Abstract: The usual dual of an infinite-dimensional order-unit space need not carry an order unit, and this is the main reason why duality for operator systems is notoriously hard. Rather than trying to compute a canonical dual object, we introduce a relative notion of duality for operator systems: Two systems are in duality when a pairing of their underlying vector spaces induces conic pairings at every matrix level. We develop basic properties of this notion, showing that beyond finite-dimensional operator systems, also separable $B(H)$ and $C^*$-algebras with a faithful trace admit duals, while some other operator systems do not. We prove that subsystems, quotients and suitable tensor products of systems with duals again admit duals. As consequences, we show that finite-level maximality is dual to finite-level minimality, and we establish weakly continuous and weak$^*$ closed realizations into $\ell^\infty$-products of matrix algebras.

</details>


### [158] [Ground states on the \(\mathrm{C}^*\)-algebras of Fell bundles over étale groupoids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16588)
*Md Amir Hossain*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文研究Fell丛C*-代数的实动力学σ^c的基态空间。作者建立了σ^c基态空间与边界群胚G(Z)上的Fell丛C*-代数C*(G(Z); A|G(Z))的状态空间之间的仿射同胚，其中Z是1-上循环c的边界集。特别地，C*(G;A)存在σ^c基态当且仅当Z≠∅。文章还探讨了基态与KMS_∞态的关系，给出了局部常值上循环情形下边界C*-代数的结晶化解释，并将其应用于Deaconu–Renault群胚的扭C*-代数和高阶图C*-代数等例子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基态是量子统计力学中零温极限条件下的平衡态，理解C*-代数的基态结构对研究其热力学性质和相关物理模型至关重要。对于Fell丛C*-代数这样一类重要的群胚C*-代数，此前缺少对实动力学下基态空间的系统刻画，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 利用群胚Fell丛的框架，通过实值1-上循环c诱导实动力学σ^c，将基态问题转化为关于边界集Z和边界群胚G(Z)的分析。作者构造了基态空间与边界C*-代数状态空间之间的仿射同胚，并研究了基态与KMS_∞态的关系，在局部常值上循环情形下给出了结晶化解释。

Result: 建立了σ^c基态空间与C*(G(Z); A|G(Z))状态空间之间的仿射同胚；证明了C*(G;A)存在σ^c基态当且仅当边界集Z非空；给出了基态与KMS_∞态关系的刻画；对Deaconu–Renault群胚的扭C*-代数和高阶图C*-代数等具体例类给出了基态的显式描述。

Conclusion: 本文为Fell丛C*-代数的基态理论提供了一个完整的框架，通过边界群胚的概念统一了基态的刻画，并展示了该理论在多个重要C*-代数类中的具体应用。

Abstract: Let \(p\colon \mathcal{A}\to G\) be a Fell bundle over a locally compact Hausdorff second countable étale groupoid \(G\) and let \(\mathrm{C}^*(G; \mathcal{A})\) be the associated Fell bundle \(\mathrm{C}^*\)-algebra. Suppose \(σ^c\) is the real dynamics on \(\mathrm{C}^*(G;\mathcal{A})\) induced by a real-valued \(1\)-cocycle \(c\). We establish an affine homeomorphism between the \(σ^c\)-ground state space of \(\mathrm{C}^*(G;\mathcal{A})\) and the state space of \(\mathrm{C}^*(G(Z); \mathcal{A}|_{G(Z)})\), where \(Z\) is the boundary set of \(c\) and \(G(Z)\) is the boundary groupoid. In particular, \(\mathrm{C}^*(G; \mathcal{A})\) admits \(σ^c\)-ground states if and only if \(Z\neq \emptyset\). We further investigate the relation between ground states and KMS\(_{\infty}\) states and give a crystallization interpretation of the boundary \(\mathrm{C}^*\)-algebra \(\mathrm{C}^*(G(Z); \mathcal{A}|_{G(Z)})\) when the cocycle is locally constant. Finally, we apply our results to several classes of examples, including twisted \(\mathrm{C}^*\)-algebras of Deaconu--Renault groupoids and twisted higher-rank graph \(\mathrm{C}^*\)-algebras, obtaining explicit descriptions of their ground states.

</details>
