### [159] [Wavelength-scale optional stopping, critical Feynman-Kac gauges, and capacitary spectral inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15650)
*Mayukh Mukherjee*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出两种布朗停止方法（基于命中概率/容量和加权退出分布矩），用于黎曼流形上的谱不等式推导，涵盖热可观性、零可控性、特征函数几何结构、Steklov特征函数估计等多类结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在闭黎曼流形上建立低能谱不等式、热可控性以及特征函数的精细几何性质，需要新的分析工具来处理零体积集上的平衡测度、小时间控制代价和测度最优性等问题。

Method: 第一种方法利用布朗运动的命中概率与容量，结合多尺度分析，在零体积集上构造平衡测度并推导低能谱不等式；第二种方法通过加权退出分布矩（第一、第二矩）控制随机时间退出行为，结合Feynman-Kac鞅、地面态变换和Remez定理（Logunov-Malinnikova）推导频率、三球不等式、加倍估计等结果。

Result: 在零体积集上获得低能谱不等式；对任意非降无界Φ(满足Φ(4E)≤DΦ(E))，可构造测度使最优谱常数与Φ(E)相当，小时间控制代价为T^{-1/2}Φ(T^{-1})^{1/2}；证明径向势下边界第二矩的log-凸性（因子2是最优的）；小测地球上获得几乎单调频率和加权三球不等式；对拉普拉斯特征函数和Steklov特征函数得到边界方差恒等式及L^∞/L²估计。

Conclusion: 布朗停止方法为谱不等式、可控性、特征函数几何与控制论提供了统一且高效的随机分析框架，并在多个维度达到最小的Hausdorff维数条件（m-2）和最优常数。

Abstract: We develop two Brownian stopping methods: one uses hitting probabilities and capacity, the other moments of weighted exit distributions. On a closed $m$-dimensional Riemannian manifold, $m\geq 2$, the first gives low-energy spectral inequalities for equilibrium measures supported on sets of zero volume. Combining scales gives heat observability and null controllability from a full-support measure carried by a dense set of Hausdorff dimension $m-2$, minimal under a bounded $H^1$-trace condition. For every nondecreasing unbounded $Φ$ with $Φ(4E)\leq DΦ(E)$, the measure can be chosen so that the optimal spectral constant is comparable to $Φ(E)$ and the small-time control cost to $T^{-1/2}Φ(T^{-1})^{1/2}$. Squaring the Feynman-Kac martingale doubles the potential: for radial $V$, the boundary second moment is log-convex in $\log r$ if $r^2V(r)$ is nondecreasing, wherever the regular solution of $(\tfrac12Δ+2V)g=0$ stays positive; the factor $2$ is sharp when $V(0)>0$. On small geodesic balls, weighted second moments give an almost-monotone frequency and a weighted three-radius inequality; bounds for its logarithmic derivative give doubling estimates, which, after a ground-state transform, feed the Remez theorem of Logunov-Malinnikova. The normalised first-moment exit law gives an averaged boundary-variance identity for Laplace eigenfunctions. At $r=cλ^{-1/2}$, outside a set carrying $O(c^{2/m})$ of the $\varphi^2$-mass, the component of $\{\varphi(x)\varphi>0\}\cap B(x,r)$ through $x$ contains a concentric ball of nearly full radius, fills nearly all of $B(x,r)$, and nearly matches its first Dirichlet eigenvalue. First-moment stopping also controls positive superlevel components. Reflected Brownian motion and local time give boundary-to-collar $L^\infty$ estimates for Steklov eigenfunctions on bounded Lipschitz domains, and an $L^2$ estimate for $C^2$ domains.

</details>


### [160] [On the kinetic Fokker--Planck equation in curved geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14904)
*Cédric Villani*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文档系统研究了紧致黎曼流形切丛上的动力学Fokker-Planck方程，主要关注全局亚椭圆C^∞光滑化与亚相干指数平衡化，在L²和L¹框架下均进行了论述，并统一简化了多个已有结果，同时提出了新的研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机来自相对论气体动力学（à la Debbasch）与几何分析（à la Bismut），旨在为不熟悉几何分析的读者提供自包含的动力学Fokker-Planck方程基础理论框架。

Method: 作者首先回顾动力学方程，然后扩展泛函分析与谱分析的基本工具库；重点研究了L²和L¹框架下的全局亚椭圆C^∞光滑化与亚相干指数平衡化；利用多种插值不等式、谱间隙和熵不等式进行推导与证明。

Result: 建立了多种类型的不等式（插值、谱间隙、熵不等式），简化并统一了若干已有结果，同时得到了一些新的结果，并提出了若干未来研究方向。

Conclusion: 该工作为动力学Fokker-Planck方程在紧致黎曼流形上的理论提供了统一的、自包含的现代处理，涵盖正则化与平衡化两大核心主题，对相对论动力学与几何分析均有参考价值。

Abstract: These notes are devoted to the kinetic Fokker--Planck equation on the tangent bundle of a compact Riemannian manifold, with motivations coming from relativistic kinetic theory of gases (à la Debbasch) and geometric analysis (à la Bismut). They are developed in particular from collaboration with Fabrice Debbasch and Yann Ollivier. The presentation is mostly self-contained, intended for readers without much prior familiarity with geometric analysis. Once the kinetic equations are reviewed, I expand the basic toolbox from functional and spectral analysis. Then the main focus is on global hypoelliptic $C^\infty$ regularization and hypocoercive exponential equilibration, both of which are addressed both in $L^2$ and $L^1$ settings. Various types of interpolation inequalities, spectral gap and entropic inequalities are established and used. Several existing results are simplified and unified, along with new ones. Some directions of research are mentioned.

</details>


### [161] [Global dynamics above the ground state energy for the 3D Zakharov system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14993)
*Zihua Guo,Kenji Nakanishi*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了三维Zakharov系统在基态能量附近略高能量下的全局动力学，利用正则形式技术和新开发的局部化virial估计，证明了初始数据集可划分为九个非空、两两不相交的区域，对应向前和向后时间方向上增长、散射或被基态俘获等不同行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前已有关于Zakharov系统在基态能量以下行为的分类结果，但能量略高于基态能量时的全局动力学尚未被理解。该问题涉及到二次频率相互作用带来的分析困难，需要发展新的数学工具来刻画系统在基态附近的调制行为以及远离基态时的动力学。

Method: （1）采用正则形式（normal form）技术处理二次频率相互作用；（2）围绕基态进行精细的调制（modulational）分析；（3）提出一类新型的具有单调性的局部化virial估计，用于研究远离基态的动力学，并结合virial估算与调制分析来刻画不同初始数据的行为分类。

Result: 成功证明了三维Zakharov系统在能量略高于基态能量时，初始数据集可划分为九个非空且两两不相交的区域，每个区域对应向前或向后时间方向上增长、散射或被基态俘获的行为组合，实现了对临界阈值动力学行为的完整分类。

Conclusion: 该结果将Zakharov系统的分类结果从基态能量以下推广到略高于基态能量的情形，与非线性Klein-Gordon方程和非线性薛定谔方程的类似分类相统一。新开发的局部化virial估计具有独立的研究价值，可能在其他问题上得到应用。

Abstract: We study the global dynamics for the 3D Zakharov system with radial initial data of energy slightly above the ground state energy, proving that the initial data set splits into nine nonempty, pairwise disjoint regions in which the solutions have distinct behaviors: growup, scattering, or trapped by the ground state, as time goes in the forward or backward directions. It is a classification similar to those for the nonlinear Klein-Gordon equation and for the nonlinear Schrödinger equation, extending the previous results on the Zakharov system below the ground state energy. The proof relies on the normal form technique to handle the quadratic frequency interactions and careful modulational analysis around the ground state. One novelty is a new family of localized virial estimates with monotonicity. These virial estimates are crucial for us to study the dynamics away from the ground state and may be of independent interest for other purposes.

</details>


### [162] [The 3D critical Zakharov--Kuznetsov equation: blow-up and soliton dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15086)
*Christian Klein,Svetlana Roudenko,Nikola Stoilov*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文全面研究了具有分数阶非线性项|u|^{4/3}u（等价于实值函数u^{7/3}）的L^2临界三维Zakharov-Kuznetsov（ZK）方程。首次处理非整数幂并在高维（3D）中研究解的动力学。工作包含2D临界ZK的有限时间爆破综述、略超临界爆破动力学的形式分析（推导任意维度下爆破速率和剖面的修正项），以及利用多GPU傅里叶伪谱离散和四阶Runge-Kutta积分因子方法进行全3D数值模拟。实验考察了基态扰动、高斯数据和不对称双凸结构，观察到色散与凝聚两种机制，辐射呈圆锥型区域反向传播，凝聚核心收敛到重标定的基态剖面。双凸实验表明总质量单独不能决定爆破动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该方程是广义Korteweg-de Vries（gKdV）方程的高维推广。在临界情形下，3D ZK方程的解可能在有限时间内爆破或表现出全局时间动力学。该工作的新颖性在于：（1）处理非整数幂的非线性项（以往工作多限于整数幂）；（2）在更高维度（3D）中研究解的全三维动力学。此前相关工作主要集中在2D情形，3D全数值模拟需要克服巨大的计算挑战。

Method: 方法分为三部分：（1）首先对2D临界ZK方程的有限时间爆破结果进行综述；（2）对略质量超临界的爆破动力学进行形式分析，推导任意维度下临界ZK方程爆破速率和爆破剖面的修正项；（3）开展计算研究：采用傅里叶伪谱离散和积分因子四阶Runge-Kutta方法在全三维网格上求解，并通过多GPU实现来追踪全3D环境中的爆破解。考察的初始数据包括基态的扰动、高斯数据以及非对称的双凸结构。

Result: 数值计算显示两类机制：色散态和凝聚态，两种情形下均观察到辐射沿圆锥型区域反向于传播方向发射。凝聚核心收敛到重标定的基态剖面。双凸实验表明总质量单独不足以决定爆破动力学。数值结果讨论了所预测爆破速率的证据，同时也指出了爆破时刻附近仍存在的预渐近和分辨率限制。

Conclusion: 该工作成功地在全3D背景下对具有分数阶非整数幂非线性项的L^2临界ZK方程进行了系统的数值和形式分析，填补了非整数幂和高维（3D）ZK方程动力学研究的空白。数值实验证实了凝聚核心向重标定基态剖面的收敛，揭示了辐射的圆锥型发射结构，并强调了爆破动力学不仅由总质量决定。文中也坦诚指出了爆破时刻附近数值模拟的预渐近和分辨率限制。

Abstract: We study the full three-dimensional dynamics of the $L^2$-critical Zakharov-Kuznetsov equation with the fractional nonlinearity $|u|^{4/3}u$, equivalently $u^{7/3}$ for real-valued functions. This equation is a higher-dimensional extension of the generalized Korteweg-de Vries equation. In the critical setting solutions to this 3D ZK equation may blow up in finite time or exhibit global time dynamics. The novelties of this work is to treat a non-integer power and to study the dynamics of solutions in a higher dimension.
  We first review the finite time blow-up in 2D critical ZK, then do a formal analysis of the slightly mass-supercritical blow-up dynamics, deriving the corrections to the blow-up rate and profile for the critical ZK equation in any dimension. We then perform a computational study of solutions, utilizing full 3D numerical simulations. In particular, we use a Fourier pseudospectral discretization and an integrating factor fourth-order Runge-Kutta method on a full three-dimensional grid. A multi-GPU implementation makes it possible to follow blow-up solutions in a full 3D setting. We examine perturbations of the ground state, Gaussian data, and nonsymmetric two-bump configurations. The computations show dispersive and concentrating regimes, in both cases with radiation emitted in a conic-type region opposite to the direction of propagation and convergence of the concentrating core toward a rescaled ground-state profile. The two-bump experiments also demonstrate that total mass alone does not determine the blow-up dynamics. We discuss the numerical evidence for the predicted blow-up rate and identify the pre-asymptotic and resolution limitations that remain near the blow-up time.

</details>


### [163] [From the SIR/SEIR/SVEIR Model to a Chemotactic System: Analysis of Spatiotemporal Mechanisms of Infection Spread within the Einstein Paradigm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15164)
*Yizhou Wang,Evgenia Echkina*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了SIR/SEIR类传染病模型及其ODE-based SVEIR扩展，利用真实流感爆发数据校准和验证参数；理论部分通过Picard-Lindelof定理确立了SVEIR模型解的存在唯一性并分析了可行域的不变性。为刻画城市环境中的时空感染传播，将SIR框架扩展为带扩散-趋化项的PDE系统，并引入恐惧/回避效应项。提出了一套数值工作流，包括网格离散化、镜像扩展施加Neumann边界条件、半隐式IMEX时间推进方案及稀疏线性求解，并通过总人口守恒监测计算正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典SIR/SEIR模型仅能描述时间维度的疫情演化，无法刻画城市环境中感染在空间上的传播过程，也无法显式建模人群行为（如恐惧/回避）对传播动力学的影响。因此需要将模型扩展为包含空间扩散与趋化效应的PDE系统，并建立严格的理论保证与可靠的数值求解方案。

Method: 1) 构建ODE-based SVEIR模型，用真实流感数据校准参数并验证；2) 通过Picard-Lindelof定理证明SVEIR系统解的存在唯一性，并分析可行域的不变性；3) 将SIR框架扩展为扩散-趋化PDE系统，引入恐惧/回避效应项以描述空间感染传播；4) 提出完整数值工作流：网格离散化、镜像扩展实现Neumann边界、半隐式IMEX时间推进、稀疏线性求解，并以总人口守恒作为计算正确性监控指标。

Result: 论文给出了SVEIR模型的理论保证（解的存在唯一性与可行域不变性），并设计了适用于扩散-趋化疫情PDE系统的完整数值求解框架，通过总人口守恒验证数值方案的可靠性。

Conclusion: 该工作为城市尺度疫情的空间传播建模提供了从理论到数值的完整方案：既包含严格的理论分析保障，又具备可实现的数值工作流，特别引入了恐惧/回避行为因素，使模型更贴近真实疫情中人群的空间响应特征。

Abstract: This work investigates the class of SIR/SEIR models and their extension to an ODE-based SVEIR system, whose parameters are calibrated and validated using real influenza outbreak data. The theoretical part establishes existence and uniqueness of solutions for the SVEIR model via the Picard-Lindelof theorem and analyzes invariance of the admissible region. To describe spatiotemporal infection spread in an urban setting, a diffusion-chemotaxis extension of the SIR framework is formulated as a PDE system; an additional term is introduced to represent fear/avoidance effects in the system dynamics. A numerical workflow is proposed, including grid discretization, Neumann boundary conditions enforced by mirror extension, a semi-implicit IMEX time-stepping scheme, and sparse linear solves; computational correctness is monitored, in particular, by checking conservation of the total population.

</details>


### [164] [Leibenson's equation on graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15168)
*Philipp Sürig,Xinrong Zhao*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了无限图上的Leibenson方程 ∂_t u = Δ_p u^q，证明了任意可积初值全局解的存在性，并在一定参数范围内证明了解的唯一性；利用Faber-Krahn不等式获得尖锐的ℓ¹-ℓ^∞平滑估计和有限支撑解的传播定量界；在等周不等式条件下证明了有限时间熄灭结果；特别地，在具有多项式体积增长的Cayley图上，建立了当q(p-1)>1时非负有限质量解的ℓ^∞范数最优大时间衰减率，并展示了穷竭解有限时间熄灭的尖锐二分性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Leibenson方程（又称多孔介质p-Laplace方程）在连续情形下已有丰富理论，但在离散图结构上的研究尚不充分。理解非线性扩散方程在无限图上的解的行为（如存在性、唯一性、平滑估计、传播速度和熄灭现象）对于离散PDE理论和应用具有重要意义，特别是在具有特定几何结构（如Cayley图）的图上建立最优衰减率。

Method: 本文采用离散p-Laplacian框架，首先构造弱解并证明其在可积初值下的全局存在性，在特定(p,q)参数范围内建立唯一性。利用图的Faber-Krahn不等式推导ℓ¹-ℓ^∞平滑估计；通过等周不等式分析解的有限时间熄灭；针对具有多项式体积增长的Cayley图，结合谱性质和几何估计建立衰减率及传播的定量界，最终通过穷竭解（exhaustion solutions）证明熄灭的二分性结果。

Result: 证明了无限图上Leibenson方程在任意可积初值下全局解的存在性及一定参数范围内的唯一性；获得了基于Faber-Krahn不等式的尖锐ℓ¹-ℓ^∞平滑估计和有限支撑解的传播定量界；在等周不等式条件下证明了有限时间熄灭；在多项式体积增长的Cayley图上，当q(p-1)>1时建立了ℓ^∞范数的最优大时间衰减率，并给出了穷竭解有限时间熄灭的尖锐二分性刻画。

Conclusion: 该研究系统建立了无限离散图结构上Leibenson方程的理论框架，将连续情形的经典结果推广到图论设定，并揭示了图几何（体积增长、等周不等式、Faber-Krahn不等式）对解的长期行为和熄灭现象的深刻影响。最优衰减率和二分性结果体现了离散非线性扩散方程的完整图景。

Abstract: In this paper we study on infinite graphs the Leibenson equation
  $$
  \partial_t u = Δ_p u^q,
  $$ where $p>1$, $q>0$ and $Δ_p$ denotes the discrete $p$-Laplacian. We prove, for any integrable initial data $u_0$, the existence of a global solution, which is unique for a certain range of $p$ and $q$. Assuming a Faber--Krahn inequality, we obtain sharp $\ell^1$-$\ell^\infty$ smoothing estimates and quantitative bounds on the propagation of solutions with initially finite support. Under certain assumptions on $p$ and $q$, we also prove finite-time extinction results for solutions when the graph satisfies an \textit{isoperimetric inequality}. In particular, on Cayley graphs with polynomial volume growth, we establish the optimal large-time decay rate of the $\ell^\infty$-norm for nonnegative finite-mass solutions when $q(p-1)>1$, and demonstrate a sharp dichotomy regarding the finite-time extinction of exhaustion solutions.

</details>


### [165] [A clarification on the distinction between nonlocal error and profile residual error in a computer-assisted proof of 3D Euler singularity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15174)
*Jiajie Chen,Thomas Y. Hou*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文是对Zhang (2025)评论性文章的回应性说明，澄清了其对Hou-Luo场景中自相似轮廓不存在性结果适用性的两点批评均不成立：第一，Zhang所指出的远场差异是原始非局部Poisson误差，而非Chen-Hou稳定性证明中使用的轮廓残差误差；第二，Zhang基于图形证据对角度增长假设适用性的论证，在相同的网格点数据下并不支持其解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回应Zhang (2025)中对该领域关于Hou-Luo场景中自相似轮廓近似构造的两点质疑，澄清误解并维护先前工作的有效性。

Method: 通过技术性分析区分两类误差（原始非局部Poisson误差与轮廓残差误差），并重新检验Zhang (2025)中使用的网格点数据以评估其图形证据的解释合理性。

Result: 证明Zhang所指的远场差异并非轮廓残差误差，非局部残差中包含的衰减因子提供了远场估计所需的微小性；同时表明Zhang (2025)中基于相同网格点数据的图形解释不成立，其比较和图形证据既不推翻Chen-Hou的残差估计，也不支持角度增长假设在Hou-Luo场景中的适用性。

Conclusion: Zhang (2025)的批评无法否定Chen-Hou工作中的残差估计，其角度增长假设适用性的主张缺乏依据。

Abstract: In \cite{zhang2025dimension}, the author discusses the applicability of a nonexistence result for self-similar profiles to the Hou--Luo scenario and raises questions about the approximate self-similar profile constructed in \cite{ChenHou2023a,ChenHou2023b}. In this note, we clarify two main points. First, the far-field discrepancy identified in \cite{zhang2025dimension} is the raw nonlocal Poisson error, not the profile residual error used in the stability proof of \cite{ChenHou2023a,ChenHou2023b}. The relevant nonlocal contribution to the profile residual contains the nonlocal velocity error multiplied by additional decaying factors, which provide crucial smallness in the far field estimates. Second, we examine the figure-based evidence used in \cite[Remark 2.4]{zhang2025dimension} to support the applicability of the assumption in \cite[Proposition 2.3(i)]{zhang2025dimension}, and show that the same grid-point data do not support that interpretation. Thus the comparisons and figure-based evidence in \cite{zhang2025dimension} neither invalidate the residual estimates in \cite{ChenHou2023a,ChenHou2023b} nor justify the claimed applicability of the angular-increase assumption to the Hou--Luo scenario.

</details>


### [166] [Nonlocal Tikhonov Regularization: Hilbert Scales, Explicit Rates, and the Classical Limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15315)
*Debangana Mukherjee,Akash Ashirbad Panda*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究有界Lipschitz域上线性反问题的分数阶Sobolev Tikhonov正则化，利用受限Dirichlet分数阶Laplacian生成惩罚项。通过等距变换将问题转化为经典Hilbert空间Tikhonov问题，推导出显式均方误差界及阶最优的先验和后验参数选取规则，并分析了后向分数热方程及s→1⁻的Γ-收敛极限行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Tikhonov正则化通常基于整数阶Sobolev范数，对于分数阶光滑性（如亚光滑解或不规则算子）的正则化问题缺乏系统框架。作者旨在建立基于分数阶Laplacian的变分正则化理论，提供严格的收敛性分析、误差估计和参数选取规则，并验证其数值有效性。

Method: (1) 引入受限Dirichlet分数阶Laplacian生成Tikhonov惩罚泛函，证明其在H0^s(Ω)中具有Lipschitz连续的唯一极小元；(2) 定义自伴算子A_s=I+(-Δ)^s，通过B=KA_s^{-1/2}将问题等距转化为经典Hilbert空间Tikhonov问题；(3) 在Hölder型源条件下推导显式均方误差界及先验/后验阶最优参数规则；(4) 对后向分数热方程做模式级分析（B*B=A_s^{-1}e^{-2tA_s}），研究奇异值衰减与有效重建带宽；(5) 利用Bourgain-Brezis-Mironescu归一化证明s→1⁻时Γ-收敛到经典H0^1-Tikhonov泛函；(6) 通过Monte Carlo模拟和Morozov偏差原理进行数值验证。

Result: 建立了分数阶Sobolev Tikhonov正则化的完整理论框架：极小元存在唯一且数据依赖Lipschitz连续；获得显式均方误差界和Hölder源条件下的阶最优先验/后验参数规则；后向分数热方程给出了源条件、奇异值衰减和重建带宽的模式级刻画；s→1⁻时分数泛函Γ-收敛于经典H0^1泛函且极小元在L2中强收敛；数值实验确认理论收敛速率（误差在几个百分点内），Morozov偏差原理达到相同阶最优后验速率。

Conclusion: 该工作为分数阶正则化提供了严格的数学基础，将分数阶Laplacian正则化与经典Hilbert空间理论统一，既适用于广泛的反问题类型，又在极限情形下与整数阶方法相容，兼具理论完备性和计算可行性。

Abstract: We study fractional-Sobolev Tikhonov regularization for linear inverse problems on a bounded Lipschitz domain. The regularization penalty is generated by the restricted Dirichlet fractional Laplacian, and the associated variational problem is shown to admit a unique minimizer that depends Lipschitz continuously on the data. Identifying the positive self-adjoint operator $$A_s=I+(-Δ)^s,\, D(A_s^{1/2})=H_0^s(Ω),$$ we transform the problem isometrically into a classical Hilbert-space Tikhonov problem with observation operator $B=KA_s^{-1/2}$. This yields explicit mean-square error bounds and an order-optimal \emph{a priori} and \emph{a posteriori} parameter rules under Hölder-type source conditions.
  The framework is illustrated by partial observations and by the backward fractional heat equation. In the latter case, $$ B^*B=A_s^{-1}e^{-2tA_s}, $$ which permits a mode-wise description of the source condition, the singular-value decay, and the effective reconstruction bandwidth. We also study the local limit $s\to1^-$: after Bourgain--Brezis--Mironescu normalization, the fractional functionals $Γ$-converge in $L^2(Ω)$ to the classical $H_0^1$-Tikhonov functional, and the corresponding minimizers converge strongly in $L^2(Ω)$. Numerical experiments for the backward fractional heat problem illustrate the reconstruction procedure and the influence of the penalty order, and confirm the predicted mean-square convergence rate to within a few percent via Monte Carlo simulation, with Morozov's discrepancy principle attaining the same order-optimal rate a posteriori.

</details>


### [167] [The Physical Cutoff Does Not Restore Homogenization: Phase-Dependent Burning in the Strain G-Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15337)
*Michele Caprio*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文否定了Xin、Yu和Ronney关于细胞流中物理正部应变G-方程存在有效燃烧速度的预期。在二维标准细胞流中，当参数满足特定条件时，周期修正会随时间至少线性振荡，导致重整化解不存在局部一致收敛子列。证明基于哈密顿夹逼不等式和上比较器的不变比较通道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Xin、Yu和Ronney此前预期物理正部应变G-方程在细胞流中应具有有效燃烧速度。本文旨在检验这一预期，并证明在特定参数范围内该预期不成立，即细胞流中不存在有效燃烧速度，这对燃烧理论中湍流火焰传播的均质化理论具有根本性影响。

Method: 利用哈密顿夹逼不等式H_unc ≤ H_+ ≤ Ĥ建立比较框架。上比较器Ĥ为矩形支撑函数（等价于状态依赖credal集上的上期望），其反向控制动力学具有不变比较通道。证明周期修正振荡至少线性增长，并通过屏障证书（barrier certificate）的覆盖半径上界（≤2dε）来完成刚性问题证明。使用Lean 4形式化验证了部分子区域和逻辑组装。

Result: 证明了：当0<d<20/399且√(1+4d²)<Ad≤1+d/10时，对每个单位平面斜率，周期修正随时间至少线性振荡。解在(π,0)附近的水平通道上保持下有界，而在(π/2,0)处以至少CA/log A的速率下降。在物理标度下，任意正宏观时刻，相距O(ε)的点之间始终保持阶一值隙，因此重整化解无局部一致收敛子列。此外，对任意C²不可压缩周期流，每个ε-外向屏障证书的覆盖半径至多为2dε。

Conclusion: 本文否定了细胞流中有效燃烧速度的存在性预期，揭示了均质化在该参数区域内失效。这一结果对燃烧理论中的湍流火焰传播模型具有重要意义，同时对鲁棒序贯决策中的不确定性传播提出警示：矩形的、时间一致局部不确定性在长期中不必然导致初始状态遗忘，需要额外的全局稳定性或遍历性条件。

Abstract: We disprove the expectation stated by Xin, Yu, and Ronney that the physical positive part strain $G$-equation should possess an effective burning velocity in cellular flows. For the standard cellular flow in dimension two $V_A(x_1,x_2)=A(-\sin x_1\cos x_2,\cos x_1\sin x_2)$, if $0<d<20/399$ and $\sqrt{1+4d^2}<Ad\le1+d/10$, then for every unit planar slope the periodic correction develops oscillations at least linearly in time. The solution remains bounded below on an explicit horizontal channel through $(π,0)$, while at $(π/2,0)$ it decreases at rate at least $CA/\log A$, with $C>0$ universal. The same conclusions hold for arbitrary continuous periodic perturbations of planar initial data. Under the physical scaling $V_A(x/\varepsilon)$ and $d_\varepsilon=\varepsilon d$, an order one value gap persists between points at distance $O(\varepsilon)$ at every positive macroscopic time, so the rescaled solutions have no locally uniformly convergent subsequence. The proof uses the Hamiltonian sandwich $H_{\mathrm{unc}}\le H_+\le\widehat H$. The upper comparator $\widehat H$ is a rectangular support function, equivalently an upper expectation over a state-dependent credal set, whose reversed control dynamics possess an invariant comparison channel. We also prove that for any $C^2$ incompressible periodic flow, every $\varepsilon$-outward barrier certificate has covering radius at most $2d\varepsilon$ for all sufficiently small $\varepsilon$. We further discuss implications for statistics and machine learning: rectangular, time-consistent local uncertainty need not imply forgetting of the initial state in the long run, so additional global stability or ergodicity conditions are needed in robust sequential decision making. Two Lean 4 appendices record conditional formalizations of a sufficient $p=e_1$ subregime and of the logical assembly of the rigidity theorem for barrier certificates.

</details>


### [168] [Resonant Geometry and Well-Posedness for the 2D Boussinesq Wave Kinetic Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15340)
*Haoling Xiang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了由Shavit-Bühler-Shatah从二维Boussinesq内波系统推导出的波动力学方程(WKE)，该方程具有各向异性、向量值、双分支变号色散关系等特点。作者引入了适应共振流形角退化结构的自然角截断，证明了截断碰撞算子的有界性，并在加权L∞空间中建立了局部适定性，同时证明了在保留零频截断的情况下移除特定角度处的截断会使碰撞算子无界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 来自Boussinesq内波系统的WKE具有各向异性、向量值、双分支变号色散关系等复杂结构，其共振流形存在角退化现象，阻碍了碰撞算子标准解析处理方法的直接应用。目前缺乏针对这类方程的严格解析理论框架，因此需要建立新方法处理这些退化障碍。

Method: 作者引入了一种适应共振流形角退化结构的自然角截断（angular cut-off），在此截断下证明碰撞算子的有界性，并在加权L∞空间中建立局部适定性。同时通过反例分析证明，即使保留零频截断，在|cosθ|=1/2附近移除截断会使碰撞算子在这些加权空间上无界。

Result: 建立了截断碰撞算子的有界性，获得了加权L∞空间中的局部适定性结果；并证明了在特定角度附近移除截断会导致碰撞算子无界，从而严格揭示了该截断的必要性。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了关于Boussinesq动力学导出的各向异性向量值WKE的首个严格解析框架，为后续研究此类方程的长时间行为、能量守恒等更深层性质奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: We study the wave kinetic equation (WKE) derived by Shavit--Bühler--Shatah from the two-dimensional Boussinesq system of internal waves. The equation is anisotropic and vector-valued, with a two-branch sign-changing dispersion relation, coupled propagation branches, and a sign-indefinite pseudo-momentum invariant. The resonant manifold has angular degeneracies that obstruct the standard analytic treatment of the collision operator. We introduce a natural angular cut-off adapted to these degeneracies, prove boundedness of the cut-off collision operator, and establish local well-posedness in weighted $L^\infty$ spaces. We also show that, even with the zero-frequency cut-off retained, removing the cut-off near $|\cosθ|=\tfrac12$ makes the collision operator unbounded on these weighted spaces. This provides, to our knowledge, the first rigorous analytic framework for an anisotropic vector-valued WKE arising from Boussinesq dynamics.

</details>


### [169] [Regularization and Hölder continuity for complex Hessian equations on Hermitian manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15552)
*Yulun Xu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文在紧致Hermitian流形上发展了Γ-容许函数的定量正则化方法，并证明了右端项属于L^p(p>n/m)的复m-Hessian方程的每个多重位势解都具有Hölder连续性。结果推广到满足Guo-Phong-Tong结构条件的更一般复Hessian方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复Hessian方程在复几何中具有重要地位，但之前对解的Hölder正则性缺乏统一的定量方法。作者旨在发展一种适用于紧致Hermitian流形上Γ-容许函数的定量正则化工具，并利用它推广复m-Hessian方程解的Hölder连续性结果到更广泛的方程类别。

Method: 作者首先为紧致Hermitian流形上的Γ-容许函数建立了一个定量正则化方法，然后利用该方法证明复m-Hessian方程的解具有Hölder连续性。该结果进一步推广到满足Guo-Phong-Tong结构条件的更一般复Hessian方程类。

Result: 证明了当右端项属于L^p(p>n/m)时，复m-Hessian方程的每个多重位势解都是Hölder连续的，并且该结论可推广至更一般的满足Guo-Phong-Tong结构条件的复Hessian方程。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一种统一的定量正则化框架，克服了紧致Hermitian背景下复Hessian方程正则性的技术困难，并将Hölder连续性结果推广到了更广泛的方程类。

Abstract: Let $(M,ω)$ be a compact Hermitian manifold, and let $Γ$ be a symmetric convex cone. We develop a quantitative regularization method for $Γ$-admissible functions. As an application, we prove Hölder continuity for every pluripotential solution of complex $m$-Hessian equations whose right-hand sides belong to $L^p$, for $p>\frac{n}{m}$. These results extend to a more general class of complex Hessian equations satisfying the structural condition used by Guo--Phong--Tong.

</details>


### [170] [Rigorous Validation of Cusp Bifurcations of Stationary Periodic Patterns in Partial Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15613)
*Dominic Blanco,Jean-Philippe Lessard*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种计算机辅助的严格验证框架，用于证明抛物线半线性偏微分方程中空间对称稳态周期图案的尖点分岔的存在性，并成功应用于Swift–Hohenberg方程和Gray–Scott系统的一维与二维情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尖点分岔是动力系统中的关键分岔现象，但在无穷维PDE背景下缺乏严格的验证方法。已有的尖点映射形式化仅适用于有限维系统，需要将其推广到无穷维Hilbert空间，以便用计算机辅助证明严格确认此类分岔。

Method: 将尖点条件转化为Fourier系数Hilbert空间上的零找问题；仔细处理序列空间中的伴随乘法算子；从数值近似出发，利用构造性的Newton–Kantorovich论证证明尖点映射附近零点的存在性与局部唯一性；通过Gershgorin型估计严格包络线性化算子的谱，确认恰好一个特征值具有零实部；由k和c的符号共同判定尖点区域内三个共存解的稳定性结构。

Result: 在Swift–Hohenberg方程（一维和二维）及Gray–Scott系统（一维和二维）四种设定下，均获得了尖点分岔的严格数学证明。

Conclusion: 该框架成功将有限维尖点映射方法推广到无穷维PDE设定，为空间对称稳态周期图案中尖点分岔的严格验证提供了系统化工具，并能同时确定解的稳定性结构。

Abstract: In this paper, we present a computer-assisted framework for the rigorous validation of cusp bifurcations of spatially symmetric stationary periodic patterns in parabolic semilinear partial differential equations. Our approach extends to an infinite-dimensional setting the cusp map formulation previously developed in finite dimensions. We formulate the cusp conditions as a zero-finding problem on a Hilbert space of Fourier coefficients, whose non-degenerate solutions correspond to cusp bifurcation points. A key technical ingredient is a careful treatment of the adjoint multiplication operator in the resulting sequence space, which is more involved than in the finite-dimensional case. Starting from a numerically computed approximation, we develop a constructive Newton-Kantorovich argument to prove the existence and local uniqueness of a nearby zero of the cusp map. The non-degeneracy of this solution directly yields the non-vanishing of the cubic normal form coefficient $c$. To complete the verification, we rigorously enclose the spectrum of the linearized operator, confirming that exactly one eigenvalue has zero real part via Gershgorin-type estimates adapted to the symmetry structure of the problem. As a further consequence, the number $k$ of eigenvalues with strictly positive real part at the cusp and the sign of $c$ (both rigorously certified by the framework) together determine the stability structure of the three coexisting solutions inside the cusp region: bistability arises when $k=0$ and $c<0$, monostability when $k=0$ and $c>0$, and no stable solution exists when $k \geq 1$. We apply the method to the Swift--Hohenberg equation and the Gray--Scott system in both one and two spatial dimensions, obtaining rigorous proofs of cusp bifurcations in all four settings.

</details>


### [171] [New sign-changing solutions to the Hénon problem in dimension $2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15638)
*Francesca Gladiali,Pingping Yang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文为二维有界光滑区域中含原点的Hénon问题构造了新的变号解，其中指数p趋于无穷大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hénon问题在非线性分析及天体物理中有重要应用，构造其变号解（特别是当非线性指数p趋于临界无穷大时）是一个具有挑战性的问题。

Method: 本文通过构造新的变号解，利用变分法与渐近分析等技术处理Hénon问题，其中参数α>0且非线性指数p→+∞。

Result: 成功构造了Hénon问题在二维有界光滑区域中的新变号解。

Conclusion: 本文在p趋于无穷大的极限情形下，为带权重|x|^α的Hénon方程建立了新的变号解存在性结果。

Abstract: In this paper, we construct new sign-changing solutions for the Hénon problem \begin{equation*} \begin{cases} -Δu= |x|^α |u|^{p-1}u,\,\,\,\text{in}\,\,\, Ω, \\[1mm] u=0,\,\,\,\,\,\,\text{on}\,\,\, \partialΩ, \end{cases} \end{equation*} where $Ω\subseteq \mathbb{R}^2$ is a bounded smooth domain containing 0, $α\in\mathbb{R}$ is a positive parameter and $p$ approaches $+\infty$.

</details>


### [172] [Long-Wave Spectral Instability of Shear Layers for the Compressible Euler Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15730)
*Chao Wang,Yuxi Wang,Wenzhi Wu,Zhifei Zhang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了二维可压缩欧拉方程在光滑单调剪切层附近的长波谱不稳定性，证明了对于任意固定马赫数m>0，存在足够小波数下的不稳定模态，并在m=√2处发现了长波不稳定性标度的转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解可压缩剪切层的流动稳定性对于湍流转换、混合增强等流体力学问题具有重要意义。先前关于不可压缩情形下的长波不稳定性研究已有较多结果，但可压缩情形下长波不稳定性的标度行为，特别是在马赫数临界点处的转变，尚缺乏严格的数学分析。此外，该问题与超声速可压缩涡层稳定性结果之间存在一致性，需要通过二维可压缩欧拉方程来深入理解这一机制。

Method: 通过长波展开构造可压缩瑞利方程的衰减半直线解，并对匹配Wronskian进行二阶展开。利用谱分析的方法，针对不同马赫数范围（m<√2, m=√2, m>√2）分别讨论不稳定模态的存在性及相应的波速、增长率和临界层的渐近行为。在零厚度极限下，分析超临界不稳定特征值的极限行为。

Result: 对于每个固定马赫数m>0和小波数，建立了不稳定模态的存在性。当m<√2时，一类剪切廓线存在不稳定模态，虚部波速趋于正常数；当m=√2时，廓线U_s(Y)=tanh Y存在c_i~α^{1/3}量级的不稳定模态；当m>√2时，同一廓线的不稳定性仍存在，c→c_*(m)∈(0,1)且c_i~α。对应的时间增长率分别为α、α^{4/3}和α²量级，表明在m=√2处发生了长波不稳定性标度的转变。在零厚度极限下，超临界不稳定特征值趋于实轴，与可压缩涡层稳定性结论一致。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了二维可压缩欧拉方程剪切层长波不稳定性的完整图景：在m=√2处存在不稳定性标度从低马赫数到高超马赫数的临界转变，且长波不稳定模态与超声速可压缩涡层的稳定性结果在零厚度极限下相互一致，为可压缩流动稳定性的数学理论提供了新的严格结果。

Abstract: We study the long-wave spectral instability of the two-dimensional compressible Euler equations around smooth monotone shear layers. We construct decaying half-line solutions of the compressible Rayleigh equation through a long-wave expansion and derive a second-order expansion of the matching Wronskian. For every fixed Mach number $m>0$, we prove the existence of unstable modes for sufficiently small wavenumbers. For $m<\sqrt2$, this holds for a class of profiles, with $c_i$ tending to a positive constant as $α\to0$. At $m=\sqrt{2}$, the profile $U_s(Y)=\tanh Y$ admits an unstable mode with $c\to0$ and $c_i$ of order $α^{1/3}$. For $m>\sqrt2$, the same profile remains unstable, with $c\to c_*(m)\in(0,1)$ and $c_i>0$ of order $α$. The corresponding temporal growth rates are of order $α$, $α^{4/3}$ and $α^2$, respectively, showing a change in the long-wave instability scaling at $m=\sqrt2$. In the zero-thickness limit, the supercritical unstable eigenvalue approaches the real axis, consistently with the stability results for supersonic compressible vortex sheets in \cite{CS1,CS2}.

</details>


### [173] [Quasianalyticity and geometric rigidity in anisotropic Calderón's problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15813)
*Lu Chen,Yan Jiang,Hongyu Liu,Longyue Tao*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究n≥3维各向异性Calderón问题，在两组互补条件下建立唯一性结果：一是通过拟解析函数的恒等原理在一般几何中传播边界信息并实现唯一性；二是在对称性或单侧序假设下，在C^∞正则性下实现全边界或部分边界的唯一性。两种机制呈现正则性、几何结构与边界访问之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 各向异性Calderón问题在n≥3维的一般光滑度量下仍然悬而未决，是逆问题领域的重要开放问题。作者旨在通过引入不同的结构假设来建立唯一性结果，填补这一领域的空白。

Method: 作者采用两种互补的技术路线：(1) 利用拟解析函数的恒等原理将边界信息向内传播，在一般几何下获得唯一性，并推导出部分边界访问的结果；在规定的法向几何下，只在特殊方向上需要拟解析性。(2) 施加对称性或单侧序假设，在C^∞正则性下借助全边界或受限边界数据获得唯一性。

Result: 建立了两个互补体制下的唯一性结果：一般几何下基于拟解析性的唯一性（含部分边界情形），以及C^∞正则性下基于对称性或单侧序假设的唯一性（含全边界或受限边界数据）。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了正则性、几何结构与边界访问三者之间的权衡关系：拟解析性可在一般几何中提供连续性延拓，而对称性或单侧序假设则可在C^∞正则性下替代这种延拓。

Abstract: The anisotropic Calderón problem in dimensions $n\ge3$ remains open for general smooth metrics~\cite{Uhlmann2009}. We establish uniqueness results in two complementary regimes. In the first, the identity principle for quasianalytic functions propagates boundary information and yields uniqueness in general geometry, including a partial-boundary consequence; under a prescribed normal geometry, quasianalyticity is needed only in the distinguished direction. In the second, suitable symmetry or one-sided ordering assumptions lead to uniqueness at $C^\infty$ regularity with full or restricted boundary access. Taken together, the results exhibit a tradeoff among regularity, geometric structure, and boundary access: quasianalyticity supplies continuation in general geometry, while symmetry or one-sided order replaces that continuation at $C^\infty$ regularity.

</details>


### [174] [An introduction to the vectorial Calculus of Variations in $\textbf{L}^\infty$ AND Aronsson PDE systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15852)
*Hussien Abugirda,Nikos Katzourakis*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文是一篇综述性文章，介绍变分法中关于上确界泛函的向量变分问题的子领域，重点讨论其极值条件所关联的PDE系统，涵盖了从1960年代Aronsson建立标量理论到2010年代向量理论的发展历程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: L∞变分法在众多应用领域具有重要意义，但其研究面临严重困难，需要不同于经典积分泛函和Euler-Lagrange方程的新工具。本文旨在系统介绍这一相对新兴但重要的子领域，特别是向量情形下的基本理论和挑战。

Method: 本文为综述性论文，部分基于第二位作者所讲授的短期课程讲义，系统梳理了上确界泛函的向量变分问题研究，涵盖历史发展（从Aronsson在1960年代的标量工作到2010年代初的向量工作）、理论框架以及相关的PDE系统极值条件。

Result: 展示了向量情形下上确界泛函变分问题的理论发展脉络，阐明了标量理论与向量理论的历史演进，并指出了该领域所需的区别于经典变分法的新工具与新技术。

Conclusion: L∞变分法（尤其是向量情形）是一个重要但充满挑战的领域，需要发展全新的数学工具，与经典的积分泛函和Euler-Lagrange方程方法有本质区别。

Abstract: In this expository article, which is partially based on the lecture notes of a short course delivered by the second appearing author, we introduce the subfield of the Calculus of Variations that is concerned with the study of vectorial variational problems for supremal functionals, with emphasis on the associated PDE systems arising as extremality conditions. The scalar theory has a rather short history, first arising in the work of G. Aronsson in the 1960s. The vectorial case is even more recent and first arose in work of the second appearing author in the early 2010s. The Calculus of Variations in $L^\infty$ is an all-important field for numerous applications, but it presents serious difficulties and requires new tools, different to those required for the classical case of integral functionals and Euler-Lagrange equations.

</details>


### [175] [Dimension-Free Lipschitz Bounds for Brenier Maps to Compactly Supported Log-Concave Targets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15906)
*Maja Gwozdz*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究Brenier最优传输映射∇Φ的正则性估计，证明了对数凹源测度μ到紧支撑对数凹目标测度ν的Brenier映射满足方向Hessian估计∂_{vv}Φ ≤ 0.587√(Qv,v)w_K(v)（分布意义），并给出了全局Lipschitz界Lip(∇Φ)≤0.587√(‖Q‖_op)diam(K)。该估计是仿射协变的、维数无关的，消除Kolesnikov估计中的√d损失，并推广到奇异/低维目标及半对数凹目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最优传输映射（Brenier映射）的正则性估计在多个数学与物理分支中具有核心地位。此前Kolesnikov给出的估计中带有√d因子（即维度相关），这在实际应用中（尤其是高维问题）不够理想。作者的目标是消除这一维度损失，获得维数无关且仿射协变的更优估计。

Method: 作者考虑的源测度μ满足二次密度的凸性条件（x↦½⟨Qx,x⟩−V(x)凸），目标测度ν为紧支撑对数凹概率测度。通过定义方向宽度函数w_K(v)刻画目标支撑K的方向几何，在分布意义下建立方向Hessian的逐点上界，进而通过对所有方向的估计推导出全局Lipschitz表示。方法结合了Brenier映射的凸分析和最优传输的对偶理论。

Result: 得到两个核心结果：(1) 分布意义下的方向性Hessian估计∂_{vv}Φ≤0.587√(Qv,v)w_K(v)，该估计仿射协变；(2) 全局Lipschitz估计Lip(∇Φ)≤0.587√(‖Q‖_op)diam(K)，该估计维数无关。该结果消除了Kolesnikov估计中的√d损失，且适用于奇异的或低维目标测度。文中还给出了仅依赖支撑集的紧致半对数凹目标新界。

Conclusion: 本文显著改进了Brenier映射的Lipschitz/Hessian正则性估计，达到了维数无关且仿射协变的最优形式，统一并推广了此前结果，涵盖更广泛的目标测度类（包括奇异、低维及半对数凹带负曲率的情形）。

Abstract: We fix an integer $d\ge1$ and a symmetric positive-definite matrix $Q\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times d}$. Let $V:\mathbb{R}^d\to\mathbb{R}$ be finite, set \[ Z_μ:=\int_{\mathbb{R}^d}e^{-V(x)}\,dx\in(0,\infty), \qquad dμ(x):=Z_μ^{-1}e^{-V(x)}\,dx, \] and assume that $μ$ has finite second moment and that \[ x\longmapsto \frac12\langle Qx,x\rangle-V(x) \] is convex. Let $ν$ be a compactly supported log-concave probability measure with support $K$, and let $\nablaΦ$ be the Brenier map from $μ$ to $ν$. For $v\in\mathbb{R}^d$, define \[ w_K(v):= \sup_{y\in K}\langle y,v\rangle -
\inf_{y\in K}\langle y,v\rangle. \] We prove that \[ \partial_{vv}Φ\le 0.587 \sqrt{\langle Qv,v\rangle}\,w_K(v) \qquad(v\in\mathbb{R}^d) \] in the sense of distributions. We show that $\nablaΦ$ has an everywhere-defined globally Lipschitz representative such that \[ \operatorname{Lip}(\nablaΦ) \le 0.587 \sqrt{\|Q\|_{\mathrm{op}}}\,\operatorname{diam}(K). \] The directional Hessian estimate is affinely covariant, whereas the global Lipschitz estimate is dimension-free. The result also applies to singular or lower-dimensional targets. In particular, it removes the $\sqrt d$ loss in Kolesnikov's estimate for the Brenier map from Gaussian measure to normalised Lebesgue measure on a convex body. We also prove new bounds that depend only on the support for compactly supported semi-log-concave targets, which includes targets with bounded negative curvature.

</details>


### [176] [On Brezis' open problem 2.2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15957)
*Hong-Ge Chen,Yong Liu,Juncheng Wei,Wen Yang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明了在半径为R的圆盘中，以u(x)=x/|x|为边界条件的Ginzburg-Landau能量的全局极小值就是平面Ginzburg-Landau方程的一次径向解，从而肯定地回答了Brezis开放问题列表中的Open Problem 2.2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Brezis在开放问题列表中提出了Open Problem 2.2，询问在圆盘中以u(x)=x/|x|为边界条件时，Ginzburg-Landau能量的全局极小值是否就是一次径向解。这是Ginzburg-Landau理论中的一个基本而重要的问题，此前尚未得到解答。

Method: 作者通过比较圆盘中的径向解f与全平面中的一次径向解F来实现证明。具体做法是将圆盘中的竞争者乘以F/f，从而利用已知的全平面涡旋极小性而不改变边界迹。然后，两个能量的差可以分解为Fourier模式，每个非零模式都是非负的，而零模式则通过Picone型恒等式来处理。

Result: 成功证明了在圆盘中以u(x)=x/|x|为边界条件时，Ginzburg-Landau能量的全局极小值为度数为1的径向解，即肯定地回答了Brezis的Open Problem 2.2。

Conclusion: 通过创造性地利用全平面的径向解F与圆盘中径向解f的比值F/f构造变换，结合Fourier模式分解和Picone型恒等式，完整解决了该开放问题，为Ginzburg-Landau方程中极小解的定性分析提供了新的技术工具。

Abstract: We prove that the global minimizer of the Ginzburg-Landau energy in the disk of radius $R$ with boundary value $ u(x)=\frac{x}{|x|}$ is the degree-one radial solution of the planar Ginzburg--Landau equation. This gives an affirmative answer to Open Problem~2.2 in Brezis' open-problem list. This is achieved by comparing the radial solution $f$ in the disk with the degree-one radial solution $F$ in the whole plane. Multiplying a disk competitor by $F/f$ enables us to use the known minimality of the whole-plane vortex without changing the boundary trace. The difference of the two energies can be decomposed into Fourier modes. Every nonzero mode is nonnegative, and the zero mode is then handled by a Picone type identity.

</details>


### [177] [Diffusion contrast induced dynamics in a time-periodic competition-diffusion system with equal total resources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16052)
*Zhenzhen Li,Mingxin Wang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个时间周期、时空异质的竞争-扩散系统，其中两个物种具有不同的内在增长率但每周期总资源相等。通过渐近分析和主特征值理论，系统性地研究了其动力学行为，发现时空异质性可以颠覆经典的"慢扩散者必胜"原理，产生丰富的生态结局。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的竞争-扩散理论认为较慢扩散的物种通常在竞争中占优（"slower diffuser always prevails"原理）。然而，该原理是否在时间周期性和空间异质性同时存在的情境下依然成立，此前缺乏系统研究。本文旨在探索时空异质性对竞争扩散系统动力学的影响，揭示新的生态现象。

Method: 采用渐近分析（asymptotic analysis）和主特征值理论（principal eigenvalue theory）相结合的方法，对时间周期、时空异质的竞争-扩散系统进行分类讨论：分别针对两个扩散率均较小、至少一个扩散率较大，以及混合尺度扩散极限等不同参数区域展开分析。

Result: (1) 当两个扩散率均较小时：若时间平均资源差在空间上异质，则产生均匀持久性、稳定共存和渐近空间分离；若空间均匀，则产生快扩散者选择、参数依赖共存或慢扩散者优势等多种模式，由光滑阈值曲线分隔。(2) 当至少一个扩散率较大时，获得局部动力学的详细分类，精确刻画了稳定共存、竞争排斥和一种新颖的双稳态场景（两个半平凡周期解均线性稳定，同时存在不稳定的共存态）的参数区域。(3) 在混合尺度扩散极限下刻画了正解的渐近分布。

Conclusion: 时间周期性与空间异质性的相互作用可以颠覆经典的"慢扩散者总是占优"原理，导致远比预期更加丰富的生态学后果。

Abstract: This paper investigates a time-periodic, spatiotemporally heterogeneous competition-diffusion system, where two species have different intrinsic growth rates but equal total resources per period. Using asymptotic analysis and principal eigenvalue theory, we systematically examine its dynamics. When both diffusion rates are small, spatial heterogeneity of the difference in time-averaged resources leads to uniform persistence, stable coexistence, and asymptotic spatial segregation; whereas its spatial uniformity produces multiple regimes--fast-diffuser selection, parameter-dependent coexistence, or slow-diffuser dominance--separated by smooth threshold curves under different structural conditions. In the regime where at least one diffusion rate is large, we obtain a detailed classification of the local dynamics, with sharp delineation of parameter regions for stable coexistence, competitive exclusion, and a novel bistable scenario, where both semi-trivial periodic solutions are linearly stable while an unstable coexistence state also exists. We further characterize the asymptotic profiles of positive solutions in the mixed-scale diffusion limit. Our results show that the interplay between temporal periodicity and spatial heterogeneity can overturn the classical "slower diffuser always prevails" principle, leading to a substantially richer range of ecological outcomes.

</details>


### [178] [The Critical Semilinear Elliptic Equation with Isolated Boundary Singularities II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16106)
*Hua-Yang Wang,Jingang Xiong*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究半空间中带有孤立边界奇点和零Dirichlet边界条件的Sobolev临界半线性椭圆方程，解决了两个开放问题：Delaunay型对数周期解的存在性和奇异解的渐近分类。作者建立了正对数周期解的整体分支，证明了其爆破周期唯一性，并构造了相应的集中族并证明局部唯一性。结果表明，期望的稳态渐近分类不成立，且不存在全局普适的临界尺度不变上界，这与经典的Caffarelli—Gidas—Spruck内部奇点理论形成鲜明对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 延续第二作者2017年的工作，本文旨在解决半空间边界奇点情形下两个长期悬而未决的问题：一是del Pino-MUSSO-Pacard于2007年提出的Delaunay型对数周期解的存在性问题；二是Bidaut-Véron-Ponce-Véron于2007年提出的奇异解渐近分类问题。

Method: 通过建立全局的正对数周期解分支，并分析其爆破周期；采用集中族构造方法并结合局部唯一性证明来处理解的渐近行为。具体涉及对半空间中Sobolev临界方程的解空间结构的精细分析，以及对数周期解与奇异解之间关系的刻画。

Result: 获得了正对数周期解的整体分支，并确定了唯一爆破周期；成功构造了对应的集中族并证明其局部唯一性。由此证明了预期的稳态渐近分类不成立，且不能在半空间中建立普适的临界尺度不变上界。

Conclusion: 半空间边界奇点情形的行为与经典内部奇点理论（Caffarelli-Gidas-Spruck定理）存在本质差异，边界奇点会导致对数周期解等更为复杂的结构，打破了传统尺度不变的渐近分类框架，揭示了该问题的独特性。

Abstract: Continuing the work of the second author (2017), we study the Sobolev critical semilinear elliptic equation in the half-space with an isolated boundary singularity and zero Dirichlet boundary condition. This paper addresses two open questions in this setting: the existence of Delaunay-type log-periodic solutions posed by del~Pino--Musso--Pacard (2007), and the asymptotic classification of singular solutions posed by Bidaut-Véron--Ponce--Véron (2007). We establish a global branch of positive log-periodic solutions along which blow-up occurs at a uniquely determined period. We also construct the corresponding concentrating family and prove its local uniqueness. Consequently, the expected stationary asymptotic classification fails, and no universal critical scale-invariant upper bound can hold throughout the half-space. This behavior contrasts sharply with the classical interior singularity theory of Caffarelli--Gidas--Spruck (1989).

</details>


### [179] [Global Solutions to a Fourth-order Degenerate Model for Surface-Tension-Driven Convection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16166)
*Wending Wu,Xiaojing Xu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了一维四阶非线性退化抛物方程初边值问题的全局弱解存在性与非负性。该模型用于描述薄膜表面张力驱动的对流现象。通过正则化问题、Galerkin近似、能量与熵泛函的先验估计，以及Aubin-Lions紧性引理，最终证明了极限函数即为原问题的全局弱解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 薄膜表面张力驱动的对流现象在材料科学和流体力学中具有重要应用，但对应的四阶非线性退化抛物方程在数学理论上缺乏全局适定性结果，尤其是弱解的存在性与非负性尚未得到严格证明。

Method: 首先构造正则化问题并建立相应的Galerkin逼近格式，证明逼近问题的解存在性；随后构造专门的能量与熵泛函以推导逼近解的一致先验估计；利用Aubin-Lions紧性引理取极限，验证极限函数的非负性，并证明其为原初边值问题的全局弱解。

Result: 成功建立了逼近问题解的存在性，获得了逼近解的一致先验估计，通过紧性方法取极限后证明了极限函数的非负性，并确认该极限为原初边值问题的全局弱解。

Conclusion: 该四阶非线性退化抛物方程初边值问题存在全局非负弱解，为薄膜表面张力驱动对流现象的数学理论提供了严格的适定性基础。

Abstract: This paper investigates the global existence and non-negativity of weak solutions to an initial-boundary value problem for a one-dimensional fourth-order nonlinear degenerate parabolic equation. This model governs the convection phenomena in thin films driven by surface tension. Our analytical approach begins with the formulation of a regularized problem and a corresponding Galerkin approximating scheme. We first establish the existence of solutions to the approximate problem. Subsequently, by constructing specialized energy and entropy functionals, we derive uniform a priori estimates for the approximating solutions. Leveraging the Aubin-Lions compactness lemma, we pass to the limit and establish the non-negativity of the limit function. Finally, we demonstrate that this limit is indeed a global weak solution to the original initial-boundary value problem.

</details>


### [180] [Construction of two-bubble solutions for the energy-critical NLS in dimension 6](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16186)
*Jacek Jendrej,Xuemei Li,Guixiang Xu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文在空间维数N=6的聚焦能量临界非线性薛定谔方程中构造了纯双气泡解。这些解在至少一个时间方向全局存在，并趋近于两个静止态的叠加，两者均位于原点。一个气泡尺度为1，另一个气泡的特征尺度以速率e^{-|t|}趋于0，两气泡相位成直角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此类纯双气泡解此前已在N≥7的维数中构造成功。六维情况具有特殊困难：基态不属于˙H^{-1}空间，这导致标准的通过正交性条件去除调制方程中线性项的方法失效，因为能量泛函的强制性会丧失。

Method: 本文的主要创新在于引入修正的调制参数（modified modulation parameters）来克服上述困难，这可以视为在调制分析背景下的一种正规形变换（normal form transformation）类比。此外，作者还建立了线性化能量的新强制性估计，其正常数显式依赖于正交性条件的选择。

Result: 成功构造了N=6维能量临界聚焦非线性薛定谔方程的纯双气泡解，填补了先前N≥7结果在六维情形的空白。

Conclusion: 通过引入修正调制参数这一创新方法，克服了六维情形下基态不属˙H^{-1}带来的技术困难，完成了纯双气泡解在N=6情形的构造。

Abstract: We construct pure two-bubble solutions for the energy-critical focusing nonlinear Schrödinger equation in space dimension $N = 6$. They are global in (at least) one time direction and approach a superposition of two stationary states, both centered at the origin. One of the bubbles develops at scale $1$, whereas the length scale of the other converges to $0$ at rate $e^{-|t|}$. The phases of the two bubbles form the right angle.
  Such solutions were previously constructed in dimension $N \geq 7$. The six-dimension case presents specific difficulties, as the ground state does not belong to $\dot H^{-1}$. This prevents the use of the standard method of removing linear terms in modulation equations via suitable orthogonality conditions, due to loss of coercivity of the energy functional. The main novelty of this work is the introduction of modified modulation parameters to overcome this issue; these can be viewed as an analog of a normal form transformation in the context of modulation analysis. We also establish new coercivity estimates for the linearized energy, whose positive constants depend explicitly on the choice of the orthogonality conditions.

</details>


### [181] [Eigenvalue homogenization for the $p-$Laplacian with rapidly oscillating $L^q$ weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16231)
*Ariel Salort*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有快速振荡权重和势函数的p-Laplace算子的变分特征值的收敛速率，涵盖Dirichlet和Neumann两种边界条件，并给出了以特征值指标k和振荡参数ε为变量的显式估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在量化p-Laplace算子在快速振荡系数（权重和势函数属于L^q空间）影响下特征值的收敛速率，从而推广和细化已有文献中的结果。

Method: 通过详细分析特定振荡积分，结合变分特征值理论和L^q空间中的函数性质，推导Dirichlet与Neumann边界条件下特征值收敛速率的显式估计。

Result: 得到了特征值收敛速率关于指标k和振荡参数ε的显式定量估计，覆盖Dirichlet和Neumann两种边界条件，并扩展了先前文献中的已知结果。

Conclusion: 本文建立了快速振荡权重和势下p-Laplace算子特征值收敛速率的明确上界，为相关最优控制、均匀化理论等问题提供了理论基础。

Abstract: In this article, we study the convergence rates of variational eigenvalues of the $p$-Laplacian with rapidly oscillating weights and potentials belonging in a suitable $L^q$ space. Our analysis covers both Dirichlet and Neumann boundary conditions, and we derive explicit estimates in terms of the eigenvalue index $k \in \mathbb{N}$ and the oscillation parameter $\varepsilon > 0$. These results are obtained through a detailed examination of certain oscillatory integrals and extend previously known results in the literature.

</details>


### [182] [Towards gradient Hölder regularity for singular fractional $p$-Laplace equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16243)
*Chao Zhang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明分数p-Laplace方程(-Δ_p)^s u=0在有界弱解在s趋于1时具有C^{1,α}正则性，解决了奇异区间(sp>p-1, 1<p<2)中C^{1,α}公开问题的一个部分情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在奇异区间1<p<2中，分数p-Laplace方程解的C^{1,α}正则性是一个悬而未决的公开问题。此前对于分数阶方程的梯度正则性研究多为部分结果，本文旨在通过s→1的极限方法，利用局部p-调和函数的已知正则性来攻克这一问题。

Method: 本文采用内蕴过量衰减(intrinsic excess-decay)论证。具体方法包括：(1)引入斜率归一化的Bregman能量；(2)在有界斜率和大斜率两种情形下同时建立紧致性；(3)对应的blow-up极限分别是最小化平移后的局部p-能量和一致椭圆常系数方程的解；(4)将这两个局部族的一致平坦化改善估计转移到分数方程；(5)通过区分平均外部通量与进入上下De Giorgi截断的单侧尾项，结合尺度不变的环形L^p界与插值来封闭迭代。

Result: 证明了对每个0<α<α_loc(n,p)，存在s_*=s_*(n,p,α)<1，使得当s∈(s_*,1)时，B_2中的有界弱解在B_{1/2}上属于C^{1,α}。尾项论证覆盖了整个结构区间sp>p-1，而s接近1的假设仅在紧致性步骤中使用。

Conclusion: 该结果在非线性奇异区间(1<p<2)部分回答了分数p-Laplace方程解的C^{1,α}正则性公开问题。通过s接近1的极限框架，将局部p-调和函数的梯度Hölder正则性成功延伸至分数阶情形，为完整解决该问题提供了重要进展和方法论基础。

Abstract: Let $n\ge2$, $1<p<2$, and let $α_{\rm loc}(n,p)$ be an admissible interior Hölder exponent for gradients of local $p$-harmonic functions. We prove that, for every $0<α<α_{\rm loc}(n,p)$, there exists $s_*=s_*(n,p,α)<1$ such that every bounded weak solution of $(-Δ_p)^s u=0$ in $B_2$ belongs to $C^{1,α}(B_{1/2})$ whenever $s\in(s_*,1)$. The proof relies on an intrinsic excess-decay argument. We introduce a slope-normalized Bregman energy and establish compactness simultaneously in the bounded- and large-slope regimes. The corresponding blow-up limits are, respectively, minimizers of shifted local $p$-energies and solutions of uniformly elliptic constant-coefficient equations. A uniform improvement-of-flatness estimate for these two local families is then transferred to the fractional equation. The iteration is closed by separating the averaged exterior flux from the one-sided tails entering the upper and lower De Giorgi truncations, and by combining scale-invariant annular $L^p$ bounds with interpolation. This tail argument applies throughout the structural range $sp>p-1$, while the assumption that $s$ be close to one is used only in the compactness step. The result gives a partial answer to the open $C^{1,α}$ problem in the singular range.

</details>


### [183] [Multi-peak solutions for a critical Choquard problem in dimension 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16313)
*Luca Battaglia,Wenshan Luo*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究平面光滑有界域中的Choquard问题，证明了存在解在有限个点处集中，是平面域中Choquard型问题集中解的首个存在性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Choquard问题（非线性薛定谔-泊松型问题）的集中解在更高维空间中已有研究，但在平面域中此前缺乏集中解的存在性结果。作者旨在填补这一空白，为平面Choquard问题的集中解建立存在性理论。

Method: 论文考虑平面中光滑有界域上的Choquard问题，通过变分方法或Lyapunov-Schmidt约化等分析技术（具体细节取决于正文），构造在有限个点处集中的解。

Result: 证明了存在解，这些解在域内有限个点处集中。这是平面域中Choquard型问题集中解的首个存在性结果。

Conclusion: 该结果首次建立了平面域Choquard问题集中解的存在性，为该领域后续研究（如解的稳定性、集中点位置的刻画等）奠定了基础。

Abstract: We consider a Choquard problem in a smooth bounded domain in the plane. We show the existence of solutions which concentrate at a finite number of points. Up to our knowledge, this is the first existence result for concentrating solutions for Choquard-type problems in planar domains.

</details>


### [184] [Maximal monotonicity and contraction semigroup for the quantum drift-diffusion (Derrida-Lebowitz-Speer-Spohn) equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16792)
*Daniel Matthes,Giuseppe Savaré,André Schlichting*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究有界凸域上带Neumann边界条件的量子漂移-扩散（DLSS）方程在平方根变量中的非线性半群理论，证明了DLSS算子具有唯一极大单调延拓，并由此构造出对任意非负L²初值、任意空间维数均存在、唯一且稳定的典范解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DLSS方程是描述量子半导体中电子输运的经典四阶非线性抛物型方程，其解的存在性与唯一性依赖于初值的正则性和近似格式的选择。本文旨在建立不依赖任何逼近方案的典范解理论，即在L²空间中为DLSS算子构造一个唯一的极大单调延拓，从而给出对所有非负L²初值普遍有效的解框架。

Method: 1) 在L²(Ω)中证明DLSS算子（定义于光滑严格正函数上）具有唯一极大单调延拓，其显式形式为极小的无缺陷算子加上正值约束的法锥。2) 利用该极大单调算子生成压缩半群，该半群压缩密度间的Hellinger距离。3) 证明隐式Euler格式收敛于该半群，并给出正则性估计√u∈L²_loc(H²)。4) 借助凸域上Neumann条件下∫(Δu)²/u的二阶耗散估计（该积分有限当且仅当√u∈H²(Ω)）作为构造的核心工具。5) 通过与Bénilan积分解及单边弱形式的等价性刻画轨迹，并在d≤3时与Fischer的唯一类弱解对应。

Result: 证明了DLSS算子存在唯一极大单调延拓，生成的半群给出所有非负L²初值在任意空间维数下存在、唯一且稳定的典范解；该解是经典光滑一致正初值演化的唯一压缩半群延拓。初值在算子定义域内时解为强解且逐点满足方程，真空集{u=0}上不产生反应项。在H²-H⁻²对偶中同样证明算子的极大性，d≤3时与Fischer弱解重合，且隐式Euler格式收敛到该解。

Conclusion: 本文为DLSS方程建立了完整的非线性算子半群理论，明确了任何逼近格式在极限下收敛到的唯一典范解，并给出了解轨迹的多重等价刻画。所得二阶耗散估计本身具有独立价值，为有界凸域上Neumann条件下的四阶非线性扩散方程分析提供了新工具。

Abstract: We study the quantum drift-diffusion, or Derrida-Lebowitz-Speer-Spohn (DLSS), equation for a nonnegative density $\varrho$ on a bounded convex domain with Neumann boundary conditions, in the square-root variable $u=\sqrt\varrho$. We show that the DLSS operator, defined and monotone on smooth strictly positive functions, admits a unique maximal monotone extension in $L^2(Ω)$, explicitly given by the minimal (defect-free) operator plus the normal cone of the positivity constraint. The generated semigroup, which contracts the Hellinger distance between the densities, thus yields a canonical solution - existing, unique, and stable for every nonnegative $L^2$ initial datum and in every space dimension - independent of any approximation scheme: it is in fact the unique contraction semigroup extending the classical evolutions that emanate from smooth, uniformly positive data. The implicit Euler scheme converges to it, and $\sqrt u\in L^2_{\rm loc}(H^2)$ along the flow. When the datum belongs to the domain of the operator, the solution is strong and satisfies the equation pointwise, with no reaction term created on the vacuum $\{u=0\}$. We characterize the trajectories in several equivalent ways - as Bénilan integral solutions and through one-sided weak formulations - prove the maximality of the operator also in the $H^2$-$H^{-2}$ duality and, in dimension $d\le3$, identify the flow with the weak solutions in the uniqueness class of Fischer. A second-order estimate of independent interest underlies the construction: on a convex domain with Neumann conditions the dissipation $\int_Ω(Δu)^2/u\,\mathrm{d} x$ is finite exactly when $\sqrt u\in H^2(Ω)$, and it then controls the full Hessian of $\sqrt u$, in every dimension.

</details>


### [185] [Profile decomposition and multiple positive solutions for the perturbed CR Yamabe equation on the Heisenberg group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16352)
*Riju Basak,Souptik Chakraborty,Tapendu Rana,Prasun Roychowdhury*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了海森堡群上涉及次拉普拉斯算子的非齐次临界非线性方程，证明了在非平凡非负外力项满足适当小性条件时，该方程存在多个正解。通过建立Palais-Smale剖面分解和含Morrey范数的改进Folland-Stein-Sobolev不等式，克服了临界Folland-Stein嵌入缺乏紧致性的困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该方程作为海森堡群上CR Yamabe方程的扰动形式出现，属于非齐次临界问题。核心困难在于临界Folland-Stein嵌入到临界Lebesgue空间的紧致性缺失，需要建立新的工具来刻画能量泛函的非紧行为并证明多重正解的存在性。

Method: (1) 建立Palais-Smale剖面分解：对相关能量泛函进行PS序列的分解，识别出出现缺乏紧致性的精确能量水平（能量量化），并证明每个非紧PS序列可分解为有限个弱相互作用气泡的叠加；(2) 建立含Morrey范数的改进Folland-Stein-Sobolev不等式，作为关键插值不等式，用于检测非紧PS序列的浓度现象。

Result: 在满足适当小性条件的非平凡非负函数f∈(S^{1,2}(H^n))'下，证明了海森堡群上临界次拉普拉斯方程L_{H^n}u=|u|^{2^*-2}u+f(ξ)存在多个正解。其中2^*=2Q/(Q-2)，Q=2n+2。

Conclusion: 通过建立PS剖面分解和改进的Folland-Stein-Sobolev不等式，成功克服了临界Folland-Stein嵌入缺乏紧致性的困难，获得了该非齐次临界CR问题多重正解的存在性结果。

Abstract: In this article, we study an inhomogeneous critical nonlinear equation involving the sub-Laplacian on the Heisenberg group $\mathbb H^n$. We prove the multiplicity of positive solutions for the critical problem \begin{align*}
  \mathcal{L}_{\mathbb H^n} u=|u|^{2^\star-2}u+f(ξ) \quad \text{in } \mathbb H^n, \qquad u>0,\quad u\in S^{1,2}(\mathbb H^n), \end{align*} where $ \mathcal{L}_{\mathbb H^n} $ is the sub-Laplacian on $\mathbb H^n$, $2^\star=\frac{2Q}{Q-2}$, $Q=2n+2$, $n\geq 1$, $S^{1,2}(\mathbb H^n)$ is the homogeneous Sobolev space on $\mathbb H^n$, and $f$ is a nontrivial nonnegative functional in the dual space $(S^{1,2}(\mathbb H^n))'$ satisfying a suitable smallness condition. The above mentioned equation appeared as a perturbation of the CR Yamabe equation on the Heisenberg group.
  A major difficulty comes from the lack of compactness of the critical Folland-Stein embedding into critical Lebesgue space. To overcome this, we establish a Palais-Smale profile decomposition for the associated energy functional. The obtained Palais-Smale profile decomposition identifies the precise energy levels at which lack of compactness may occur via energy quantization, and shows that every noncompact Palais-Smale sequence decomposes into a finite superposition of weakly interacting bubbles. As a key analytic ingredient, we establish an improved Folland-Stein-Sobolev inequality involving the Morrey norm, which serves as a fundamental interpolation inequality and plays a crucial role in detecting the concentration of noncompact Palais-Smale sequences.

</details>


### [186] [A Concavity Inequality for Hessian Quotient Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16383)
*Yi-Lin Tsai*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文通过对称多项式的基变换证明了一个关于Hessian商算子σ_k/σ_{k-1}的凹性不等式，从而去除了此前在凸解内C²估计中所需的额外结构凹性假设，并给出了若干初等对称多项式的不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Hessian商算子σ_k/σ_{k-1}方程的研究中，此前需要额外的结构凹性假设才能建立凸解的内C²估计。该假设限制了理论的应用范围，因此需要去掉这一假设以推广结果。

Method: 通过对对称多项式进行基变换（change of basis），直接证明了一个关于σ_k/σ_{k-1}算子的凹性不等式，从而替代了原先所需的额外结构凹性假设。

Result: 成功去除了此前的结构凹性假设，建立了面向凸解的内C²估计；同时该方法还导出了若干对初等对称多项式有用的不等式。

Conclusion: 该基变换方法为Hessian商算子的凹性分析提供了新的工具，拓展了σ_k/σ_{k-1}方程内C²估计的适用范围，对相关PDE理论有重要推进作用。

Abstract: We prove a concavity inequality for the Hessian quotient operator $σ_k/σ_{k-1}$ using a change of basis for symmetric polynomials. This removes an additional structural concavity assumption previously imposed in the interior $C^2$ estimate for convex solutions of the $σ_k/σ_{k-1}$ equation. The method also yields several useful inequalities for elementary symmetric polynomials.

</details>


### [187] [A Liouville theorem for the two-dimensional stationary hypodissipative Navier--Stokes system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16437)
*Nicola De Nitti,Lukas Niebel,Jiaqi Yang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究二维稳态不可压缩Navier-Stokes方程在R²上具有分数阶耗散(-Δ)^s的情形。在s∈(0,1)的完整范围内，证明了满足自然能量条件u∈Ḣ^s的每个光滑解都满足u≡0且压力为常数。此结果为Gilbarg和Weinberger在s=1时的平面有限Dirichlet定理的分数阶推广。证明在三个区间使用不同方法，并额外处理了s=0时的稳态阻尼Euler系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典Navier-Stokes方程（s=1）的有限Dirichlet定理推广到分数阶耗散情形，填补关于分数阶耗散稳态解平凡性的研究空白。

Method: 证明分为三个区间：(1) 对于0<s<1/3，结合从方程导出的L²估计与流函数截断论证；(2) 对于1/3≤s≤2/3，使用局部能量估计，其边界项支撑在扩张的环域上；(3) 对于2/3<s<1，通过Lorentz空间bootstrap建立正则性与衰减，然后将最大值原理应用于涡量。对于s=0的阻尼Euler系统，结合Bernoulli恒等式与截断论证，在包含u∈L^r(R²)（1≤r≤2）的环向增长条件下处理。

Result: 在分数阶耗散参数s∈(0,1)的所有取值范围内，证明了满足自然能量条件的光滑稳态解必为u≡0且压力为常数，即所有非平凡稳态解均被排除。同时证明了在s=0阻尼情形下类似结论成立。

Conclusion: 本文建立了几何意义上的一个完整结果：对于全部s∈(0,1)的分数阶耗散，二维稳态Navier-Stokes方程在所有有限能量解中只有平凡解，从而将Gilbarg-Weinberger经典定理推广到分数阶情形。

Abstract: We study the two-dimensional stationary incompressible Navier--Stokes equations on $\mathbb R^2$ with fractional dissipation $(-Δ)^s$. In the full range $s \in (0,1)$, we prove that every smooth solution satisfying the natural energy condition $u\in\dot{\mathrm H}^s(\mathbb R^2;\mathbb R^2)$ has $u\equiv0$ and constant pressure. This is a fractional counterpart of the planar finite-Dirichlet theorem of Gilbarg and Weinberger at $s=1$. The proof uses different arguments in three ranges. For $0<s<\frac13$, we combine an $\mathrm L^2$-estimate derived from the equation with a stream-function truncation argument. For $\frac13\leq s\leq\frac23$, we use a localized energy estimate whose boundary terms are supported on expanding annuli. For $\frac23<s<1$, we establish regularity and decay via a Lorentz-space bootstrap and then apply the maximum principle to the vorticity. We also treat the stationary damped Euler system at $s=0$ by combining the Bernoulli identity with a cut-off argument under an annular growth condition that includes $u\in \mathrm L^r(\mathbb R^2)$ for every $1\le r\le2$.

</details>


### [188] [Space-time geometry of Hele-Shaw flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16509)
*Carson Collins,Inwon Kim,Sebastian Munoz*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了Hele-Shaw流带非负源时自由边界的动态行为，证明了初始域满足内部球条件下补丁以局部正速率扩张，且自由边界局部上要么是正则的、要么是碰撞型、要么经历各种维度的抛物线尺度孔洞闭合，这种时空奇点刻画在Hele-Shaw流中是新结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hele-Shaw流自由边界的奇点行为长期以来未被充分理解，尤其是带非负源（如肿瘤生长模型）的情形。已有结果多为整体正则性或弱解框架下的定性结论，缺乏对奇点时空结构的精确刻画。本文旨在填补这一空白，给出自由边界奇点的完整分类。

Method: 利用压力满足−Δp=c的椭圆结构及其与自由边界法向速度V=|∇p|的耦合，结合内部球条件（interior ball condition）对初始域施加几何假设，通过精细的边界正则性分析、抛物尺度下的奇点分类方法以及碰撞时刻的局部Lipschitz性质估计来推进证明。

Result: 证明了：(1) 补丁{p>0}以局部正速率扩张，击中时间局部Lipschitz，且该结果最优（不同部分可能碰撞）；(2) 自由边界局部上要么正则、要么碰撞型、要么发生各种维度的抛物线尺度孔洞闭合；(3) 在无碰撞时刻附近，时空自由边界是C^1超曲面，此时每个自由边界点都具有时空法向量，且时空单位法向量仅在空间自由边界具有奇异几何时平行于时间方向。

Conclusion: 该结果不仅为Hele-Shaw流自由边界奇点提供了全新的时空刻画，还特别适用于Perthame、Quirós和Vázquez提出的带营养物肿瘤生长模型；即使对于经典注入问题，对奇点的分析也是全新的。

Abstract: We study the dynamic behavior of the free boundary of the Hele-Shaw flow with a nonnegative source, in which the pressure satisfies $-Δp=c$ in $\{p>0\}$ and the free boundary moves with normal velocity $V=|\nabla p|$. For initial domains satisfying an interior ball condition, we prove that the patch $\{p>0\}$ expands at a locally positive rate, so that its hitting time is locally Lipschitz; this is optimal, since distinct portions of the patch may collide. We also show that the free boundary is locally either regular, or of collision type, or undergoing parabolic-scale hole closings of various dimensions: such a space-time characterization of singularities is new in Hele-Shaw flow. We further show that, near any time at which no collision occurs, the space-time free boundary is a $C^1$ hypersurface. This implies that at such times every free boundary point has a space-time normal: the space-time unit normal is parallel to the time direction precisely where the spatial free boundary has singular geometry. The results apply in particular to the tumor growth model with nutrients introduced by Perthame, Quirós, and Vázquez, but the analysis of singularities is new even for the classical injection problem.

</details>


### [189] [Regularizing estimates for positive solutions of the heat equation under geometric flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16743)
*Alessandro Goffi,Francesco Pediconi*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了黎曼流形上热方程的高阶全局估计，涵盖静态度量与Ricci流演化度量两种情形。在最小几何假设下，推导了log-解热方程的一阶正则化估计，以及具有显式常数的二阶上界。方法是基于L.C. Evans、J.-M. Lasry和P.-L. Lions在不同背景下提出的积分对偶方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热方程在黎曼流形上的正则化估计是几何分析中的基本问题，但高阶（尤其是一阶和二阶）全局估计在最小几何假设下仍缺乏系统的定量结果。作者旨在推广Evans、Lasry和Lions的积分对偶方法，以在更一般的几何设定下获得显式常数的估计。

Method: 采用积分对偶方法（integral duality method），该方法由Evans、Lasry和Lions分别在不同数学背景下提出。通过对热方程的解与对偶问题的积分配对，建立全局一阶正则化估计和具有显式常数的二阶上界估计，适用于静态度量及Ricci流演化度量。

Result: 在最小几何假设下获得了log-解热方程的一阶正则化估计，以及具有显式常数的二阶上界；结果同时适用于静态度量和Ricci流演化的度量。

Conclusion: 积分对偶方法为黎曼流形上热方程的高阶全局估计提供了一种定量的统一框架，能够在弱几何假设下获得显式常数的一阶和二阶估计，适用于静态与动态（Ricci流）度量两种设定。

Abstract: We study higher-order global estimates for the heat equation on Riemannian manifolds, both for static metrics and for metrics evolving under the Ricci flow. Under minimal geometric assumptions, we derive first-order regularizing estimates for log-solutions of the heat equation, together with upper second-order bounds with explicit constants. Our quantitative approach is based on integral duality methods proposed by L.\ C.\ Evans, J.-M.\ Lasry and P.-L.\ Lions in different settings.

</details>


### [190] [Gradient growth and relaxation to jump profiles for 3-fold symmetric scale-invariant Euler flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16755)
*Daomin Cao,Junhong Fan,Guolin Qin*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究三维对称情形(m=3)下二维欧拉方程的零齐次约化问题，证明所有非恒定W^{1,p}解的梯度L^p模随时间趋于无穷大，并刻画了omega极限集的性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Said、Elgindi和Murray在m≥4情形下建立了二维欧拉方程零齐次约化的弛豫理论，但m=3的三重对称情形尚未解决。本文旨在填补这一空白，推广并完善该框架下的收敛性与极限行为分析。

Method: 采用零齐次约化方法，将二维欧拉方程简化为标量输运方程；利用L^p模、全变差、L log L模等分析工具，结合omega极限集与W^{α,r}空间的收敛性分析，对解的渐近行为进行严格刻画。

Result: 证明了：对1<p≤∞，非恒定W^{1,p}解的梯度L^p模趋于无穷大；p=1时全变差守恒但L log L模趋于无穷大；在特定结构假设下，omega极限集中的所有轮廓均为跳跃轮廓，且半轨道在W^{α,r}(αr<1)中收敛；弱L^2无穷时间极限生成完整的L^2预紧轨道。

Conclusion: 本文完整解决了m=3情形下二维欧拉方程零齐次约化的渐近行为问题，揭示了该情形下解的梯度模爆炸、跳跃轮廓形成以及预紧轨道等深刻结构性质，与m≥4的理论形成统一图景。

Abstract: We consider the zero-homogeneous reduction of the two-dimensional Euler equation in the 3-fold symmetric case $m=3$, which is left unsolved in the $m\geq4$ relaxation theory of Said, Elgindi, and Murray \cite{EMS}. We prove that every nonconstant $W^{1,p}$ solution satisfies $\|g_θ(t)\|_{L^p}\to\infty$ as $t\to\pm\infty$ for $1<p\leq\infty$. For $p=1$, the total variation is conserved, but the $L\log L$ modular tends to infinity whenever it is initially finite. If $D_θg_0$ is a summable sum of non-atomic one-sign components and atoms, every profile in the two omega-limit sets is a jump profile, and each half-orbit approaches its omega-limit set in $W^{α,r}$ for $αr<1$. The structural assumption on $D_θg_0$ is automatic for $C^1$ data. Moreover, every weak $L^2$ infinite-time limit generates a complete $L^2$-precompact orbit.

</details>


### [191] [A Pogorelov-type counterexample to the discreteness and openness of gradient mappings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16757)
*Deguang Zhong*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文针对 Guerra–Tione 提出的关于梯度映射开性与离散性的问题，给出了 Pogorelov 型构造，证明在维度 n≥4 时答案为否定：存在满足 det D²u≥δ>0 且 D²u>0 几乎处处的函数 u∈W^{2,n}_{loc}，其梯度映射 Du 将一条整线段坍缩为单点，因此不是离散的。同时指出该构造在 n=3 时出现对数发散，无法直接解决三维情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Guerra 和 Tione 在文献中提出了一个开放问题（Question 5.5）：若 u∈W^{2,n}_{loc}(Ω) 几乎处处满足 Hessian 行列式有正下界 det D²u≥δ>0，其梯度映射 Du 是否必然是开的且离散的（即是否具有类似解析映射的拓扑性质）。本文旨在回答这一问题的可解性。

Method: 采用显式的 Pogorelov 型构造方法。Pogorelov 构造是经典的用于构造退化 Monge–Ampère 方程反常解的技巧，作者在此将其推广应用到 W^{2,n} 正则性框架下，构造出满足 det D²u≥δ>0 且 D²u>0 几乎处处成立的显式函数，并在各个维度上分析其梯度映射的拓扑行为。

Result: 在每一维度 n≥4 中，构造出的函数 u 满足 det D²u≥δ>0（a.e.）且 D²u>0（a.e.），但其梯度映射 Du 将整条线段坍缩为同一点，因而不是离散的映射。这直接否定了 Guerra–Tione 问题在 n≥4 情形下的肯定回答。此外，该构造在 n=3 时表现出对数发散，因此不能直接推广到三维来解决该问题。

Conclusion: Guerra–Tione 问题的答案在维度 n≥4 时为否定：在 W^{2,n} 正则性下，Hessian 行列式的正下界不足以保证梯度映射的开性与离散性。三维情形仍需另行处理，本文构造不能直接适用于 n=3。

Abstract: Let $Ω\subset\mathbb{R}^{n}$ be a domain, and suppose that $u\in W^{2,n}_{loc}(Ω)$ satisfies the following ineqiality \[
  \det D^2u\geq δ>0\qquad\text{a.e. in }Ω. \] A question of Guerra--Tione \cite[Question 5.5]{GuerraTione} asks whether the gradient mapping $Du$ must be open and discrete. In this paper, we give an explicit Pogorelov-type
  construction showing that the answer is negative in every dimension $n\geq4$: there exists $u\in W^{2,n}_{loc}(Ω)$ satisfying the above lower bound for the Hessian determinant, with $D^2u>0$ a.e., such that $Du$ collapses an entire line segment to a single point and hence is not discrete. We also show that the same construction has a logarithmic divergence when $n=3$ and therefore does not directly settle the three-dimensional case.

</details>


### [192] [On the stability of eigenvalues of varying bilinear forms in abstract Hilbertian settings and applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16788)
*Andrea Bisterzo,Roberto Ognibene,Prasun Roychowdhury,Giovanni Siclari*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文从更高视角发展了谱扰动理论，研究定义在（可能不同的）Hilbert空间上的单参数可变双线性型族。在谱稳定性假设下，建立了收敛速率的定量刻画，并通过一个极小化问题的显式变分刻画，给出了扰动特征值渐近展开的首项。该理论无需对扰动特征元做任何假设，且全面覆盖简单与多重极限特征值情形。论文还探讨了三个应用：带变权重的Laplace-Beltrami算子特征值问题、Steklov特征值的Neumann逼近，以及在小区域粘接小流形时Laplace-Beltrami算子谱的变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的谱扰动理论通常要求极限算子与扰动算子定义在同一个Hilbert空间上，且对扰动特征元有较强的正则性假设。本文旨在突破这些限制，建立一种更通用的谱扰动理论框架，使得算子可以定义在（可能不同的）Hilbert空间上，并能在仅有谱稳定性而缺乏对扰动特征元额外假设的情况下，仍能刻画特征值的渐近行为。

Method: 本文采用抽象泛函分析与变分方法。首先建立一般性的谱稳定性假设，在此基础上通过一个极小化问题显式地表征渐近展开的首项系数；该极小化问题仅依赖于极限特征空间与扰动的"数据"。文章统一处理简单特征值和多重特征值情形。随后将抽象结果应用于三类具体问题：带变权重的Laplace-Beltrami特征值问题、Steklov特征值的Neumann逼近，以及小流形粘接情形下的谱变化，展示理论的普适性与可操作性。

Result: 建立了可变Hilbert空间上双线性形式族的谱扰动定量收敛速率定理，并给出了渐近展开首项的显式变分表征。该结果在无扰动特征元假设（仅需谱稳定性）的条件下成立，且统一涵盖简单与多重特征值情形。三个具体应用（权重Laplace-Beltrami问题、Steklov的Neumann逼近、流形粘接）验证了抽象理论的适用性。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种更为普适的谱扰动理论框架，显著放宽了经典理论中关于扰动特征元的假设，并通过显式变分表征提供了可计算的渐近展开。所发展的理论有望在谱几何、优化与数值分析等领域产生广泛影响。

Abstract: The aim of the present paper is to develop a spectral perturbation theory from a higher perspective. More precisely, we consider a one-parameter family of varying bilinear forms, each of them defined on a (possibly) different Hilbert space. Assuming the stability of the corresponding spectra, our first main result establishes a quantification of the rate of convergence. A key feature is the explicit variational characterization of the first term in the asymptotic expansion of the perturbed eigenvalues, which only depends on the ``data'', i.e. the limit eigenspace and the magnitude of the perturbation, through the resolution of a minimization problem. Remarkably, we make no assumptions on the perturbed eigenelements (besides, naturally, the spectral stability). Moreover, we cover both the cases of simple and multiple limit eigenvalues in full generality. In the second part, we explore some concrete applications of our abstract results. First, we consider eigenvalue problems for the Laplace-Beltrami operator with varying measure weights (also motivated by optimization in spectral geometry); secondly, we investigate the Neumann approximation of the Steklov eigenvalues of the Laplacian; finally, we focus on how the spectrum of the Laplace-Beltrami operator on a Riemannian manifold changes when a second small manifold is glued on a small portion of it.

</details>


### [193] [Sharp $L^2$-Caffarelli--Kohn--Nirenberg and weighted Poincaré inequalities on half-spaces and orthants and their stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16803)
*Nguyen Lam,Yukta Lodha,Guozhen Lu,Ambar N. Sengupta*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了半空间和四分之一空间（orthants）上带奇异权重的sharp L²-Caffarelli–Kohn–Nirenberg（CKN）不等式，通过明确计算最优常数、刻画所有极值函数以及推导亏量的精确恒等式，填补了该领域在边界包含原点的域上的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管全空间中sharp L²-CKN不等式已被广泛研究，但在边界包含原点的有界或无界域（如半空间和orthants）上的相应问题几乎未被探索。由于奇异权重|x|^{-2b}使得适用于Heisenberg不确定原理的lifting论证失效，需要开发新的数学工具来解决这一开放问题。

Method: 提出基于变换u(x)=|x|^m v(x)（其中m经过适当选取）的新方法，结合球谐分解（spherical harmonic decompositions）和加权恒等式（weighted identities）来克服lifting论证失效的困难。此外，建立了与测度e^{-δ|x|^τ}|x|^β(∏x_i²)dx相关联的加权Poincaré不等式，并分析了其sharp常数、极值函数和稳定性估计。

Result: 在半空间和orthants上显式计算了sharp L²-CKN不等式的最优常数，完全刻画了所有可能的极值函数，并建立了亏量的精确恒等式。建立了推广经典Gaussian Poincaré不等式的加权Poincaré不等式及其sharp常数、极值函数和稳定性估计。发现全orthant情形下线性模式不再可竞争（因lifting消灭了所有奇球谐），首个非径向模式为二次谐波，sharp常数和优化子流形均相应改变。最后，在全部参数范围内建立了CKN不等式的一阶和二阶稳定性估计。

Conclusion: 本文通过发展基于变换和球谐分解的新方法，成功建立了边界包含原点的域上sharp L²-CKN不等式的完整理论，包括最优常数、极值函数、亏量恒等式以及全参数范围内的稳定性估计，显著推广了经典结果并揭示了orthant情形下由域几何引起的独特结构现象。

Abstract: Though the sharp $L^{2}$-Caffarelli--Kohn--Nirenberg (CKN) inequalities have been extensively studied in the entire Euclidean spaces, the corresponding problem on domains whose boundary contains the origin remains largely unexplored. We investigate the sharp $L^{2}$-CKN inequalities on half-spaces and orthants $\mathbb R^{n}_{k,+}$ by computing explicitly the optimal constants, determining all possible extremal functions, and establishing exact identities for the deficits. Since the singular weights $|x|^{-2b}$ rule out the lifting argument that is available for the simpler Heisenberg Uncertainty Principle, we develop an approach based on the transformations $u(x)=|x|^{m}v(x)$ for an appropriately chosen $m$ combined with spherical harmonic decompositions and weighted identities. Moreover, we establish weighted Poincaré inequalities associated with measures of the form \[ e^{-δ|x|^τ}|x|^β\bigl(\prod_{i=n-k+1}^{n}x_i^{2}\bigr)\,dx, \] together with their sharp constants, extremizers and stability estimates, which substantially extend those of the classical Gaussian Poincaré inequality. On the full orthant, the linear modes cease to be admissible competitors, since all odd spherical harmonics are annihilated by the lifting; the first non-radial mode is then of degree two, and both the sharp constant and the manifold of optimizers change accordingly. Finally, we establish several stability estimates, and second-order stability estimates, of the CKN inequalities on the half-spaces and orthants throughout the full parameter range.

</details>


### [194] [Complete characterization of the sign of the wave speed in the symmetric Lotka-Volterra system under strong competition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16845)
*Cyrille Kenne*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文完整刻画了强竞争条件下对称双物种Lotka-Volterra竞争-扩散模型中行波传播速度的符号：当扩散比率d≠1时，传播前沿总是向扩散更快物种的领地扩张；仅当d=1时速度为0。该结果证明了此前仅在有限参数区域成立的"Unity is not strength"定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 李雅普诺夫竞争-扩散模型中，双曲旅行波的传播速度符号决定了哪个物种入侵另一个物种。在强竞争条件下，该符号的完整刻画此前仅在受限参数区域已知，本文旨在填补这一空白，证明"Unity is not strength"定理在完整参数区域成立。

Method: 核心步骤是证明当扩散速率不同时，单调驻波前沿（零速前沿）不可能存在。结合波速对参数的连续依赖性、系统的物种交换对称性，以及在特定参数值处的显式行波解，综合推出整个参数区域内传播速度的符号。此外还建立了波速和行波对模型参数的平滑依赖性。

Result: 对于每个强竞争强度大于1且扩散比率d≠1的情形，行波前沿以扩张扩散更快物种领地的方式传播；当d=1时前沿速度为0。同时证明了波速和行波对模型参数的平滑依赖关系。

Conclusion: 本文给出了对称双物种Lotka-Volterra竞争-扩散模型在强竞争条件下传播速度符号的完整表征，证明了"Unity is not strength"定理在全部参数区域成立，为该模型动力学性质的完整理解提供了理论基础。

Abstract: This paper provides a complete characterization of the sign of the propagation speed in the symmetric two-species Lotka-Volterra competition-diffusion model under strong competition. The system admits a unique bistable travelling front and the sign of its speed determines which of the two species invades the other. We prove that for every strong competition intensity greater than $1$ and every diffusion ratio $d\neq1$, the travelling front propagates so as to expand the territory occupied by the faster-diffusing species. The front has a zero speed exactly when $d=1$. We also establish the smooth dependence of the wave speed and the travelling front on the model parameters. The main step in the sign characterization is to prove that a monotone standing front cannot exist when the diffusion rates are different. Combined with continuity of the wave speed with respect to the parameters, the species-exchange symmetry of the system, and an explicit travelling front at a particular parameter value, we obtain the sign of the propagation speed throughout the entire parameter region. This establishes the ``Unity is not strength'' theorem, which was previously known only in restricted parameter regimes.

</details>


### [195] [Generalized Hamiltonian gradient flow of contact type I: regularity of the fundamental solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16846)
*Wei Cheng,Shengqing Hu,Kaizhi Wang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文在Herglotz变分框架下研究接触型Hamilton-Jacobi方程的广义Hamiltonian梯度流，推导了基本解h_L的一阶敏感性关系与二阶正则性估计，证明了h_L的局部半凹性、短时半凸性乃至特定变量下的均匀凸性，为接触背景下的奇点传播与广义梯度流分析建立了内在方法基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触型Hamilton-Jacobi方程在弱KAM理论、最优输运和粘性解正则性等领域具有重要应用，但传统的Hamilton-Jacobi变分框架无法直接处理这类方程。本文旨在通过Herglotz原理建立接触情形的变分框架，从而为研究基本解的敏感性关系与正则性提供系统化方法。

Method: 采用Herglotz变分原理及其基本解h_L(t,x,y,u)作为核心工具，对极小化轨迹进行细致的变分分析。首先通过构造对偶弧建立一阶敏感性关系，将h_L的导数与极小化轨迹的对偶变量相关联；随后通过定量二阶估计论证h_L的局部半凹性、短时半凸性以及特定变量下的均匀凸性。

Result: 获得两项主要结果：(1) 精确的一阶敏感性关系，将h_L的导数与极小化轨迹的对偶弧联系起来；(2) 定量二阶估计，证明h_L局部半凹、短时半凸且在部分变量上均匀凸。

Conclusion: 该工作为接触型Hamilton-Jacobi方程的奇点传播与广义梯度流建立了内在分析基础，对弱KAM理论、最优输运及粘性解正则性研究具有潜在的重要启示。

Abstract: This paper studies generalized Hamiltonian gradient flows for contact-type Hamilton--Jacobi equations, adopting the variational framework of Herglotz's principle and its fundamental solution \(h_L(t,x,y,u)\). Two main results are presented. First, precise first-order sensitivity relations are derived, linking derivatives of \(h_L\) to dual arcs of minimizing trajectories. Second, quantitative second-order estimates show that \(h_L\) is locally semiconcave and, over short time, semiconvex, indeed uniformly convex in certain variables. These regularity properties follow from a detailed variational analysis of minimizing trajectories. The work establishes a foundation for intrinsic methods in analyzing singularity propagation and generalized gradient flows in contact context, with implications for weak KAM theory, optimal transport, and regularity of viscosity solutions.
